为评估大语言模型在现实PCB布局设计中处理复杂几何约束的能力，作者提出了OmniLayout，一个包含1681个工业级原理图耦合PCB布局的多模态基准。该基准涵盖四项任务：元件在板内几何布局、可布线性布局、电气功能保持及工具辅助推理。实验结果表明，当前大语言模型在几何精度、布线优化和电气功能保持方面表现不佳，揭示其在电子设计自动化实际应用中的关键能力缺陷。
On the feasibility of dependency parsing of non-human sequences without a gold standard. Is evaluation possible in other species?
Authors: Ramon Ferrer-i-Cancho, Catherine Hobaiter, Thore Bergman, Morgan Gustison
First: 2026-07-07T17:47:26+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-07T17:47:26+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Dependency parsing consists of finding a tree representation for a sequence. Unsupervised dependency parsing aims to develop parsing methods without a gold standard during model training. In human languages, an unsupervised parser can be evaluated because some gold standard is usually available or can be created. For other species, a gold standard is unknown. Thus one may conclude that it is impossible to determine the accuracy of an unsupervised parser and, consequently, dependency parsing is unfeasible in other species. However, here we apply recent advances in network science to demonstrate that the proportion of correct edges retrieved by a parser must be high for the sequences of vocalizations or gestures that non-human primates produce due to the fast decay of the sequence length distribution. In contrast, human language sequences lack that property. Therefore, evaluation without a gold standard is feasible in non-human primates but a hard problem in humans.
中文标题/摘要
标题：在没有黄金标准的情况下，对非人类序列进行依存句法分析的可行性研究。其他物种的评估是否可能？
依存句法分析旨在为序列构建树状表示。无监督依存句法分析的目标是在模型训练过程中不依赖黄金标准。在人类语言中，无监督解析器可被评估，因为通常存在或可创建黄金标准。对于其他物种，黄金标准未知，因此人们可能认为无法确定无监督解析器的准确性，从而得出在其他物种中依存句法分析不可行的结论。然而，本文应用网络科学的最新进展表明，由于非人类灵长类动物发声或手势序列的长度分布衰减迅速，解析器所提取的正确边比例必须很高。相比之下，人类语言序列不具备该特性。因此，在非人类灵长类动物中无需黄金标准即可进行评估，但在人类中则是一个难题。
Summary / 总结
This paper investigates whether unsupervised dependency parsing can be evaluated without a gold standard in non-human species, challenging the assumption that such evaluation is impossible outside human languages. It proposes using network science principles to analyze sequence length distributions, showing that non-human primate vocalization or gesture sequences exhibit rapid decay in length, which constrains possible dependency structures and enables meaningful evaluation of parsers without ground truth. In contrast, human language sequences lack this decay property, making evaluation without gold standards significantly harder. The key finding is that dependency parsing is theoretically feasible for non-human primates under these conditions, while remaining difficult for humans.
本文探讨了在缺乏黄金标准的情况下，非人类物种的无监督依存句法分析是否可评估，挑战了该评估在非人类语言中不可能的假设。研究利用网络科学最新方法指出，非人类灵长类动物的发声或手势序列长度分布衰减迅速，这意味着任何能提取大量正确边的解析器必然具有较高准确性，因此评估是可行的。相比之下，人类语言序列不具备这种衰减特性，使得无黄金标准评估变得困难。研究因此表明，非人类灵长类动物的依存句法分析可在无黄金标准情况下进行有效评估，而人类则面临更大挑战。
Hierarchical Acoustic-Semantic Modeling: Modality Separation and Semantic Coherence for Full-Duplex SLMs
Authors: Zhenyu Liu, Yunxin Li, Xuanyu Zhang, Qixun Teng, Shenyuan Jiang, Haolan Chen, Minjun Zhao, Fanbo Meng, Yu Xu, Yancheng He, Baotian Hu, Haizhou Li, Min Zhang
First: 2026-07-07T17:43:36+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-07T17:43:36+00:00
Comments: 22 pages, 9 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Developing seamless, high-performance, native intelligent full-duplex Spoken Language Models (SLMs) remains a critical challenge and long-standing goal for the speech and NLP community. Despite notable progress, recent endeavors are fundamentally constrained by severe modality interference, which causes substantial knowledge degradation and compromises semantic integrity -- ultimately making full-duplex SLMs feel unnatural and unintelligent. In this paper, through an exhaustive fine-grained analysis of model optimization dynamics, we uncover the root cause of such performance degradation, revealing that modality interference arises from inherent gradient conflicts between acoustic and semantic modeling when the two modalities are forced to share a deep parameter space. Guided by this key insight, we introduce Lychee-FD, a native end-to-end full-duplex framework designed to mitigate modality interference. Importantly, we propose a hierarchical parameter separation strategy that decouples conflicting modalities in deep layers while preserving cross-modality coherence via a dedicated semantic alignment channel. Extensive experiments on multiple full-duplex benchmarks demonstrate that our method significantly advances the state of the art, yielding substantial improvements in both speech intelligence (+7.4% on Spoken QA) and full-duplex interaction fluidity (+28.5% on FullDuplexBench 1.5) without compromising inference efficiency. To the best of our knowledge, this work is the first to achieve two key advances: 1) uncovering and elucidating the root cause of modality interference in full-duplex SLMs, and 2) designing an elegant hierarchical model together with a practical solution for seamless, high-performance, native intelligent full-duplex SLMs.
中文标题/摘要
标题：分层声学-语义建模：面向全双工口语模型的模态分离与语义一致性
开发无缝、高性能、原生智能的全双工口语模型（SLM）仍是语音与自然语言处理社区的关键挑战和长期目标。尽管取得显著进展，近期研究仍受严重模态干扰制约，导致知识退化和语义完整性受损，使全双工SLM显得不自然且缺乏智能。本文通过详尽的细粒度模型优化动态分析，揭示了性能退化的根本原因：当声学与语义模态被迫共享深层参数空间时，二者内在梯度冲突引发模态干扰。基于此关键洞察，我们提出Lychee-FD，一种原生端到端全双工框架，旨在缓解模态干扰。我们设计了一种分层参数分离策略，在深层解耦冲突模态的同时，通过专用语义对齐通道保持跨模态一致性。在多个全双工基准测试中，实验表明该方法显著提升当前最优性能，在口语问答（+7.4%）和全双工交互流畅度（+28.5%）上均取得显著改进，且不牺牲推理效率。据我们所知，本工作首次实现两项关键突破：1）揭示并阐明全双工SLM中模态干扰的根本成因；2）设计优雅的分层模型并提供实用方案，实现无缝、高性能、原生智能的全双工SLM。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the persistent challenge of modality interference that degrades semantic integrity and performance in full-duplex Spoken Language Models, this paper identifies gradient conflicts between acoustic and semantic modeling as the root cause when these modalities share a deep parameter space. To address this, the authors propose Lychee-FD, an end-to-end full-duplex framework that employs a hierarchical parameter separation strategy to decouple conflicting modalities in deep layers while maintaining cross-modality coherence through a dedicated semantic alignment channel. Experimental results across multiple benchmarks show significant improvements: +7.4% in spoken QA accuracy and +28.5% in full-duplex interaction fluidity, without sacrificing inference efficiency, marking the first work to both explain the interference mechanism and deliver a practical, high-performance full-duplex SLM solution.
为解决全双工语音语言模型中模态干扰导致语义完整性下降和性能退化的问题，本文通过细粒度分析发现，声学与语义建模在共享深层参数空间时产生的梯度冲突是根本原因。为此，作者提出Lychee-FD框架，采用分层参数分离策略，在深层解耦冲突模态的同时，通过专用语义对齐通道保持跨模态一致性。实验表明，该方法在多个基准测试中显著提升性能：口语问答准确率提升7.4%，全双工交互流畅度提升28.5%，且不牺牲推理效率，是首个同时揭示模态干扰成因并提供实用高效全双工模型解决方案的工作。
UniLM-Nav: A Unified Framework for Zero-Shot Last-Mile Navigation
Authors: Zhuofan Zhang, Tianxu Wang, Guoxi Zhang, Yixiong Lin, Xilin Wang, Hongming Xu, Qing Li, Song-Chun Zhu, Lifeng Fan
First: 2026-07-07T17:42:08+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-07T17:42:08+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Mobile manipulation requires a robot to navigate to a target object or receptacle and then perform intended manipulation. However, reaching the vicinity of the target does not guarantee a manipulation-ready base pose, a problem known as last-mile navigation. Prior methods for last-mile navigation either rely on manual pose annotation or task-specific training, limiting their scalability to open-vocabulary settings with fine-grained spatial constraints. We propose UniLM-Nav, a unified framework for zero-shot open-vocabulary last-mile navigation. UniLM-Nav decomposes last-mile navigation into view selection, task-conditioned affordance grounding, and geometry-aware base-pose reasoning, all resolved with a shared multimodal large language model (MLLM) backend. Specifically, UniLM-Nav first selects a reference view that best captures the target object or receptacle from recently collected observations. It then grounds task-relevant affordance point in the selected view and lifts the result into the robot-centric coordinate frame. Finally, conditioned on the grounded affordance, task context, and robot geometry, it infers a manipulation-ready base pose for the robot. We evaluate UniLM-Nav on the OVMM benchmark, where it outperforms the previous state-of-the-art method, MoTo, by 3.13 percentage points. Analyses show that the components of our method are crucial to final performance, and that the choice of MLLM also has a substantial effect. We further deploy UniLM-Nav on a Unitree B2 quadruped robot with a 6-DoF Unitree Z1 manipulator, validating its applicability to real-world mobile manipulation tasks.
中文标题/摘要
标题：UniLM-Nav：面向零样本开放词汇最后一公里导航的统一框架
移动操作要求机器人导航至目标物体或容器并执行预定操作。然而，到达目标附近并不能保证机器人具备操作所需的基座位姿，这一问题被称为最后一公里导航。此前方法要么依赖人工位姿标注，要么需要任务特定训练，限制了其在开放词汇且具有细粒度空间约束场景下的可扩展性。我们提出UniLM-Nav，一种面向零样本开放词汇最后一公里导航的统一框架。UniLM-Nav将最后一公里导航分解为视图选择、任务条件下的可操作性锚定与几何感知的基座位姿推理，所有步骤均由共享的多模态大语言模型（MLLM）后端解决。具体而言，UniLM-Nav首先从近期采集的观测中选择最能体现目标物体或容器的参考视图；接着在选定视图中锚定任务相关的可操作点，并将其提升至机器人中心坐标系；最后，基于锚定的可操作性、任务上下文和机器人几何结构，推断出机器人可执行操作的基座位姿。我们在OVMM基准上评估UniLM-Nav，其性能比先前最先进方法MoTo高出3.13个百分点。分析表明，各组件对最终性能至关重要，且MLLM的选择对性能有显著影响。我们进一步在配备6自由度Unitree Z1机械臂的Unitree B2四足机器人上部署UniLM-Nav，验证其在真实移动操作任务中的适用性。
Summary / 总结
To address the challenge of last-mile navigation in mobile manipulation—where reaching a target’s vicinity does not ensure a manipulation-ready robot pose—UniLM-Nav introduces a unified zero-shot framework leveraging a shared multimodal large language model. The method decomposes navigation into three stages: selecting an optimal reference view, grounding task-relevant affordance points within that view, and reasoning a robot-centric base pose conditioned on affordance, task context, and robot geometry. Evaluated on the OVMM benchmark, UniLM-Nav outperforms the prior state-of-the-art MoTo by 3.13 percentage points, with ablation studies confirming the necessity of each component and the impact of MLLM choice. The framework is further validated on a real Unitree B2 quadruped robot with a Z1 manipulator, demonstrating practical applicability.
为解决移动操作中‘最后一公里导航’难题——即抵达目标位置并不保证机器人具备操作所需姿态——UniLM-Nav 提出了一种统一的零样本框架，基于共享的多模态大语言模型。该方法将任务分解为三步：选择最佳参考视角、在该视角内定位任务相关可操作点、并结合操作上下文与机器人几何结构推理出操作准备姿态。在 OVMM 数据集上，UniLM-Nav 比当前最优方法 MoTo 提高 3.13 个百分点，消融实验验证各组件必要性及大模型选择的重要性。该框架已在 Unitree B2 四足机器人搭载 Z1 机械臂上成功部署，证实其在真实移动操作场景中的可行性。
PerCaM-Health: Personalized Dynamic Causal Graphs for Healthcare Reasoning
Authors: Elahe Khatibi, Ziyu Wang, Saba A. Farahani, Di Huang, Hung Cao, Ramesh Jain, Amir M. Rahmani
First: 2026-05-08T05:31:45+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-07T17:41:45+00:00
Comments: This paper has been withdrawn by the authors to allow further internal review of privacy, permission, and attribution considerations before public dissemination
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Personalized healthcare decisions require reasoning about how physiological and behavioral variables influence an individual patient over time. Existing temporal causal discovery methods are poorly matched to this setting: cohort-level models provide stable but non-personalized structures, while per-patient discovery is unreliable because individual trajectories are short, noisy, irregular, and non-stationary. This creates a fundamental gap between population-level causal modeling and the patient-specific, time-varying mechanisms needed for intervention reasoning. We introduce PerCaM-Health, a framework for learning personalized dynamic causal graphs from longitudinal health data. The framework learns a knowledge-guided population temporal graph, then conservatively adapts and evolves it using patient-specific temporal evidence and rolling-window updates, producing interpretable and auditable graph sequences. By coupling these graphs with temporal structural equations, the framework enables patient-level counterfactual queries, such as estimating short-horizon outcome changes under hypothetical behavioral interventions. Experiments on a semi-synthetic dynamic health benchmark show that PerCaM-Health improves graph recovery, dynamic edge tracking, and intervention direction accuracy compared to cohort-level, per-patient, and non-personalized temporal baselines. These results demonstrate that jointly modeling personalization and temporal evolution yields more reliable causal structure and intervention reasoning.
中文标题/摘要
标题：PerCaM-Health：面向医疗决策的个性化动态因果图
个性化医疗决策需要推理生理和行为变量如何随时间影响个体患者。现有时序因果发现方法难以适应此场景：群体级模型提供稳定但非个性化的结构，而每位患者的发现则不可靠，因为个体轨迹短、噪声大、不规则且非平稳。这导致群体级因果建模与干预推理所需的患者特异性、时变机制之间存在根本性差距。我们提出PerCaM-Health框架，从纵向健康数据中学习个性化动态因果图。该框架首先学习知识引导的群体时序图，再通过患者特异性时序证据和滚动窗口更新保守地适应和演化该图，生成可解释且可审计的图序列。通过结合这些图与时序结构方程，框架支持患者级反事实查询，如估计在假设行为干预下的短期结果变化。在半合成动态健康基准上的实验表明，PerCaM-Health相比群体级、每位患者级及非个性化时序基线，在图恢复、动态边追踪和干预方向准确性方面均有提升。结果表明，联合建模个性化与时序演化可获得更可靠的因果结构和干预推理。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for personalized healthcare reasoning that accounts for individual patient dynamics over time, PerCaM-Health introduces a framework that learns personalized dynamic causal graphs from longitudinal health data. It begins by constructing a knowledge-guided population-level temporal graph, then adaptively refines it per patient using temporal evidence and rolling-window updates to handle short, noisy, and non-stationary trajectories. Experimental results on a semi-synthetic health benchmark show that this approach outperforms cohort-level, per-patient, and non-personalized baselines in graph recovery, dynamic edge tracking, and intervention direction accuracy, demonstrating that jointly modeling personalization and temporal evolution enhances causal reasoning and counterfactual prediction.
为解决个性化医疗决策中对个体患者随时间变化的生理与行为变量因果关系建模需求，本文提出PerCaM-Health框架，从纵向健康数据中学习个性化动态因果图。该框架首先构建知识引导的群体级时序图，再通过患者特定期望证据和滚动窗口更新保守地适应并演化该图，确保可解释性和可审计性。在半合成健康基准实验中，PerCaM-Health在图恢复、动态边追踪及干预方向预测上优于群体级、单患者及非个性化基线方法，表明联合建模个性化与时序演化可提升因果结构与干预推理的可靠性。
Neural-ESO: A Dual-Pathway Architecture for Provably Robust Learning-Based Control
Authors: Fan Zhang, Richie Suganda, Jinfeng Chen, Wenhua Liu, Hantao Fu, Bin Hu, Qin Lin
First: 2026-07-07T17:41:38+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-07T17:41:38+00:00
Comments: Accepted to IEEE RA-L
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
A learning-enabled disturbance-rejection framework based on a Neural Extended State Observer (Neural-ESO) is presented in this letter. Unlike existing learning-based control methods that largely rely on the learned model once deployed, Neural-ESO adopts a dual-pathway architecture: a predictive pathway uses a neural network to provide a feedforward disturbance estimate that accelerates convergence, while a corrective pathway employs a conventional ESO to compensate prediction errors and prevent over-reliance on the neural component. Using Lyapunov theory and a small-gain analysis, we show that enforcing a Lipschitz bound on the learning component guarantees uniform ultimate boundedness of the closed-loop error dynamics. The proposed framework is validated on a quadrotor landing task subject to strong ground-effect disturbances across normal and out-of-distribution scenarios, demonstrating accuracy-robustness trade-off and greater operational reliability during training, deployment, and transfer compared with state-of-the-art baselines.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Neural-ESO：一种具有可证明鲁棒性的学习控制双路径架构
本文提出了一种基于神经扩展状态观测器（Neural-ESO）的学习型抗干扰框架。与现有依赖部署后学习模型的学习控制方法不同，Neural-ESO采用双路径架构：预测路径利用神经网络提供前馈扰动估计以加速收敛，校正路径则采用传统ESO补偿预测误差，避免过度依赖神经网络部分。通过李雅普诺夫理论与小增益分析，我们证明：对学习组件施加利普希茨有界性可保证闭环误差动力学的均匀最终有界性。该框架在受强地面效应扰动影响的四旋翼着陆任务中得到验证，涵盖正常与分布外场景，展现出优于现有最先进基线方法的精度-鲁棒性权衡及更可靠的训练、部署与迁移性能。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for robust learning-based control systems that maintain performance under disturbances without over-relying on learned models, this work introduces Neural-ESO, a dual-pathway architecture combining a neural network for fast disturbance prediction with a conventional Extended State Observer for error correction. The method ensures stability via Lyapunov analysis and small-gain theory, requiring only a Lipschitz bound on the neural component to guarantee uniform ultimate boundedness of system errors. Experiments on a quadrotor landing task under strong ground-effect disturbances show that Neural-ESO achieves better accuracy-robustness balance and operational reliability across in-distribution and out-of-distribution scenarios compared to state-of-the-art baselines.
为提升学习控制在不确定干扰下的鲁棒性，本文提出Neural-ESO双路径架构，结合神经网络快速预测干扰与传统扩展状态观测器进行误差补偿。该方法通过李雅普诺夫分析与小增益理论保证稳定性，仅需神经组件满足利普希茨约束。在强地面效应干扰下的四旋翼着陆实验中，Neural-ESO在分布内与分布外场景下均表现出更优的精度-鲁棒性权衡及更高的运行可靠性，优于现有方法。
CAIRN: Cross-Room 3D Scene Understanding with Topology-Aware Large Multimodal Models
Authors: He Liang, Chenyang Ma, Yiming Zhang, Sangyun Shin, Andrew Markham, Niki Trigoni, Yuhang He
First: 2026-07-07T17:39:41+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-07T17:39:41+00:00
Comments: Project Page: https://oceansdepp.github.io/cairn_web/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Existing 3D scene-grounded Large Language Models (3D-LLMs) focus on answering questions grounded in simplified single-room 3D scenes, lacking the ability to reason over real-world household environments containing multiple interconnected rooms and diverse object categories. We introduce CAIRN, a topology-aware 3D-LLM for multi-room 3D scene understanding. CAIRN aligns transformer attention with scene hierarchy, giving the model explicit awareness of object-level relations and room-level connectivity. It enriches object tokens with room-local relational context via a graph neural network, introduces learned room tokens for room-level abstraction, and applies a hierarchical attention mask with geometric bias to route information according to scene topology. CAIRN is developed on CAIRN-MR, a benchmark we introduce on HM3D for multi-room 3D scene understanding, covering grounding, captioning, and four question-answering tasks that progressively evaluate from intra-room perception to cross-room reasoning. Experiments show that CAIRN outperforms prior 3D-LLMs by a large margin across all CAIRN-MR tasks while remaining competitive on five single-room benchmarks.
中文标题/摘要
标题：CAIRN：基于拓扑感知的大规模多模态模型的跨房间3D场景理解
现有的3D场景接地大型语言模型（3D-LLMs）主要关注在简化单房间3D场景中回答问题，缺乏对包含多个互联房间和多样物体类别的真实家庭环境进行推理的能力。我们提出了CAIRN，一种拓扑感知的3D-LLM，用于多房间3D场景理解。CAIRN将Transformer注意力与场景层次结构对齐，使模型具备明确的物体级关系和房间级连通性感知。它通过图神经网络丰富物体标记的房间局部关系上下文，引入学习到的房间标记以实现房间级抽象，并应用带有几何偏置的分层注意力掩码，根据场景拓扑路由信息。CAIRN基于我们为多房间3D场景理解在HM3D上引入的新基准CAIRN-MR开发，涵盖接地、描述和四项逐步评估从房间内感知到跨房间推理的问题回答任务。实验表明，CAIRN在所有CAIRN-MR任务上显著优于现有3D-LLMs，同时在五个单房间基准上保持竞争力。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitation of existing 3D-LLMs that can only handle simplified single-room scenes, CAIRN introduces a topology-aware architecture to enable reasoning across multiple interconnected rooms in real-world household environments. It achieves this by aligning transformer attention with scene hierarchy, enriching object tokens with room-local relational context via graph neural networks, introducing learned room tokens for abstraction, and applying geometrically biased hierarchical attention masks to route information according to spatial topology. Evaluated on the newly proposed CAIRN-MR benchmark covering grounding, captioning, and four QA tasks that escalate from intra-room to cross-room reasoning, CAIRN significantly outperforms prior 3D-LLMs across all tasks while maintaining competitive performance on five single-room benchmarks.
为解决现有3D-LLM仅能处理简化单房间场景的问题，CAIRN提出一种拓扑感知架构以支持跨房间3D场景理解。其通过将Transformer注意力与场景层次对齐，利用图神经网络增强物体令牌的房间局部关系上下文，引入可学习房间令牌进行抽象，并采用带几何偏置的分层注意力掩码按空间拓扑路由信息。在新提出的CAIRN-MR基准（覆盖HM3D多房间场景的定位、描述及四项问答任务）上，CAIRN在所有任务中显著优于现有模型，同时在五个单房间基准上保持竞争力。
GraphBU: MILP Instance Generation with Graph-Native Block Units
Authors: Xiaolei Guo, Chenyu Zhou, Jianghao Lin, Dongdong Ge
First: 2026-07-07T17:39:06+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-07T17:39:06+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Mixed-integer linear programming (MILP) instances used for solver development are hard to obtain when models come from private or application-specific pipelines. A generator must keep the structure that solvers and learned policies rely on. Existing general generators usually choose their generation unit from a formulation template, summary statistics, local graph edits, or blocks found after recombination. These units do not explicitly record how a local part of the MILP is coupled to the rest of the instance. We propose GraphBU, a graph-native generator whose basic unit is a local subproblem plus its interface. The method promotes coupling nodes into master constraints or boundary variables and uses the resulting block units for compatibility-checked replacement. The analysis focuses on the properties needed by this construction: promotion separates interfaces, replacement can preserve feasibility under an interface-slack condition, and the graph construction is invariant to row-column permutations. On MILP instances generation, this unit keeps graph statistics close to the source family, preserves feasibility on most datasets, and improves downstream Predict-and-Search training. Genrated by GraphBU, The average graph-statistical similarity was approximately 0.934, the average feasibility was approximately 96.7%, and the average increase in the main index of downstream PS was approximately 8.0%.
中文标题/摘要
标题：GraphBU：基于图原生块单元的混合整数线性规划实例生成
用于求解器开发的混合整数线性规划（MILP）实例，当模型来自私有或应用特定管道时难以获取。生成器必须保持求解器和学习策略所依赖的结构。现有通用生成器通常从公式模板、摘要统计、局部图编辑或重组后发现的块中选择生成单元。这些单元未显式记录MILP局部部分如何与实例其余部分耦合。我们提出GraphBU，一种图原生生成器，其基本单元为局部子问题及其接口。该方法促进节点耦合至主约束或边界变量，并使用生成的块单元进行兼容性检查的替换。分析聚焦于该构造所需性质：提升分离接口，替换可在接口松弛条件下保持可行性，且图构造对行-列置换不变。在MILP实例生成中，该单元保持图统计接近源族，多数数据集保持可行性，并提升下游Predict-and-Search训练效果。由GraphBU生成的实例，平均图统计相似度约为0.934，平均可行性约为96.7%，下游PS主指标平均提升约8.0%。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the difficulty of obtaining realistic MILP instances from private or application-specific pipelines, GraphBU introduces a graph-native generator that constructs instances using local subproblems and their interfaces as basic units. The method promotes coupling of nodes into master constraints or boundary variables, enabling compatibility-checked replacement of blocks while preserving structural and feasibility properties. Experimental results show that GraphBU maintains high graph-statistical similarity (0.934) to source families, achieves 96.7% average feasibility, and improves downstream Predict-and-Search performance by 8.0% on average.
为了解决从私有或应用特定管道获取真实MILP实例的困难，GraphBU提出一种基于图结构的生成器，以局部子问题及其接口作为基本生成单元。该方法通过将节点耦合到主约束或边界变量，形成块单元，并支持在接口松弛条件下保持可行性的兼容替换。实验表明，GraphBU在图统计相似性上平均达0.934，可行性平均为96.7%，且显著提升下游Predict-and-Search性能约8.0%。
The Large Cancer Assistant (LCA): A Model-Agnostic Orchestration Framework for Scalable Clinical Decision Support in Oncology
Authors: Ghassen Marrakchi, Basarab Matei
First: 2026-07-07T17:38:47+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-07T17:38:47+00:00
Comments: 22 pages, 6 figures, 8 tables, 9 appendices, 14 references, Elsevier JBI format
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
- Objective: Multimodal deep learning models in oncology are currently limited by monolithic designs that rigidly couple data ingestion, clinical routing, and artificial intelligence (AI) inference. To address this inflexibility, we propose the Large Cancer Assistant (LCA), a model-agnostic, post-hoc orchestration framework designed for scalable clinical decision support. - Methods: The LCA is mathematically formalized as a 7-tuple architecture grounded in the principle of Algorithmic Impermeability, ensuring the orchestration logic remains strictly independent of underlying black-box AI models. We introduce the Entry Theory, leveraging Geometric Deep Learning (GDL) to standardize multimodal patient data along distinct structural and medical axes. The system dynamically orchestrates data via a Cancer Switching Module and intentionally isolates the core AI execution from volatile hospital IT infrastructures by outputting a Standardized Intermediate Payload (SIP). - Results: A Proof of Concept (PoC) validated the orchestration logic across four technical scenarios. The framework executed a nominal flow with negligible orchestration overhead. It empirically demonstrated algorithmic impermeability by maintaining an invariant routing projection during AI model swaps, and it validated strict failure-safety by achieving a 100\% recall rate in generating targeted Supplementary Data Requests (SDR) under injected data anomalies. Multi-protocol execution capability was also successfully verified. - Conclusion: By structurally decoupling multimodal ingestion from feature inference, the LCA provides a highly adaptable and modular orchestration foundation. The SIP establishes a clear architectural boundary, natively setting the stage for downstream Electronic Medical Record (EMR) interoperability as an independent future paradigm.
中文标题/摘要
标题：大型癌症助手（LCA）：一种面向肿瘤学可扩展临床决策支持的模型无关编排框架
目标：当前肿瘤学中的多模态深度学习模型受限于将数据摄取、临床路由和人工智能（AI）推理刚性耦合的单体设计。为解决此僵化问题，我们提出大型癌症助手（LCA），一种模型无关、事后编排框架，旨在实现可扩展的临床决策支持。方法：LCA在算法不可渗透原则基础上，数学形式化为七元组架构，确保编排逻辑与底层黑盒AI模型严格独立。我们引入入口理论，利用几何深度学习（GDL）沿不同结构与医学轴标准化多模态患者数据。系统通过癌症切换模块动态编排数据，并通过输出标准化中间载荷（SIP）有意隔离核心AI执行与易变的医院IT基础设施。结果：概念验证（PoC）在四个技术场景中验证了编排逻辑。框架执行标准流程，编排开销可忽略。实证表明算法不可渗透性——在AI模型切换过程中保持路由投影不变；并通过注入数据异常验证了严格故障安全——在生成目标补充数据请求（SDR）时达到100%召回率。多协议执行能力亦成功验证。结论：通过结构化解耦多模态数据摄取与特征推理，LCA提供高度可适应且模块化的编排基础。SIP建立清晰架构边界，原生支持未来作为独立范式与电子病历（EMR）的互操作性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the rigid coupling of data ingestion, routing, and AI inference in current oncology multimodal models, the Large Cancer Assistant (LCA) introduces a model-agnostic orchestration framework to enable scalable clinical decision support. It formalizes orchestration as a 7-tuple architecture based on Algorithmic Impermeability, ensuring logic independence from underlying AI models. The system employs Entry Theory with Geometric Deep Learning to standardize patient data, uses a Cancer Switching Module for dynamic data orchestration, and outputs a Standardized Intermediate Payload (SIP) to isolate AI execution from volatile hospital IT. Experimental results from a Proof of Concept across four scenarios confirmed negligible orchestration overhead, maintained invariant routing during model swaps, achieved 100% recall in generating Supplementary Data Requests under data anomalies, and verified multi-protocol execution capability.
为解决当前肿瘤学多模态模型中数据摄入、路由与AI推理紧密耦合导致的灵活性不足问题，本文提出模型无关的大型癌症助手（LCA）编排框架，以支持可扩展的临床决策支持。该框架以算法不可渗透性为原则，形式化为七元组架构，确保编排逻辑与底层AI模型解耦。系统采用几何深度学习标准化患者数据，通过癌症切换模块动态编排数据，并输出标准化中间载荷（SIP）以隔离AI执行与医院IT环境波动。实验验证表明，编排开销可忽略，模型更换时路由投影保持不变，数据异常下补充数据请求召回率达100%，且支持多协议执行。
Deep Reinforcement Learning for Dynamic Origin-Destination Matrix Estimation in Microscopic Traffic Simulations Considering Credit Assignment
Authors: Donggyu Min, Seongjin Choi, Dong-Kyu Kim
First: 2025-11-09T05:07:42+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-07T17:38:23+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
This paper focuses on dynamic origin-destination matrix estimation (DODE), a crucial calibration process necessary for the effective application of microscopic traffic simulations. The fundamental challenge of the DODE problem in microscopic simulations stems from the complex temporal dynamics and inherent uncertainty of individual vehicle dynamics. This makes it highly challenging to precisely determine which vehicle traverses which link at any given moment, resulting in intricate and often ambiguous relationships between origin-destination (OD) matrices and their contributions to resultant link flows. This phenomenon constitutes the credit assignment problem, a central challenge addressed in this study. We formulate the DODE problem as a Markov Decision Process (MDP) and propose a novel framework that applies model-free deep reinforcement learning (DRL). Within our proposed framework, the agent learns an optimal policy to sequentially generate OD matrices, refining its strategy through direct interaction with the simulation environment. This approach was evaluated through a toy experiment on the Nguyen-Dupuis network and a case study utilizing an actual highway subnetwork spanning Santa Clara and San Jose. Experimental results show that the proposed method consistently improves calibration performance relative to the strongest conventional baseline, reducing link-flow MSE by 23.7% in the toy experiment and by 59.2-88.3% in the real-world case study. By reframing DODE as a sequential decision-making problem, our approach addresses the credit assignment challenge through a learned policy and provides a novel framework for calibration of microscopic traffic simulations.
中文标题/摘要
标题：考虑信用分配的微观交通仿真中动态OD矩阵估计的深度强化学习方法
本文聚焦于动态OD矩阵估计（DODE），这是微观交通仿真有效应用的关键校准过程。微观仿真中DODE的核心挑战源于个体车辆动态的复杂时变性和固有不确定性，导致难以精确确定任意时刻哪辆车通过哪条路段，从而形成OD矩阵与其对路段流量贡献之间复杂且常具歧义的关系，即信用分配问题。本文将DODE建模为马尔可夫决策过程（MDP），提出一种基于无模型深度强化学习（DRL）的新型框架。在该框架中，智能体通过与仿真环境直接交互，学习生成OD矩阵的最优策略，逐步优化其策略。该方法在Nguyen-Dupuis网络的玩具实验和覆盖圣克拉拉与圣何塞的实际高速公路子网络案例研究中进行了评估。实验结果表明，该方法相较最强传统基线方法持续提升校准性能，在玩具实验中将路段流量均方误差降低23.7%，在真实案例研究中降低59.2%-88.3%。通过将DODE重构为序列决策问题，本文方法通过学习策略解决信用分配挑战，为微观交通仿真的校准提供了新框架。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the difficulty of accurately assigning credit in dynamic origin-destination matrix estimation for microscopic traffic simulations, this study formulates the problem as a Markov Decision Process and applies a model-free deep reinforcement learning framework to learn an optimal policy for sequentially generating OD matrices. Evaluated on both a toy Nguyen-Dupuis network and a real-world highway subnetwork between Santa Clara and San Jose, the method consistently outperformed the strongest baseline, reducing link-flow mean squared error by 23.7% in the toy case and by 59.2–88.3% in the real-world scenario.
为解决微观交通仿真中动态起讫矩阵估计的信用分配难题，本文将该问题建模为马尔可夫决策过程，并提出一种基于模型无关深度强化学习的框架，通过与仿真环境交互学习最优策略以顺序生成起讫矩阵。在Nguyen-Dupuis网络的玩具实验和圣克拉拉至圣何塞高速公路子网的实际案例中，该方法均显著优于最强传统基线，将链路流量均方误差分别降低23.7%和59.2–88.3%，验证了其通过学习式序列决策有效应对信用分配挑战的能力。
A Gibbs posterior sampler for inverse problem based on prior diffusion model
Authors: Jean-François Giovannelli
First: 2026-02-11T17:28:11+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-07T17:38:19+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
This paper addresses the issue of inversion in cases where (1) the observation system is modeled by a linear transformation and additive error, (2) the problem is ill-posed and regularization relies on a Bayesian strategy, (3)~the prior is modeled by a diffusion process adjusted on an available large set of examples. In this context, it is known that the issue of posterior sampling is a thorny one and the paper introduces a Gibbs algorithm. It appears that this avenue has not been explored, and we show that it is particularly effective and remarkably simple. In addition, it provides clear elements regarding convergence guarantees in a specific case and arguments supporting such guarantees in practical cases. The results are clearly confirmed by numerical simulations based on a toy example.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于先验扩散模型的逆问题Gibbs后验采样器
本文处理了以下情况下的逆问题：(1) 观测系统由线性变换和加性误差建模；(2) 问题不适定，正则化依赖于贝叶斯策略；(3) 先验由在可用大量示例上调整的扩散过程建模。在此背景下，后验采样问题尤为棘手，本文引入了一种Gibbs算法。该方法此前未被探索，我们证明其特别有效且极其简单。此外，本文还提供了特定情况下收敛保证的明确要素，并在实际情况下支持此类保证的论证。结果通过基于玩具示例的数值模拟得到明确验证。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to solve ill-posed inverse problems under linear observation models with Bayesian regularization and diffusion-based priors, this paper proposes a Gibbs posterior sampler that leverages the structure of diffusion processes trained on large datasets. The method constructs a sampling algorithm that efficiently explores the posterior distribution, offering both theoretical convergence guarantees in specific cases and practical support for broader applicability. Numerical experiments on a toy example confirm its effectiveness and simplicity, demonstrating strong performance in recovering latent variables from noisy observations.
为解决线性观测模型下、基于扩散模型先验的病态逆问题，本文提出一种基于Gibbs采样的后验推断方法。该方法利用在大规模数据上训练的扩散模型结构，高效探索后验分布，展现出简洁性和实用性。在玩具示例的数值实验中，结果验证了算法在特定情形下的收敛性，并提供了实际场景下的收敛保障论证。
Life Style Levels: Neighborhood Delineation using Geospatial Data
Authors: Srivatsa Kulkarni, Debarag Banerjee
First: 2026-07-07T17:37:03+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-07T17:37:03+00:00
Comments: 43 pages, 38 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Fine-scale socioeconomic information is often unavailable across rapidly ur-banizing regions of the developing world, like India, limiting the ability to delineate intra-urban variations in affluence and deprivation. This study pro-poses a scalable, grid-based urban delineation framework using building morphology derived from open-source satellite imagery. Urban areas across 59 Indian cities and towns are partitioned into high-resolution spatial grids and characterized using interpretable morphological indicators, which are combined into a transparent, rule-based scoring framework to delineate areas with contrasting levels of urban affluence. The resulting classifications are validated through ground-level Google Street View observations, revealing a sharp contrast between the grid classes which are consistent with the ex-pected effects of the lifestyle affluence indicators. We further investigate density-based clustering of building footprints in Mumbai to identify dense urban settlements, demonstrating that the resulting clusters exhibit substan-tial spatial overlap with known informal settlements across the city. Finally, we conduct an exploratory analysis mapping consumer loan delinquency across the derived affluence classes. By relying entirely on publicly available geospatial data, the proposed framework provides a scalable, interpretable, and cost-effective approach for granular urban affluence mapping across In-dian cities.
