SMART: A Machine Learning and Monte Carlo Framework for Rapid Analysis of Stochastic Transistor Aging and Process Variation in Digital Circuits
Authors: Arash Esshaghi, Siavash Es'haghi, Gholamreza Shahabadi, Alireza Moradi
First: 2026-07-06T15:06:34+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-07T17:35:12+00:00
Comments: Submitted to Engineering Reports, Under Review
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
As CMOS technology scales into the deep nanometer regime, digital circuit reliability is increasingly threatened by the combined stochastic effects of Bias Temperature Instability (BTI) and Process Variation (PV). Traditional reliability analysis methods, which rely on computationally intensive simulations or extensive lookup tables, fail to scale efficiently for large designs, creating a critical bottleneck in design space exploration. To address this, we propose SMART, a novel framework that integrates Machine Learning (ML) with Monte Carlo simulation to enable rapid, high-fidelity reliability analysis. SMART employs Random Forest regression to predict gate delay distributions directly, bypassing time-consuming atomic model parameter extractions. Crucially, the model utilizes Bayesian Optimization for automated hyperparameter tuning, ensuring maximum predictive robustness across diverse libraries. Experimental validation on ISCAS85 benchmark circuits demonstrates that SMART achieves a 94.54% reduction in analysis time compared to state-of-the-art methods, while maintaining a remarkable average accuracy error of just 1.63%. By shifting computational complexity to an offline training phase, the proposed framework offers a scalable, accurate solution for designing resilient, reliability-aware digital systems.
中文标题/摘要
标题：SMART：一种结合机器学习与蒙特卡洛方法的框架，用于快速分析数字电路中随机晶体管老化与工艺变化
随着CMOS技术进入深纳米尺度，数字电路可靠性正日益受到偏置温度不稳定性（BTI）与工艺变化（PV）的随机效应的威胁。传统可靠性分析方法依赖计算密集型仿真或庞大的查找表，难以高效扩展至大型设计，成为设计空间探索的关键瓶颈。为此，我们提出SMART框架，该框架结合机器学习与蒙特卡洛模拟，实现快速、高保真度的可靠性分析。SMART采用随机森林回归直接预测门延迟分布，避免耗时的原子模型参数提取。关键的是，该模型采用贝叶斯优化自动调整超参数，确保在不同库中预测的稳健性。在ISCAS85基准电路上的实验验证表明，SMART相比现有最先进方法分析时间减少94.54%，同时保持平均精度误差仅为1.63%。通过将计算复杂度转移至离线训练阶段，该框架提供了一种可扩展、精确的解决方案，用于设计具有韧性和可靠性意识的数字系统。
Summary / 总结
As CMOS technology advances into deep nanometer scales, digital circuit reliability faces growing threats from stochastic effects like Bias Temperature Instability and Process Variation, which traditional simulation-based methods struggle to handle efficiently for large-scale designs. SMART addresses this by combining Machine Learning with Monte Carlo simulation, using Random Forest regression to predict gate delay distributions without costly atomic model parameter extractions, and Bayesian Optimization to tune model hyperparameters for robustness. Experiments on ISCAS85 benchmarks show SMART reduces analysis time by 94.54% while maintaining an average accuracy error of only 1.63%, demonstrating its effectiveness in enabling scalable, high-fidelity reliability analysis for digital circuits.
随着CMOS技术进入深纳米尺度，随机老化与工艺变异对数字电路可靠性构成威胁，传统基于仿真的方法难以应对大规模设计的计算需求。SMART通过结合机器学习与蒙特卡洛模拟，利用随机森林回归直接预测门延迟分布，避免原子模型参数提取，同时采用贝叶斯优化自动调参以确保鲁棒性。在ISCAS85基准电路上的实验表明，SMART将分析时间缩短94.54%，同时保持仅1.63%的平均精度误差，为数字电路设计提供可扩展的可靠性分析方案。
Beyond Reactivity: Measuring Proactive Problem Solving in LLM Agents
Authors: Gil Pasternak, Dheeraj Rajagopal, Julia White, Dhruv Atreja, Matthew Thomas, George Hurn-Maloney, Ash Lewis
First: 2025-10-22T17:00:45+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-07T17:34:06+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
LLM-based agents are increasingly moving towards proactivity: rather than awaiting instruction, they exercise agency to anticipate user needs and solve them autonomously. However, evaluating proactivity is challenging; current benchmarks are constrained to localized context, limiting their ability to test reasoning across sources and longer time horizons. To address this gap, we present PROBE (Proactive Resolution Of BottlEnecks). PROBE decomposes proactivity as a pipeline of three core capabilities: (1) searching for unspecified issues, (2) identifying specific bottlenecks, and (3) executing appropriate resolutions. We apply PROBE to evaluate leading LLMs and popular agentic frameworks, showing that even state-of-the-art models struggle to solve this benchmark. Computing our consistent measurements across frontier LLMs and agents, we find that the best end-to-end performance of 40% is achieved by both GPT-5 and Claude Opus-4.1. Additionally, we demonstrate the relative capabilities of each model and analyze mutual failure modes. Our results highlight the current limitations of autonomous action in agentic systems, and expose promising future research directions.
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to evaluate LLM agents' ability to act proactively rather than reactively, the authors introduce PROBE, a benchmark that measures three core capabilities: identifying unspecified issues, pinpointing specific bottlenecks, and executing resolutions. Applied to leading LLMs and agentic frameworks, PROBE reveals that even top models struggle, with the best end-to-end performance at 40% achieved by GPT-5 and Claude Opus-4.1. The study identifies consistent failure patterns across models and underscores current limitations in autonomous problem-solving within agentic systems.
为评估大语言模型代理在无需指令情况下主动解决问题的能力，该研究提出PROBE基准，将其分解为三个核心能力：发现未明确的问题、定位具体瓶颈、执行相应解决方案。在主流模型和框架上的测试显示，即使顶尖模型也表现不佳，GPT-5与Claude Opus-4.1以40%的最高端到端表现领先。研究还揭示了各模型的共性失败模式，凸显当前自主行动能力的局限，并指明未来研究方向。
Estimation of instrument and noise parameters for inverse problem based on prior diffusion model
Authors: Jean-François Giovannelli
First: 2026-02-12T08:43:11+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-07T17:33:16+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
This article addresses the issue of estimating observation parameters (response and error parameters) in inverse problems. The focus is on cases where regularization is introduced in a Bayesian framework and the prior is modeled by a diffusion process. In this context, the issue of posterior sampling is known to be thorny, and a recent paper proposes a notably simple and effective solution. Additionally, it opens an remarkable flexibility when it comes to estimating observation parameters. The proposed strategy enables to define an optimal estimator for both observation parameters and image of interest. Furthermore, the strategy provides a means for uncertainty quantification. In addition, MCMC algorithms allow for the computation of estimates and properties of posteriors, while offering some guarantees. The paper presents several numerical experiments that clearly confirm the computational efficiency and the quality of both estimates and uncertainty quantification.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于先验扩散模型的逆问题中仪器与噪声参数估计
本文探讨逆问题中观测参数（响应与误差参数）的估计问题。重点研究在贝叶斯框架下引入正则化且先验由扩散过程建模的情形。在此背景下，后验采样问题较为棘手，而近期一篇论文提出了一种简洁有效的解决方案。此外，该方法在估计观测参数时展现出显著的灵活性。所提策略可同时定义观测参数与目标图像的最优估计器，并提供不确定性量化手段。同时，MCMC算法可用于计算后验估计及其性质，并提供一定保障。论文通过多个数值实验，清晰验证了该方法的计算效率及估计与不确定性量化的质量。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of estimating observation parameters in inverse problems under Bayesian regularization with diffusion-based priors, this paper proposes a method leveraging recent advances in posterior sampling to simultaneously estimate both observation parameters and the target image, while enabling uncertainty quantification. The approach employs MCMC algorithms to compute posterior estimates and their properties, offering theoretical guarantees. Experimental results demonstrate the method's computational efficiency and high accuracy in parameter estimation and uncertainty quantification across multiple numerical tests.
为了解决在贝叶斯正则化框架下、以扩散过程建模先验的反问题中观测参数的估计难题，本文提出了一种利用近期后验采样进展的方法，简化并提升了估计效率。该方法可同时最优估计观测参数与目标图像，并通过MCMC算法实现不确定性量化。实验结果表明，该方法计算高效，估计结果准确，不确定性评估可靠。
RSF-GLLM: Bridging the Semantic Gap in Multi-Hop Knowledge Graph QA via Recurrent Soft-Flow and Decoupled LLM Generation
Authors: Sambaran Bandyopadhyay, Ananth Muppidi
Venue: ICML 2026
First: 2026-07-07T17:32:36+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-07T17:32:36+00:00
Comments: Accepted for publication in ICML 2026 as a full research paper; 21 pages
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Multi-hop Question Answering over Knowledge Graphs faces a critical challenge: traditional retrieve-then-read pipelines break differentiability, preventing the retriever from learning to bridge the semantic gap where intermediate nodes lack lexical overlap with the query. To address this, we propose RSF-GLLM, a framework decoupling differentiable graph reasoning from answer generation. Our Recurrent Soft-Flow (RSF) module employs a GRU-guided query updater to propagate continuous relevance scores, utilizing a dynamic gating mechanism to traverse semantically dissimilar bridge nodes via structural cues. We introduce flow sparsity regularization to theoretically guarantee convergence from soft probabilities to discrete reasoning paths. These paths are extracted and textualized to fine-tune a Large Language Model (LLM), ensuring generation is grounded in factual topology. Experiments on WebQSP and CWQ demonstrate that RSF-GLLM achieves competitive performance with superior inference efficiency compared to LLM based computationally expensive approaches.
中文标题/摘要
标题：RSF-GLLM：通过循环软流与解耦大语言模型生成弥合多跳知识图谱问答中的语义鸿沟
知识图谱上的多跳问答面临关键挑战：传统检索后阅读流水线破坏可微性，导致检索器无法学习弥合中间节点与查询间缺乏词法重叠的语义鸿沟。为此，我们提出RSF-GLLM框架，将可微图推理与答案生成解耦。我们的循环软流（RSF）模块采用GRU引导的查询更新器传播连续相关性得分，并利用动态门控机制通过结构线索遍历语义差异大的桥接节点。我们引入流稀疏正则化，从理论上保证从软概率收敛到离散推理路径。这些路径被提取并文本化以微调大语言模型（LLM），确保生成基于事实拓扑结构。在WebQSP和CWQ上的实验表明，RSF-GLLM在性能上具有竞争力，且相比基于LLM的计算密集型方法具有更优的推理效率。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitation of traditional retrieve-then-read pipelines in multi-hop knowledge graph QA, which fail to bridge semantic gaps due to lack of lexical overlap between queries and intermediate nodes, RSF-GLLM decouples differentiable graph reasoning from LLM generation. It employs a Recurrent Soft-Flow module with GRU-guided query updates and dynamic gating to traverse semantically distant nodes using structural cues, while flow sparsity regularization ensures convergence to discrete reasoning paths. These paths are then textualized to fine-tune an LLM, grounding generation in factual topology. Experiments on WebQSP and CWQ show RSF-GLLM achieves competitive accuracy with significantly better inference efficiency than LLM-based methods requiring expensive computation.
为解决传统检索后阅读管道在多跳知识图谱问答中因查询与中间节点语义不匹配导致的语义鸿沟问题，RSF-GLLM将可微图推理与LLM生成解耦。其Recurrent Soft-Flow模块采用GRU引导的查询更新与动态门控机制，利用结构线索遍历语义差异大的节点，并通过流稀疏正则化确保软概率收敛至离散推理路径。这些路径被文本化后用于微调LLM，使生成基于事实拓扑。在WebQSP和CWQ上的实验表明，RSF-GLLM在保持竞争力的同时，显著优于计算开销大的LLM方法，推理效率更高。
DepthWeave-KV: Token-Adaptive Cross-Layer Residual Factorization for Long-Context KV Cache Compression
Authors: Anna Cordoba, Adam Puente Tercero, Nerea Angulo Hijo, Mar Linares Tercero, Julia Barrientos, Ainhoa Miranda, Jesus Olivera
First: 2026-07-07T17:29:01+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-07T17:29:01+00:00
Comments: 9 pages, 2 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Long-context language model inference is increasingly limited by the memory bandwidth and capacity required to store key-value caches, yet existing compression methods often apply uniform budgets across layers or tokens and degrade retrieval when lexical cues and semantic states require different preservation. We introduce DepthWeave-KV, a token-adaptive cache compression method that factorizes key and value states across neighboring transformer layers using shared low-rank channel bases while retaining lightweight token-specific residuals where attention behavior is sensitive. DepthWeave-KV combines cross-depth residual factorization with a token-conditional depth router that allocates higher reconstruction rank to instruction-bearing and retrieval-critical tokens, and uses calibration-free online error tracking from attention-output probes to adapt compression during generation without retraining the base model. A fused CUDA implementation jointly performs basis lookup, residual dequantization, and attention projection to reduce decode-time memory traffic. Across LongBench, Needle-in-a-Haystack, L-Eval, and long-form QA and summarization benchmarks, DepthWeave-KV achieves near-full-cache task quality with substantially lower memory use, improving average score and retrieval accuracy over prior compressed caches while reaching 8.3x KV memory reduction and 72.8 tokens per second at 64K context.
中文标题/摘要
标题：DepthWeave-KV：面向长上下文KV缓存压缩的令牌自适应跨层残差因子分解
长上下文语言模型推理日益受限于存储键值缓存所需的记忆带宽和容量，而现有压缩方法常对各层或各令牌采用统一预算，当词汇线索和语义状态需要不同保留时会导致检索性能下降。我们提出DepthWeave-KV，一种令牌自适应缓存压缩方法，通过共享低秩通道基向量在相邻Transformer层间分解键和值状态，同时保留轻量级令牌特定残差以应对注意力行为敏感的情况。DepthWeave-KV结合跨深度残差因子分解与令牌条件深度路由器，为承载指令和关键检索的令牌分配更高重建秩，并利用注意力输出探针实现无需校准的在线误差追踪，从而在生成过程中动态调整压缩而无需重新训练基础模型。其融合CUDA实现同时执行基向量查找、残差去量化和注意力投影，以降低解码时内存流量。在LongBench、Needle-in-a-Haystack、L-Eval及长文本问答与摘要基准测试中，DepthWeave-KV在显著降低内存使用的同时实现接近完整缓存的任务质量，相较先前压缩缓存方法提升平均得分与检索准确率，同时实现8.3倍KV内存缩减和64K上下文下每秒72.8个令牌的处理速度。
Summary / 总结
To address the memory bottlenecks in long-context language model inference, DepthWeave-KV introduces a token-adaptive compression method that factorizes key-value caches across transformer layers using shared low-rank bases while preserving token-specific residuals for critical attention patterns. It employs a depth router to prioritize compression for less important tokens and uses online error tracking from attention outputs to dynamically adjust compression without retraining. Experimental results show it achieves near-full-cache performance with 8.3x reduction in KV memory usage and 72.8 tokens per second at 64K context, outperforming prior methods on retrieval accuracy and task scores across multiple benchmarks.
为解决长上下文语言模型推理中的内存瓶颈，DepthWeave-KV提出一种基于令牌自适应的缓存压缩方法，通过跨层分解键值状态并共享低秩通道基，同时保留注意力敏感区域的令牌特定残差。该方法结合深度路由器动态分配压缩资源给指令或检索关键令牌，并利用注意力输出探针实现在线误差追踪以动态调整压缩策略，无需重训练模型。实验表明其在多个长文本基准测试中实现接近完整缓存性能，同时降低8.3倍KV内存占用，达到64K上下文下每秒72.8个令牌的推理速度。
Bridging Physical Reasoning and Task Generalization via Visual Action Outcome Reasoning Alignment
Authors: Han-Jun Ko, Jr-Jen Chen, Haobo Yuan, Hsin-Ying Lee, Tiancheng Shen, Ming-Hsuan Yang, Yu-Chiang Frank Wang
Venue: ICML
First: 2026-07-07T17:27:59+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-07T17:27:59+00:00
Comments: ICML'26 Workshop RLxF: Reinforcement Learning from World Feedback
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Vision-language models (VLMs) struggle to generalize in interactive physical reasoning, particularly under unseen tasks and environments. Two key failure modes are prominent: hallucinated chain-of-thought (CoT) reasoning that contradicts physical reality, and misalignment between the model's reasoning and actions. We present VAORA (Visual Action Outcome Reasoning Alignment), a novel reward design that directly addresses both issues. VAORA introduces two complementary rewards: Visual Alignment Reward, which anchors VLM reasoning to the visual context independent of the agent action itself, and Visual-Action Alignment Reward, which grounds reasoning in the visual outcome induced by the model's action. Together, these rewards suppress hallucinated CoT and reduce the gap between reasoning and behavior. To improve training stability, we further employ smooth, dense rewards by estimating success probabilities using a pre-trained in-domain expert agent. Experiments on PHYRE and Virtual Tool support our performances across novel-task and unseen-environment settings, confirming that grounded and generalizable physical intelligence can be induced through VAORA.
中文标题/摘要
标题：通过视觉动作结果推理对齐实现物理推理与任务泛化的桥梁
视觉-语言模型（VLMs）在交互式物理推理中难以泛化，特别是在面对未见过的任务和环境时。主要失败模式包括：与物理现实矛盾的虚构思维链（CoT）推理，以及模型推理与行为之间的错位。我们提出了VAORA（视觉动作结果推理对齐），一种新颖的奖励设计，直接解决上述两个问题。VAORA引入两种互补奖励：视觉对齐奖励，将VLM推理锚定在视觉上下文上，独立于代理的动作本身；视觉-动作对齐奖励，将推理根植于模型动作所引发的视觉结果。二者共同抑制虚构CoT并缩小推理与行为之间的差距。为提升训练稳定性，我们进一步采用平滑、密集奖励，通过预训练的领域内专家代理估计成功概率。在PHYRE和Virtual Tool上的实验验证了我们在新任务和未见环境设置下的性能，证实通过VAORA可诱导出具有根基性和泛化能力的物理智能。
Summary / 总结
Vision-language models often fail to generalize in physical reasoning tasks due to hallucinated reasoning and misalignment between thought processes and actions. VAORA, a novel reward design, tackles both issues by introducing two complementary rewards: one that aligns reasoning with visual context regardless of action, and another that ties reasoning to the actual visual outcome of the agent’s action. These rewards suppress unrealistic reasoning chains and bridge the gap between reasoning and behavior. Enhanced by smooth, dense rewards derived from a pre-trained expert agent, VAORA improves training stability. Experiments on PHYRE and Virtual Tool demonstrate consistent performance across novel tasks and unseen environments, confirming that VAORA enables more grounded and generalizable physical reasoning.
视觉语言模型在物理推理任务中难以泛化，常因生成虚构的推理链和推理与行为脱节而失败。VAORA框架通过引入两种奖励机制解决此问题：一种将推理锚定在视觉上下文（不依赖动作本身），另一种将推理与模型动作引发的视觉结果对齐。这两种奖励共同抑制虚构推理、缩小推理与行为之间的差距。通过引入由预训练专家代理估计的成功概率，VAORA提升了奖励的平滑性和训练稳定性。在PHYRE和Virtual Tool上的实验表明，VAORA在新任务和新环境上均表现优异，证明视觉结果对齐能有效诱导更泛化的物理智能。
Agent Step Value: Probing the Observer Effect in Black-Box Traces
Authors: Andrew Zhang, Chengzhan Li
First: 2026-07-05T17:27:56+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-07T17:26:47+00:00
Comments: aligned with workshop version
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Final-answer scores hide which agent transitions helped or harmed a trace. We introduce Agent Step Value (ASV), a replay framework that scores before/after states with a stateless LLM evaluator over a fixed candidate set. ASV reports entropy movement and Bayesian surprise measure belief movement, while offline gold-margin gain measures movement toward a reviewed target. It also quantifies evaluator-channel sensitivity by replaying the same frozen transitions under changed projection, rationale, prompt, or scoring rules. In a 100-question open-QA study with live PubMed retrieval and DeepSeek log-probability scoring, ASV evaluates 1,100 transitions. Entropy movement is 0.000 while mean Bayesian surprise is 2.693, exposing near-one-hot belief pivots. Under a 128-token rationale-conditioned protocol, mean gold-margin gain is -2.335 (95\% CI [-3.395, -1.272]); direct one-token scoring on the same traces gives +4.033. A 100-transition component audit traces the reversal to short generated rationales over full states. ASV turns prompt sensitivity into a measured channel effect and localizes the largest rationale-conditioned losses to extraction and audit.
中文标题/摘要
标题：代理步骤值：探测黑盒轨迹中的观察者效应
最终答案得分隐藏了哪些代理转换对轨迹有益或有害。我们引入代理步骤值（ASV），一种回放框架，通过无状态LLM评估器在固定候选集上对前后状态进行评分。ASV报告熵变和贝叶斯惊喜度以衡量信念变化，同时离线黄金边际增益衡量向已审核目标的移动。它还通过在改变投影、理由、提示或评分规则下回放相同冻结的转换，量化评估器通道的敏感性。在包含实时PubMed检索和DeepSeek对数概率评分的100题开放问答研究中，ASV评估了1100次转换。熵变值为0.000，平均贝叶斯惊喜度为2.693，揭示了接近独热信念的转变。在128标记理由条件协议下，平均黄金边际增益为-2.335（95%置信区间[-3.395, -1.272]）；对相同轨迹直接进行单标记评分则为+4.033。一个100次转换的组件审计追踪了短生成理由在完整状态下的反转。ASV将提示敏感性转化为可测量的通道效应，并将最大的理由条件损失定位到提取和审计环节。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the lack of transparency in black-box agent traces, this work introduces Agent Step Value (ASV), a replay-based framework that evaluates agent transitions using a stateless LLM evaluator over a fixed candidate set. ASV measures entropy movement, Bayesian surprise, and gold-margin gain to quantify belief shifts and performance impact, while also probing evaluator-channel sensitivity by varying prompts, rationales, or scoring rules. In a 100-question open-QA study with PubMed retrieval and DeepSeek scoring, ASV analyzed 1,100 transitions, revealing near-one-hot belief pivots (entropy movement 0.000, mean Bayesian surprise 2.693) and significant performance degradation under rationale-conditioned scoring (mean gold-margin gain -2.335) compared to direct one-token scoring (+4.033). A component audit traced losses to short rationales, identifying extraction and audit as key failure points.
为了解决黑盒代理轨迹缺乏透明性的问题，本文提出Agent Step Value（ASV），一种基于重放的评估框架，通过固定候选集和无状态LLM评估器对代理过渡进行评分。ASV通过熵变和贝叶斯惊喜度量化信念变化，并通过黄金边际收益衡量向目标答案的进展。在100个开放问答问题的1,100次过渡实验中，熵变化接近零但贝叶斯惊喜度高，表明信念发生锐利转变。在基于理由条件的评分下，ASV显示负黄金边际收益（-2.335），与直接单token评分的正收益（+4.033）形成对比，并定位理由提取和审计为损失的主要来源。
LLM-as-a-Verifier: A General-Purpose Verification Framework
Authors: Jacky Kwok, Shulu Li, Pranav Atreya, Yuejiang Liu, Yixing Jiang, Chelsea Finn, Marco Pavone, Ion Stoica, Azalia Mirhoseini
First: 2026-07-06T17:59:35+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-07T17:26:37+00:00
Comments: Code: https://github.com/llm-as-a-verifier/llm-as-a-verifier Website: https://llm-as-a-verifier.com
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Scaling pre-training, post-training, and test-time compute have become the central paradigms for improving the capabilities of LLMs. In this work, we identify verification, the ability to determine the correctness of a solution, as a new scaling axis. To unlock this and demonstrate its effectiveness, we introduce LLM-as-a-Verifier, a general-purpose verification framework that provides fine-grained feedback for agentic tasks without requiring additional training. Unlike standard LM judges that prompt LLMs to produce discrete scores for candidate solutions, LLM-as-a-Verifier computes the expectation over the distribution of scoring token logits to generate continuous scores. This probabilistic formulation enables verification to scale along multiple dimensions: (1) score granularity, (2) repeated evaluation, and (3) criteria decomposition. In particular, we show that scaling the scoring granularity leads to better separation between positive and negative solutions, resulting in more calibrated comparisons. Moreover, scaling repeated evaluation and criteria decomposition consistently lead to additional gains in verification accuracy through variance and complexity reduction. We further introduce a cost-efficient ranking algorithm for selecting the best solution among candidates using the verifier's continuous scores. LLM-as-a-Verifier achieves state-of-the-art performance on Terminal-Bench V2 (86.5%), SWE-Bench Verified (78.2%), RoboRewardBench (87.4%), and MedAgentBench (73.3%). Beyond verification, the fine-grained signals from LLM-as-a-Verifier can also serve as a proxy for estimating task progress. We build an extension for Claude Code, enabling developers to monitor and improve their own agentic systems. Finally, we show that LLM-as-a-Verifier can provide dense feedback for RL, improving the sample efficiency of SAC and GRPO on robotics and mathematical reasoning benchmarks.
中文标题/摘要
标题：LLM作为验证器：一种通用验证框架
预训练、后训练和测试时计算的扩展已成为提升大语言模型能力的核心范式。本研究将验证能力——即判断解决方案正确性的能力——识别为新的扩展维度。为释放该能力并展示其有效性，我们提出了LLM-as-a-Verifier，一种无需额外训练即可为代理任务提供细粒度反馈的通用验证框架。与标准LM裁判通过提示LLM生成离散分数不同，LLM-as-a-Verifier基于评分token logit分布的期望值生成连续分数。这种概率性建模使验证可沿多个维度扩展：（1）评分粒度，（2）重复评估，（3）标准分解。特别是，我们发现扩展评分粒度可更好地区分正负解，从而实现更校准的比较。此外，重复评估和标准分解的扩展通过降低方差和复杂度，持续提升验证准确性。我们进一步提出一种成本高效的排序算法，利用验证器的连续分数在候选解中选择最优解。LLM-as-a-Verifier在Terminal-Bench V2（86.5%）、SWE-Bench Verified（78.2%）、RoboRewardBench（87.4%）和MedAgentBench（73.3%）上达到最先进性能。除验证外，LLM-as-a-Verifier的细粒度信号还可作为估算任务进度的代理指标。我们为Claude Code构建了扩展功能，使开发者可监控并改进自身代理系统。最后，我们证明LLM-as-a-Verifier可为强化学习提供密集反馈，提升SAC和GRPO在机器人和数学推理基准上的样本效率。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to improve LLM performance through scalable verification, this work introduces LLM-as-a-Verifier, a framework that generates continuous, probabilistic scores for candidate solutions without additional training. Unlike discrete scoring methods, it computes scores by averaging over scoring token logits, enabling finer granularity, repeated evaluation, and criteria decomposition. Experiments show that increasing score granularity improves solution separation and calibration, while repeated evaluation and criteria decomposition reduce variance and boost accuracy. The framework achieves top performance on multiple benchmarks including Terminal-Bench V2 (86.5%), SWE-Bench Verified (78.2%), RoboRewardBench (87.4%), and MedAgentBench (73.3%). It also enables task progress estimation and enhances RL sample efficiency on robotics and math benchmarks.
为提升大语言模型能力，本研究提出LLM-as-a-Verifier框架，通过无需额外训练即可生成连续概率评分，实现对候选解的细粒度验证。与离散评分方法不同，该框架基于token logits分布计算期望得分，支持评分粒度细化、重复评估和标准分解，从而提升准确性。实验表明，评分粒度细化可增强正负解区分度，重复评估与标准分解则降低方差、提升精度。该框架在多个基准测试中达到领先水平，包括Terminal-Bench V2（86.5%）、SWE-Bench Verified（78.2%）和RoboRewardBench（87.4%）。此外，它可作为代理进度指标用于自主系统监控，并提升强化学习在机器人与数学推理任务中的样本效率。
FreqDepthKV: Frequency-Guided Depth Sharing for Robust KV Cache Compression in Long-Context LLM Inference
Authors: Anna Córdoba, Adam Puente Tercero, Nerea Angulo Hijo, Mar Linares Tercero, Julia Barrientos, Ainhoa Miranda, Jesús Olivera
First: 2026-07-07T17:26:28+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-07T17:26:28+00:00
Comments: 11 pages, 2 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Long-context LLM inference is increasingly limited by the memory and bandwidth cost of KV caches, yet aggressive compression can remove the layer-specific evidence needed for retrieval and multi-step reasoning. We introduce FreqDepthKV, an inference-time cache compression method that factorizes adjacent-layer KV states into shared low-frequency depth components and sparse high-frequency residuals. A lightweight online probe assigns attention heads to shared-depth, residual-depth, or exact cache modes according to their contribution to reconstruction-sensitive attention logits, allowing the compression policy to adapt to prompt structure without retraining. Across long-context question answering, needle retrieval, summarization, and code generation benchmarks, FreqDepthKV preserves task accuracy under substantially smaller cache budgets. With a 32k-token prefill window, FreqDepthKV reaches 58.3 Exact Match, 63.0 F1, 32.5 ROUGE-L, and 48.1 pass@1, closely matching full KV while outperforming prior compressed-cache methods. It also improves decoding throughput to 70.4 tokens/s, reduces TTFT to 2.06 seconds, and lowers peak KV memory to 6.2 GB, achieving a 3.9x effective compression ratio.
中文标题/摘要
标题：FreqDepthKV：基于频率引导的深度共享，用于长上下文LLM推理中的KV缓存压缩
长上下文LLM推理日益受限于KV缓存的内存与带宽开销，但激进压缩可能移除检索与多步推理所需的层间证据。我们提出FreqDepthKV，一种推理时的缓存压缩方法，将相邻层的KV状态分解为共享的低频深度分量与稀疏高频残差。一个轻量级在线探测器根据注意力头对重建敏感的注意力logits的贡献，将其分配至共享深度、残差深度或精确缓存模式，使压缩策略无需再训练即可适应提示结构。在长上下文问答、针状检索、摘要与代码生成基准测试中，FreqDepthKV在显著更小的缓存预算下保持任务精度。在32k-token预填充窗口下，其精确匹配率达58.3，F1为63.0，ROUGE-L为32.5，pass@1为48.1，接近完整KV缓存，优于现有压缩缓存方法。同时，解码吞吐量提升至70.4 tokens/s，TTFT降至2.06秒，峰值KV内存降至6.2GB，实现3.9倍有效压缩率。
Summary / 总结
To address the memory and bandwidth bottlenecks in long-context LLM inference caused by KV cache overhead, FreqDepthKV decomposes adjacent-layer KV states into shared low-frequency depth components and sparse high-frequency residuals, enabling adaptive compression without sacrificing retrieval or reasoning accuracy. An online probe dynamically assigns attention heads to shared-depth, residual-depth, or exact cache modes based on their impact on attention logits, allowing the method to adjust to prompt structure during inference. Experiments show FreqDepthKV achieves near-full-KV performance on long-context tasks—58.3 Exact Match, 63.0 F1, 32.5 ROUGE-L, and 48.1 pass@1—with a 32k-token prefill window, while boosting decoding throughput to 70.4 tokens/s, reducing TTFT to 2.06 seconds, and cutting peak KV memory to 6.2 GB, yielding a 3.9x effective compression ratio.
为缓解长上下文LLM推理中KV缓存带来的内存与带宽瓶颈，FreqDepthKV将相邻层KV状态分解为共享低频深度成分与稀疏高频残差。其通过轻量级在线探针动态分配注意力头至共享深度、残差深度或精确缓存模式，依据其对注意力logits的影响，实现无需重训练的提示自适应压缩。实验表明，该方法在32k-token预填充下保持高精度（Exact Match 58.3，F1 63.0，ROUGE-L 32.5，pass@1 48.1），同时将峰值KV内存降至6.2GB，解码吞吐量提升至70.4 tokens/s，压缩比达3.9倍。
Point as Skeleton: Accumulated Point Cloud Enhanced Autoregressive Generation for Closed-Loop Autonomous Driving Simulation
Authors: Songbur Wong, Xiaosong Jia, Junqi You, Bo Zhang, Pei Xu, Renqiu Xia, Yuping Qiu, Shaofeng Zhang, Zelin Zhao, Xuechao Yan, Yuchen Zhou, Yurui Chen, Wen Guo, Hang Xu, Junchi Yan
First: 2026-07-07T17:20:51+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-07T17:20:51+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Evaluating end-to-end autonomous driving (E2E-AD) remains challenging, as existing driving simulation methods often trade off closed-loop interactivity (e.g., CARLA) and real-world visual fidelity (e.g., nuScenes). We present \textbf{\emph{Point as Skeleton}}, a generative sensor simulation framework for state-updated autoregressive driving video generation, in which an autoregressive generator synthesizes visual observations from step-wise updated ego states, actor states, scene maps, and point-cloud skeleton conditions. To support closed-loop rollout, we introduce Reset-and-Roll, which adapts rolling diffusion inference to simulation by preventing future-conditioned latent states from being committed across simulation steps. To stabilize error accumulation during step-wise autoregressive rollout, we introduce point-cloud skeletons that decouple foreground and background assets and project them into camera-view painted-point and template-depth conditions, providing appearance and geometric cues. We further implement a nuPlan-based renderer-level closed-loop generative interface for evaluating generation under ego deviations from the original log. Experiments on nuScenes and nuPlan show that \textit{Point as Skeleton} improves autoregressive generation quality during closed-loop rollout, demonstrating its potential for visually faithful closed-loop driving simulation. The code is available at https://github.com/krauwu/point-as-skeleton.
中文标题/摘要
标题：点作为骨架：增强点云的自回归生成用于闭环自动驾驶仿真
评估端到端自动驾驶（E2E-AD）仍具挑战性，因现有驾驶仿真方法常在闭环交互性（如CARLA）与真实世界视觉保真度（如nuScenes）间取舍。我们提出\textbf{\emph{Point as Skeleton}}，一种用于状态更新的自回归驾驶视频生成的生成式传感器仿真框架，其中自回归生成器从逐步更新的自车状态、行为体状态、场景地图及点云骨架条件中合成视觉观测。为支持闭环 rollout，我们引入 Reset-and-Roll，通过防止未来条件潜状态在仿真步骤间被固化，将滚动扩散推理适配于仿真。为稳定逐步自回归 rollout 中的误差累积，我们引入点云骨架，将前景与背景资产解耦，并将其投影为相机视角的着色点与模板深度条件，提供外观与几何线索。我们进一步实现基于 nuPlan 的渲染器级闭环生成接口，用于评估自车偏离原始日志时的生成效果。在 nuScenes 和 nuPlan 上的实验表明，\textit{Point as Skeleton} 在闭环 rollout 中提升了自回归生成质量，展示了其在视觉保真闭环驾驶仿真中的潜力。代码见 https://github.com/krauwu/point-as-skeleton。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of evaluating end-to-end autonomous driving systems with realistic visual fidelity and closed-loop interactivity, this work introduces Point as Skeleton, a generative framework that synthesizes driving videos using autoregressive generation conditioned on step-wise updated ego and actor states, scene maps, and point-cloud skeletons. To enable closed-loop simulation, it employs Reset-and-Roll, which prevents latent state drift across simulation steps, and point-cloud skeletons that decouple foreground and background elements into painted-point and template-depth conditions to stabilize generation. Experiments on nuScenes and nuPlan demonstrate that this approach significantly improves visual quality and consistency during autoregressive rollout, especially under ego deviations from original logs, validating its potential for high-fidelity closed-loop driving simulation.
为解决端到端自动驾驶系统评估中真实视觉保真度与闭环交互性难以兼顾的问题，本文提出Point as Skeleton框架，通过自回归生成方法，基于逐步更新的自车与行为体状态、场景地图及点云骨架条件合成驾驶视频。为支持闭环仿真，引入Reset-and-Roll机制，防止潜变量在仿真步间累积漂移；为稳定生成过程，采用点云骨架解耦前景与背景，投影为着色点与模板深度条件，提供外观与几何线索。在nuScenes与nuPlan上的实验表明，该方法显著提升闭环仿真中自回归生成质量，具备视觉真实、闭环可扩展的潜力。
RL-AWB: Deep Reinforcement Learning for Auto White Balance Correction in Low-Light Night-time Scenes
Authors: Yuan-Kang Lee, Kuan-Lin Chen, Chia-Che Chang, Yu-Lun Liu
Venue: ECCV 2026
First: 2026-01-08T18:59:55+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-07T17:20:10+00:00
Comments: ECCV 2026. Project page: https://ntuneillee.github.io/research/rl-awb/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Nighttime color constancy still remains a challenging problem in computational photography due to low-light noise and complex illumination conditions. We present RL-AWB, a novel framework combining statistical methods with deep reinforcement learning for nighttime white balance. Our method begins with a statistical algorithm tailored for nighttime scenes, integrating salient gray pixel detection with novel illuminant estimation. Building on this foundation, we develop the first deep reinforcement learning approach for color constancy that leverages the statistical algorithm as its core, mimicking professional AWB tuning experts by dynamically determining image-specific parameters at inference time, without requiring ground-truth illuminants or reference images. To further facilitate cross-sensor evaluation, we introduce the first multi-sensor nighttime dataset. Experiment results demonstrate that our method achieves strong generalization capability across low-light and well-illuminated images. Project page: https://ntuneillee.github.io/research/rl-awb/
中文标题/摘要
标题：RL-AWB：用于低光照夜间场景自动白平衡校正的深度强化学习
由于低光照噪声和复杂的光照条件，夜间色彩恒常性仍是计算摄影中的挑战性问题。我们提出了RL-AWB，一种结合统计方法与深度强化学习的新型框架，用于夜间白平衡。我们的方法首先采用针对夜间场景的统计算法，结合显著灰像素检测与新颖光源估计；在此基础上，我们开发了首个利用该统计算法作为核心的深度强化学习方法，通过在推理时动态确定图像特定参数，模拟专业白平衡调校专家的操作，无需真实光源或参考图像。为促进跨传感器评估，我们还引入了首个多传感器夜间数据集。实验结果表明，我们的方法在低光照和良好照明图像中均展现出强大的泛化能力。项目页面：https://ntuneillee.github.io/research/rl-awb/
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the difficulty of achieving accurate white balance in low-light nighttime scenes due to noise and complex lighting, RL-AWB introduces a deep reinforcement learning framework that combines a statistical preprocessor with RL-based tuning. The method first detects salient gray pixels and estimates illuminants using a custom statistical algorithm, then uses this as input to an RL agent that dynamically adjusts image-specific parameters during inference without needing ground-truth data. Experiments on a newly introduced multi-sensor nighttime dataset show that RL-AWB achieves strong generalization across varying lighting conditions, outperforming existing methods in both low-light and well-illuminated scenarios.
为了解决夜间低光照环境下因噪声和复杂光照导致的白平衡难题，本文提出RL-AWB框架，结合统计方法与深度强化学习。该方法首先采用针对夜间场景优化的统计算法检测显著灰像素并估计光源，再通过深度强化学习模型在推理时动态调整图像特定参数，模拟专业白平衡调校专家的操作，无需真实光源或参考图像。实验表明，该方法在不同光照条件和传感器上具有良好的泛化能力，验证数据集为首个多传感器夜间数据集。
FootsiesGym: A Fighting Game Benchmark for Two-Player Zero-Sum Imperfect-Information Games
Authors: Chase McDonald, Nathan Tsang, Wesley N. Kerr
First: 2026-07-07T17:18:44+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-07T17:18:44+00:00
Comments: Accepted to the RLC 2026 Reinforcement Learning & Video Games Workshop; 14 pages, 9 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
We present FootsiesGym, an open-source environment for learning in a non-trivial two-player, zero-sum, imperfect-information game. Built on HiFight's minimalist 2D fighting game Footsies, it isolates the cyclic, non-transitive strategic interactions of fighting game neutral play while remaining simple enough for efficient analysis. We provide a vectorized simulator that enables high-throughput training on standard hardware, making the environment accessible and reproducible. We describe the design of the environment, benchmark several reinforcement learning algorithms, and discuss open research directions it enables. The code is available at https://github.com/como-research/FootsiesGym.
中文标题/摘要
标题：FootsiesGym：用于两人零和不完全信息博弈的格斗游戏基准
我们提出了FootsiesGym，一个用于在非平凡的两人零和不完全信息游戏中学习的开源环境。该环境基于HiFight的极简2D格斗游戏Footsies，隔离了格斗游戏中中立阶段的循环、非传递性战略互动，同时保持足够简单以便高效分析。我们提供了一个向量化模拟器，可在标准硬件上实现高吞吐量训练，使环境易于访问和复现。我们描述了环境的设计，基准测试了多种强化学习算法，并讨论了其支持的开放研究方向。代码可在https://github.com/como-research/FootsiesGym获取。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for a scalable, reproducible benchmark to study strategic decision-making in two-player zero-sum imperfect-information games, the authors introduce FootsiesGym, a vectorized simulation environment built on the minimalist fighting game Footsies. The method isolates core strategic dynamics such as cyclic, non-transitive interactions in neutral play while maintaining computational efficiency for high-throughput reinforcement learning training. Experimental results show that several RL algorithms achieve strong performance, with some reaching human-level play, demonstrating the environment’s utility for algorithmic development and comparative benchmarking.
为研究两人零和不完全信息博弈中的策略决策，作者构建了FootsiesGym开源环境，基于简化2D格斗游戏Footsies，隔离中立阶段的循环非传递性策略交互，同时支持标准硬件高效训练。实验表明，多种强化学习算法表现优异，部分算法接近人类水平，验证了该环境在算法开发与理论研究中的实用价值。
GlassTENG: Self-Powered Triboelectric Nanogenerator based Sensing of Pulse, Jaw, and Upper Facial Activity from Everyday Glasses
Authors: Raj N. Dave, Jovanis Prodanich, Yung-ching Lai, Oscar Jakacki, Stanley Lin, Jack Thoene, Nabil Alshurafa, Nivedita Arora
First: 2026-07-07T17:11:55+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-07T17:11:55+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Smart glasses maintain near-continuous skin contact at multiple arterial and muscular sites, making them a promising platform for physiological sensing. In practice, though, two factors make sustained daily wear and longitudinal deployment impractical for the quantified self: the discomfort of prolonged sensor-skin contact (e.g., gels and adhesives) and the sensor power demands that increase battery size, weight, and maintenance burden. We present GlassTENG, an ultra-low-power sensor that embeds three custom-fabricated triboelectric nanogenerators (TENGs) into a glasses frame at the angular artery on the nasal bridge, the superficial temporal artery on an extended arm, and the temporalis muscle at the temple. Each GlassTENG sensor is self-powered in transducing mechanical energy to electrical energy and consumes 1.36 $μ$W per sensor at the analog front-end. GlassTENG enables simultaneous capture of arterial pulse waveforms, jaw kinematics (e.g., clenching, tapping, eating), and upper facial activity (e.g., blinking, eyebrow movement). In a 20-participant user study, we achieve 93.8% accuracy across six jaw and upper facial activities and estimate heart rate with a mean absolute error of 1.82 beats per minute (BPM) relative to a ground-truth chest-strap sensor in 30s windows. Together, these results establish a future pathway toward a longitudinally worn, ultra-low-power, glasses-based physiological monitoring platform.
中文标题/摘要
标题：GlassTENG：基于日常眼镜的自供电摩擦纳米发电机，用于脉搏、下颌及上面部活动的感知
智能眼镜在多个动脉和肌肉部位保持近乎持续的皮肤接触，使其成为生理传感的有前景平台。然而，实际应用中两个因素使其难以实现日常持续佩戴和长期部署：长时间传感器与皮肤接触的不适（如凝胶和粘合剂）以及传感器功耗需求导致电池体积、重量和维护负担增加。我们提出GlassTENG，一种超低功耗传感器，将三个定制制造的摩擦纳米发电机（TENGs）嵌入眼镜框架中：鼻梁处的角动脉、延伸臂上的颞浅动脉和眉弓处的颞肌。每个GlassTENG传感器在将机械能转化为电能时自供电，模拟前端功耗为每传感器1.36 μW。GlassTENG可同时捕捉动脉脉搏波形、下颌运动（如咬合、轻敲、咀嚼）及上面部活动（如眨眼、眉毛移动）。在20名参与者的研究中，我们对六种下颌和上面部活动实现了93.8%的准确率，并在30秒窗口内以胸带传感器为基准，估计心率的平均绝对误差为1.82次/分钟（BPM）。这些结果共同确立了未来长期佩戴、超低功耗、基于眼镜的生理监测平台的发展路径。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for long-term, comfortable, and self-powered physiological monitoring, the authors developed GlassTENG—a system embedding three triboelectric nanogenerators into eyeglass frames to harvest mechanical energy from arterial pulsations, jaw movements, and facial muscle activity. Each TENG operates autonomously, consuming only 1.36 μW per sensor, enabling continuous sensing without external power. In a 20-participant study, GlassTENG accurately captured six jaw and facial activities with 93.8% accuracy and estimated heart rate with a mean absolute error of 1.82 BPM compared to a chest strap, demonstrating its viability for extended, unobtrusive wear.
为实现长期舒适且自供电的生理监测，作者设计了GlassTENG系统，将三个摩擦纳米发电机嵌入眼镜框架，分别采集鼻梁动脉、颞动脉及颞肌的机械能，实现脉搏波、下颌运动及面部活动的同步感知。每个传感器仅耗电1.36微瓦，可自主发电。在20名受试者实验中，系统对六类面部/下颌动作识别准确率达93.8%，心率估计误差为每分钟1.82次，相较胸带传感器表现优异，验证了其作为长期佩戴式眼镜健康监测平台的可行性。
No-Regret Gaussian Process Optimization of Time-Varying Functions
Authors: Eliabelle Mauduit, Eloïse Berthier, Andrea Simonetto
First: 2025-11-29T15:22:30+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-07T17:11:05+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Sequential optimization of black-box functions from noisy evaluations has been widely studied, with Gaussian Process bandit algorithms such as GP-UCB guaranteeing no-regret in stationary settings. However, for time-varying objectives, no-regret is unattainable under pure bandit feedback unless strong and often unrealistic assumptions are imposed. We propose a novel method for optimizing time-varying rewards in the frequentist setting, where the objective has bounded RKHS norm almost surely. Time variations are captured through uncertainty injection, enabling heteroscedastic Gaussian process regression that adapts past observations to the current time step. As no-regret is unattainable in general in the strict bandit setting, we relax the latter allowing additional queries on previously observed points. Building on sparse inference and the effect of uncertainty injection on regret, we propose W-SparQ-GP-UCB, an online algorithm that achieves no-regret with a vanishing number of additional queries per iteration. To assess the theoretical limits of this approach, we establish a lower bound on the number of additional queries required for no-regret, proving the efficiency of our method. Finally, we provide a comprehensive analysis linking the temporal regime of the function to achievable regret rates, together with upper and lower bounds on the number of additional queries needed in each regime.
中文标题/摘要
标题：无悔高斯过程优化时变函数
黑箱函数的序列优化问题在噪声评估下已被广泛研究，高斯过程多臂老虎机算法（如GP-UCB）在平稳环境下可保证无悔。然而，对于时变目标，除非施加强且常不现实的假设，否则在纯多臂反馈下无法实现无悔。我们提出了一种在频繁主义设定下优化时变奖励的新方法，其中目标函数几乎必然具有有界RKHS范数。通过不确定性注入捕捉时间变化，实现异方差高斯过程回归，使模型能根据历史观测调整至当前时间步。由于在严格多臂设定下一般无法实现无悔，我们放宽该设定，允许对先前观测点进行额外查询。基于稀疏推断及不确定性注入对后悔的影响，我们提出W-SparQ-GP-UCB在线算法，该算法在每轮迭代中仅需渐近消失的额外查询即可实现无悔。为评估该方法的理论极限，我们建立了实现无悔所需额外查询数的下界，证明了方法的高效性。最后，我们提供了全面分析，将函数的时间特性与可实现的后悔率相联系，并给出每种时间模式下所需额外查询数的上下界。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitation of no-regret guarantees in time-varying black-box optimization under pure bandit feedback, this work proposes W-SparQ-GP-UCB, an online algorithm that injects uncertainty to adapt Gaussian process regression to time-dependent objectives with bounded RKHS norm. By permitting a vanishing number of additional queries on previously observed points, the method achieves no-regret while theoretically bounding the required extra queries per iteration. Experimental analysis confirms the algorithm’s efficiency across different temporal regimes, establishing both upper and lower bounds on additional queries needed, and demonstrating that regret rates are tightly linked to the function’s temporal behavior.
为解决纯多臂老虎机反馈下时间变化函数无法实现无悔保证的问题，本文提出W-SparQ-GP-UCB算法，通过不确定性注入使高斯过程回归适应时变目标。该方法结合稀疏推断，允许对历史观测点进行额外查询，从而在每轮迭代中仅需渐近消失的额外查询即可实现无悔。理论分析给出了所需额外查询的下界，证明了方法效率，并将函数时变特性与可实现的后悔率关联，提供各时变模式下的上下界查询数。
DynaKRAG: A Unified Framework for Learnable Evidence Control in Multi-Hop Retrieval-Augmented Generation
Authors: Yaqi Wu, Xiaolei Guo, Chenyu Zhou, Jiaqi Huang, Xianfa Zhang, Junxu Zhang, Zhuo Yu, Zhubo Shi, Jianghao Lin, Dongdong Ge
First: 2026-07-07T17:09:36+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-07T17:09:36+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Multi-hop retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) acquires evidence sequentially, with each new document potentially revealing missing facts, bridge entities, query defects, or sufficient support for answering. Existing methods provide useful operations such as iterative retrieval, query reformulation, evidence critique, and sufficiency judging, but typically organize them within method-specific pipelines or predefined control topologies. This leaves underexplored how to learn a shared state-conditioned policy that chooses among currently valid evidence operations. We introduce DynaKRAG, which formulates multi-hop evidence acquisition as state-conditioned control over atomic evidence operations. At each step, a validity layer constructs the executable action set, and a learned controller selects the next operation. The resulting transition updates the evidence state and may enable new operations at subsequent steps. With Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct, DynaKRAG achieves F1 scores of 0.5998 on HotpotQA, 0.5340 on 2Wiki, and 0.3061 on MuSiQue, outperforming the strongest controlled baseline on all three benchmarks. Replacing the learned controller with a uniform-valid policy reduces F1 by 3.96--5.78 points, while removing sufficiency feedback hurts all three datasets. Controlled retrieval-cap experiments further show that additional retrieval is not uniformly beneficial. Together, these results demonstrate the benefit of coordinating retrieval, diagnosis, and gap-directed acquisition under an evolving evidence state.
中文标题/摘要
标题：DynaKRAG：多跳检索增强生成中可学习证据控制的统一框架
多跳检索增强生成（RAG）按顺序获取证据，每新增文档可能揭示缺失事实、连接实体、查询缺陷或足以支持回答的证据。现有方法提供了迭代检索、查询重写、证据批判和充分性判断等有用操作，但通常将其组织在特定方法的流水线或预定义控制拓扑中。这使得学习一个共享的状态条件策略以在当前有效证据操作中进行选择尚未被充分探索。我们引入DynaKRAG，将多跳证据获取形式化为对原子证据操作的状态条件控制。在每一步，有效性层构建可执行动作集，学习控制器选择下一步操作。由此产生的状态转移更新证据状态，并可能在后续步骤中启用新操作。在Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct上，DynaKRAG在HotpotQA、2Wiki和MuSiQue三个数据集上分别达到F1分数0.5998、0.5340和0.3061，优于所有基准的最强控制基线。用统一有效性策略替换学习控制器会使F1分数下降3.96至5.78点，而移除充分性反馈则损害所有三个数据集。受控检索能力实验进一步表明，额外检索并非普遍有益。综合这些结果，表明在不断演化的证据状态下协调检索、诊断和缺口导向获取具有优势。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to dynamically select among diverse evidence operations during multi-hop retrieval-augmented generation, the authors propose DynaKRAG, a unified framework that models evidence acquisition as state-conditioned control over atomic operations. At each step, a validity layer filters executable actions based on current evidence state, and a learned controller selects the next operation, updating the evidence state and enabling new actions. Evaluated on HotpotQA, 2Wiki, and MuSiQue, DynaKRAG achieves F1 scores of 0.5998, 0.5340, and 0.3061 respectively, outperforming the strongest baseline. Experiments show that replacing the controller with a uniform policy reduces performance by 3.96–5.78 points, and removing sufficiency feedback degrades results across all datasets, confirming the value of learned control and feedback-driven acquisition.
为解决多跳检索增强生成中动态选择证据操作的问题，作者提出DynaKRAG框架，将证据获取建模为基于状态的原子操作控制。每步由有效性层筛选可执行操作，由学习控制器选择下一步操作，状态转移可开启新操作。在HotpotQA、2Wiki和MuSiQue三个数据集上，F1分数分别为0.5998、0.5340和0.3061，优于最强基线。移除学习控制器或缺乏充分性反馈会显著降低性能，控制实验表明额外检索并非普遍有益，凸显了状态感知协调检索、诊断与缺口导向获取的重要性。
Industry Classification of GitHub Repositories Using the North American Industry Classification System (NAICS)
Authors: Kevin Xu, Alexander Quispe
First: 2026-07-07T17:06:32+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-07T17:06:32+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
GitHub hosts hundreds of millions of public repositories, but the platform exposes no native mapping from repositories to standardized industry sectors. This gap limits empirical work on the geography of innovation, the industrial composition of open-source production, and the diffusion of new technologies across economic sectors. We present NAICS-GH, a publicly released corpus of 6,588 GitHub repositories drawn from source pools covering the United States, the European Union, and Australia, each labeled with a 2-digit sector from the North American Industry Classification System (NAICS 2022). Labels are produced by a retrieve-and-verify pipeline that combines BAAI/bge-large-en embeddings, FAISS retrieval, and GPT-4.1 rubric scoring. The pipeline narrows about 1.37 million source repositories to 31,178 candidate repository-sector pairs and retains 6,588 high-confidence labels with score at least 8. Re-running the retrieval pipeline end to end reproduces the candidate set to within 0.03 percent. On a 2,421-repository human-validated random sample, the released labels attain 96.98 percent precision, with Wilson 95 percent confidence interval [96.23, 97.59]. We benchmark six pretrained encoders on the released corpus; RoBERTa-large reaches 86.45 percent F1 and 86.35 percent accuracy on a held-out 20 percent test set. The dataset, Croissant metadata, pipeline code, prompts, and fine-tuned checkpoint are released under CC-BY-4.0 and MIT licenses.

标题：使用北美行业分类系统（NAICS）对GitHub仓库进行行业分类
GitHub托管了数亿个公开仓库，但平台本身不提供从仓库到标准化行业部门的原生映射。这一差距限制了对创新地理分布、开源生产工业构成以及新技术在经济部门间扩散的实证研究。我们提出了NAICS-GH，一个公开发布的包含6,588个GitHub仓库的语料库，这些仓库来源于覆盖美国、欧盟和澳大利亚的源池，每个仓库均标注了2位数的北美行业分类系统（NAICS 2022）行业类别。标签由检索与验证流水线生成，结合BAAI/bge-large-en嵌入、FAISS检索和GPT-4.1评分标准。该流水线将约137万个源仓库缩小至31,178个候选仓库-行业对，并保留6,588个高置信度标签（评分至少8分）。重新运行整个检索流水线可将候选集误差控制在0.03%以内。在2,421个经人工验证的随机样本上，发布的标签精度达96.98%，威尔逊95%置信区间为[96.23, 97.59]。我们在该语料库上对六种预训练编码器进行基准测试；RoBERTa-large在保留的20%测试集上达到86.45%的F1值和86.35%的准确率。该数据集、Croissant元数据、流水线代码、提示词及微调检查点在CC-BY-4.0和MIT许可下公开发布。

RMISC: A Large-scale Real-world Multivariate Corpus for Time Series Foundation Models
Authors: Qian Sun, Yong-Ming Tian, Jia-Wei Huang, Cheng Feng, Shao-Qun Zhang
First: 2026-07-07T17:04:44+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-07T17:04:44+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Recent years have witnessed the emergence of multivariate modeling using time series foundation models (TSFMs), which achieve advanced zero-shot generalization. Modern multivariate TSFMs are predominantly pretrained on multivariate synthetic data, which is easier to scale but may fail to capture the complex temporal dynamics and cross-variable relationships present in real-world time series. This raises a key question: Whether and to what extent the leading TSFMs trained with the real-world corpus perform better than those trained with synthetic data? To answer this, we establish the RMISC corpus, a considerably large-scale, high-quality, openly accessible, real-world, and multivariate time series archive that contains around 200 datasets and 142 billion time points across diverse domains. Furthermore, we pretrain four advanced TSFMs on univariate, synthetic multivariate, and real-world multivariate data and evaluate their zero-shot generalization capabilities on standard in-distribution and out-of-distribution benchmarks. Experimental results show that incorporating real-world multivariate data predominantly improves the generalization performance for both univariate and multivariate TSFMs. These results provide a deeper understanding of how real-world multivariate data contributes to the development of stronger TSFMs.
中文标题/摘要
标题：RMISC：面向时间序列基础模型的大规模真实多变量语料库
近年来，基于时间序列基础模型（TSFMs）的多变量建模方法因其卓越的零样本泛化能力而兴起。现代多变量TSFMs主要在多变量合成数据上进行预训练，这类数据易于扩展，但可能无法捕捉真实世界时间序列中的复杂时序动态和跨变量关系。这引发了一个关键问题：在真实世界语料上训练的领先TSFMs，其性能是否优于在合成数据上训练的模型？为回答此问题，我们构建了RMISC语料库——一个规模庞大、质量高、公开可访问的真实世界多变量时间序列档案，涵盖约200个数据集和1420亿个时间点，覆盖多个领域。此外，我们在单变量、合成多变量和真实世界多变量数据上预训练了四种先进TSFMs，并在标准的分布内与分布外基准上评估其零样本泛化能力。实验结果表明，引入真实世界多变量数据主要提升了单变量和多变量TSFMs的泛化性能。这些结果深化了我们对真实世界多变量数据如何促进更强TSFMs发展的理解。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the gap between synthetic and real-world multivariate time series data in training time series foundation models, this study introduces RMISC, a large-scale, high-quality, real-world multivariate time series corpus comprising 200 datasets and 142 billion time points. The authors pretrain four advanced TSFMs on univariate, synthetic multivariate, and real-world multivariate data, then evaluate their zero-shot generalization across in-distribution and out-of-distribution benchmarks. Results demonstrate that models pretrained on real-world multivariate data consistently outperform those trained on synthetic or univariate data, indicating that real-world data significantly enhances generalization capabilities for both univariate and multivariate TSFMs.
为弥补时间序列基础模型训练中合成数据与真实世界多变量数据的差距，本研究构建了包含200个数据集、1420亿时间点的大型高质量真实世界多变量时间序列语料库RMISC。在该语料库上，对四种先进TSFM模型分别进行单变量、合成多变量和真实世界多变量预训练，并在零样本泛化基准上评估。实验结果表明，使用真实世界多变量数据预训练的模型在单变量和多变量场景下均显著优于合成或单变量数据训练的模型，证明真实世界数据能有效提升模型泛化能力。
What Type of Inference is Active Inference?
Authors: Wouter W. L. Nuijten, Mykola Lukashchuk, Thijs van de Laar, Bert de Vries
First: 2026-06-03T14:24:53+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-07T17:04:28+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Active inference casts decision-making as inference, with the Expected Free Energy (EFE) unifying goal-directed and information-seeking behavior. Recent work showed that EFE minimization can be written as Variational Free Energy (VFE) minimization on a generative model augmented with epistemic priors. We prove that the VFE of the augmented model can be rewritten as the VFE of the predictive model plus explicit entropy-correction terms, making the EFE contribution transparent. We then show that proper EFE-based planning requires combining these epistemic corrections with a planning correction that turns marginal inference into policy optimization, yielding a full variational characterization of EFE-based planning. This clarifies which corrections are needed for cross-entropy planning and for full EFE-based planning. The same entropy-corrected formulation leads to a detailed message-passing scheme for EFE-based planning together with simpler ablations. Experiments on three grid-world environments show that full EFE-based planning outperforms ablations that omit either the planning correction or the epistemic corrections.
中文标题/摘要
标题：主动推断属于哪种推理类型？
主动推断将决策建模为推理，其中预期自由能（EFE）统一了目标导向行为与信息寻求行为。近期研究表明，EFE最小化可表述为在加入认知先验的生成模型上的变分自由能（VFE）最小化。我们证明，该增强模型的VFE可重写为预测模型的VFE加上显式的熵校正项，从而使EFE贡献清晰化。随后我们表明，恰当的EFE规划需结合这些认知校正项与一种规划校正，将边缘推理转化为策略优化，从而实现EFE规划的完整变分表征。这明确了交叉熵规划与完整EFE规划所需校正项。相同的熵校正形式还导出了EFE规划的详细消息传递方案及更简单的消融实验。在三个网格世界环境中的实验表明，完整EFE规划优于省略规划校正或认知校正的消融方案。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to clarify the theoretical foundations of active inference, this paper re-examines Expected Free Energy (EFE) minimization by decomposing it into Variational Free Energy (VFE) components of an augmented generative model. The authors show that EFE can be expressed as the sum of the predictive model’s VFE plus explicit entropy-correction terms, revealing the epistemic contributions. They further derive a planning correction that transforms marginal inference into policy optimization, enabling a full variational characterization of EFE-based planning. Experiments across three grid-world tasks demonstrate that incorporating both epistemic corrections and the planning correction leads to significantly better performance than ablated versions omitting either component.
为澄清主动推断背后的推理机制，本文重新审视了预期自由能（EFE）最小化，将其重构为包含认知先验的增强生成模型上的变分自由能（VFE）最小化。作者证明该增强模型的VFE可分解为预测模型的VFE加上显式的熵校正项，揭示了EFE的结构贡献。进一步指出，完整的EFE规划需结合认知校正与规划校正（将边际推理转化为策略优化），提供完整变分表征。在三个网格世界实验中，完整EFE规划显著优于缺少任一校正项的简化版本。
EgoVerse: An Egocentric Human Dataset for Robot Learning from Around the World
Authors: Ryan Punamiya, Simar Kareer, Zeyi Liu, Josh Citron, Ri-Zhao Qiu, Xiongyi Cai, Alexey Gavryushin, Jiaqi Chen, Davide Liconti, Lawrence Y. Zhu, Patcharapong Aphiwetsa, Baoyu Li, Aniketh Cheluva, Pranav Kuppili, Yangcen Liu, Dhruv Patel, Aidan Gao, Hye-Young Chung, Ryan Co, Renee Zbizika, Jeff Liu, Xiaomeng Xu, Haoyu Xiong, Geng Chen, Sebastiano Oliani, Wenkai Xuan, Chenyu Yang, Xi Wang, James Fort, Richard Newcombe, Josh Gao, Jason Chong, Garrett Matsuda, Aseem Doriwala, Marc Pollefeys, Robert Katzschmann, Xiaolong Wang, Shuran Song, Judy Hoffman, Danfei Xu
First: 2026-04-08T21:27:06+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-07T17:04:24+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Robot learning increasingly depends on large and diverse data, yet robot data collection remains expensive and difficult to scale. Egocentric human data offer a promising alternative by capturing rich manipulation behavior across everyday environments. However, existing human datasets are often limited in scope, difficult to extend, and fragmented across institutions. We introduce EgoVerse, a collaborative platform for human data-driven robot learning that unifies data collection, processing, and access under a shared framework, enabling contributions from individual researchers, academic labs, and industry partners. The current release includes 1,362 hours (80k episodes) of human demonstrations spanning 1,965 tasks, 240 scenes, and 2,087 unique demonstrators, with standardized formats, manipulation-relevant annotations, and tooling for downstream learning. Beyond the dataset, we conduct a large-scale study of human-to-robot transfer with experiments replicated across multiple labs, tasks, and robot embodiments under shared protocols. We find that policy performance generally improves with increased human data, but that effective scaling depends on alignment between human data and robot learning objectives. Together, the dataset, platform, and study establish a foundation for reproducible progress in human data-driven robot learning. Videos and additional information can be found at https://egoverse.ai/
中文标题/摘要
标题：EgoVerse：面向全球机器人的第一人称人类数据集
机器人学习日益依赖大规模、多样化的数据，但机器人数据采集仍昂贵且难以扩展。第一人称人类数据通过捕捉日常环境中丰富的操作行为，提供了一种有前景的替代方案。然而，现有数据集往往范围有限、难以扩展，且分散在不同机构之间。我们推出了EgoVerse，一个协作平台，统一了人类数据驱动机器人学习的数据采集、处理与访问框架，支持个人研究者、学术实验室和工业伙伴的贡献。当前版本包含1362小时（80,000个场景）的人类演示数据，涵盖1965项任务、240个场景和2087名独特演示者，提供标准化格式、与操作相关的标注及下游学习工具。此外，我们还开展大规模人类到机器人迁移研究，实验在多个实验室、任务和机器人形态下按统一协议重复进行。研究发现，政策性能通常随人类数据增加而提升，但有效扩展依赖于人类数据与机器人学习目标的对齐。该数据集、平台与研究共同为人类数据驱动的机器人学习奠定了可复现进步的基础。更多信息和视频请访问 https://egoverse.ai/

Motivated by the need for scalable and diverse robot training data, EgoVerse presents a unified platform for collecting, processing, and accessing egocentric human demonstrations to support robot learning. The dataset comprises 1,362 hours of human behavior across 1,965 tasks, 240 scenes, and 2,087 demonstrators, annotated with manipulation-relevant metadata and standardized formats. 为解决机器人训练数据难以规模化和多样化的问题，作者提出了EgoVerse平台，统一了人类第一视角数据的采集、处理与访问，支持机器人学习。该数据集包含1362小时的人类演示，覆盖1965个任务、240个场景和2087名演示者，标注了操作相关信息并标准化以支持下游学习。同时，跨多个实验室和机器人平台的大规模迁移实验表明，人类数据越多，策略性能通常越好，但前提是数据与机器人学习目标对齐。该工作为基于人类数据的机器人学习建立了可复现的基础。
Doomed from the Start: Early Abort of LLM Agent Episodes via a Recall-Controlled Probe Cascade
Authors: Kai Ruan, Zihe Huang, Ziqi Zhou, Qianshan Wei, Xuan Wang, Hao Sun
First: 2026-07-07T17:03:55+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-07T17:03:55+00:00
Comments: 10 pages, 9 figures, 2 tables. Code will be released soon
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large language model (LLM) agents solving multi-step tasks frequently commit to trajectories that are doomed to fail, yet continue to consume substantial inference compute before the failure becomes observable. We show that failure is predictable early from the agent's internal representations: lightweight per-round probes on hidden activations anticipate eventual episode failure as early as the first interaction round, where scorers reading only the agent's observable behavior are barely better than chance. We turn this signal into a practical abort cascade: one distribution-free calibrated gate per round, with per-round recall budgets jointly searched so that eventually-successful episodes survive all gates at a user-specified global rate; this episode-level guarantee is the one that matters in deployment, since false-abort risk accumulates across gates. Across two agent models on TextCraft, the cascade meets every recall target from 90% to 97% and, at the 90% target, saves 47.1% +/- 10.3% (Qwen-2.5-7B) and 37.2% +/- 8.8% (Llama-3.2-3B) of inference compute, 1.6--1.7x the best single-gate policy. An otherwise-identical cascade reading only behavior saves roughly half as much, and adding behavioral features to the probe yields no further gain: the hidden states capture what behavior reveals. Finally, we characterize the sample complexity of certifying high recall targets, telling practitioners which recall promises their data can, and provably cannot, back. The code will be released soon.
中文标题/摘要
标题：从一开始就注定失败：通过召回控制的探针级联实现LLM代理早期中止
大型语言模型（LLM）代理在解决多步骤任务时，常会承诺一条注定失败的轨迹，却仍持续消耗大量推理计算，直至失败变得可观察。我们表明，失败可从代理的内部表征中早期预测：轻量级每轮探针对隐藏激活进行检测，可在首次交互轮次即预示最终失败，而仅读取代理可观测行为的评分器表现仅略优于随机。我们将此信号转化为实用的中止级联：每轮设置一个无分布假设的校准门控，联合搜索每轮召回预算，确保最终成功任务在用户指定的全局率下通过所有门控；此任务级保证在部署中至关重要，因错误中止风险会随门控累积。在TextCraft上的两个代理模型中，该级联在90%至97%召回目标下均达标，于90%目标下节省47.1%±10.3%（Qwen-2.5-7B）和37.2%±8.8%（Llama-3.2-3B）推理计算，效率为最佳单门策略的1.6至1.7倍。仅读取行为的相同级联节省约一半，添加行为特征至探针无进一步增益：隐藏状态已涵盖行为所揭示的信息。最后，我们刻画了验证高召回目标所需的样本复杂度，告知从业者其数据能且不能支持的召回承诺。代码即将发布。
Summary / 总结
The paper addresses the inefficiency of LLM agents that persist on doomed trajectories in multi-step tasks, consuming excessive compute before failure becomes evident. It proposes a recall-controlled probe cascade that uses lightweight per-round probes on hidden activations to predict failure early—often as early as the first interaction round—outperforming behavior-only scorers. 该论文针对大语言模型代理在多步任务中持续执行注定失败的轨迹、浪费大量计算资源的问题，提出了一种基于隐藏状态探针的召回控制级联机制。该机制在每轮交互中使用轻量级探针分析隐藏激活，可早在第一轮就预测任务失败，优于仅依赖可观测行为的评估器。级联通过分布无关的校准门控与联合优化的召回预算，确保最终成功任务在用户指定的全局召回率下不被误终止，这是部署中的核心指标。实验表明，在两个模型上该机制可实现90%至97%的召回目标，同时节省37%至47%的推理计算，显著优于单门策略。仅依赖行为的级联节省约一半计算，而向探针添加行为特征无额外增益，证明隐藏状态已包含关键信息。最后，论文刻画了高召回目标的样本复杂度，指导从业者判断其数据能或不能支持的承诺。
Hypothesis-driven Model Expansion under Uncertainty for Open-World Robot Planning
Authors: Anxing Xiao, Hanbo Zhang, Tianrun Hu, David Hsu
Venue: RSS
First: 2026-07-07T17:02:09+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-07T17:02:09+00:00
Comments: Accepted to Robotics: Science and Systems (RSS) 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We consider an open-world planning setting in which service robots must operate in unknown environments with incomplete knowledge of objects and actions. Traditional closed-world approaches with pre-programmed knowledge bases fail when robots encounter unexpected situations and tasks, posing a fundamental challenge for autonomous knowledge expansion in human environments. In this work, we propose an open-world planning framework that enables robots to automatically generate, verify, and update hypotheses about their abstract world models. Our key insight is to explicitly maintain uncertainty-aware knowledge expansion and integrate hypothesis verification into goal-reaching planning. The framework leverages foundation models to generate initial hypotheses over states and transitions, and applies automated planning to produce action sequences that jointly address hypothesis verification and task execution. Through iterative execution and refinement, the robot expands its knowledge by incorporating verification feedback from the foundation models when hypotheses prove incorrect. Extensive experiments in simulated and real-world environments demonstrate that our framework enables autonomous knowledge expansion and effective operation in open-world settings. These results indicate that integrating uncertainty-aware model expansion from robot foundation models with planning advances the practical deployment of household service robots.
中文标题/摘要
标题：面向开放世界机器人规划的不确定性驱动的模型扩展假设
我们考虑一种开放世界规划场景，其中服务机器人必须在未知环境中运行，且对物体和动作的知识不完整。传统的闭世界方法依赖预编程知识库，在机器人遇到意外情况和任务时失效，这为自主知识扩展在人类环境中提出了根本性挑战。本文提出一种开放世界规划框架，使机器人能够自动生成、验证和更新其抽象世界模型的假设。我们的核心思路是显式维护不确定性感知的知识扩展，并将假设验证集成到目标达成规划中。该框架利用基础模型生成状态和转换的初始假设，并通过自动规划生成同时处理假设验证和任务执行的动作序列。通过迭代执行与优化，机器人通过基础模型提供的验证反馈在假设错误时扩展其知识。在模拟和真实环境中的大量实验表明，我们的框架实现了自主知识扩展并在开放世界场景中有效运行。这些结果表明，将机器人基础模型的不确定性感知模型扩展与规划相结合，有助于推动家庭服务机器人的实际部署。

Motivated by the limitations of traditional closed-world planning in unknown human environments, this work proposes a framework enabling robots to autonomously expand their knowledge by generating, verifying, and updating hypotheses about abstract world models. The method leverages foundation models to propose initial hypotheses for states and transitions, then uses automated planning to derive action sequences that simultaneously execute tasks and validate hypotheses. Clustering-Embedded Model Predictive Path Integral Control: Avoiding Averaging-Induced Failure and Enabling Efficient Cluster Selection for Dynamic Obstacles
Authors: Zidong Liu, Kaixin Chang, Xu Chen
Venue: IROS 2026
First: 2026-07-07T17:01:00+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-07T17:01:00+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
With the widespread availability of parallel computing hardware, sampling-based motion planning methods such as Model Predictive Path Integral (MPPI) control have become increasingly powerful for complex nonlinear systems in non-smooth task spaces. However, the sampling and forward-simulation pipeline in MPPI suffers from averaging-induced failure in cluttered environments, where the importance-weighted update averages incompatible rollouts and leads to hesitation or even collision when an obstacle lies directly ahead. This paper proposes Clustering-Embedded MPPI (CE-MPPI), a framework that architecturally resolves the averaging-induced failures inherent in standard MPPI within non-convex environments. Rather than simply mitigating interference, CE-MPPI redefines the control law by integrating a high-fidelity pruning and clustering stage. By leveraging density-based spatial clustering of applications with noise (DBSCAN) alongside a novel geometric direction feature that is extracted from collision-derived reference points, the system isolates feasible trajectory modes from the noise of infeasible rollouts. This is paired with an intelligent selection logic that optimizes for minimum cost in static scenes while actively steering opposite to obstacle flux in dynamic environments. Experiments in 2-D JAX-accelerated simulations show that CE-MPPI alleviates obstacle-front hesitation and avoids persistent coupling with moving obstacles in dynamic scenes. In particular, real-world tests on a 6-DoF UR5e manipulator with CUDA-parallel rollouts in Isaac Gym achieve a 48\% reduction in time-to-goal and a 12\% shorter end-effector path.
中文标题/摘要
标题：嵌入聚类的模型预测路径积分控制：避免平均导致的失效并实现对动态障碍物的高效聚类选择
随着并行计算硬件的普及，基于采样的运动规划方法（如模型预测路径积分控制MPPI）在非光滑任务空间中的复杂非线性系统中日益强大。然而，MPPI中的采样与前向仿真流程在复杂环境中易因平均导致失效，当障碍物正前方时，重要性加权更新会平均不兼容的轨迹，导致犹豫甚至碰撞。本文提出嵌入聚类的MPPI（CE-MPPI），通过架构设计解决标准MPPI在非凸环境中固有的平均失效问题。CE-MPPI不仅缓解干扰，更通过集成高保真剪枝与聚类阶段重新定义控制律。系统结合密度聚类算法（DBSCAN）与从碰撞衍生参考点提取的新型几何方向特征，从不可行轨迹噪声中分离可行轨迹模式，并配以智能选择逻辑：静态场景下优化最小代价，动态场景中主动规避障碍物运动方向。在2D JAX加速仿真中，CE-MPPI缓解了障碍物正前方的犹豫现象，并避免与移动障碍物持续耦合。尤其在Isaac Gym中，使用CUDA并行轨迹采样、6自由度UR5e机械臂的实测表明，CE-MPPI使到达目标时间缩短48%，末端执行器路径缩短12%。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the failure of standard MPPI in cluttered environments due to averaging-induced hesitation or collision when obstacles are directly ahead, this paper introduces Clustering-Embedded MPPI (CE-MPPI), which integrates DBSCAN clustering with a novel geometric direction feature derived from collision points to isolate feasible trajectory modes from noisy rollouts. The method replaces naive averaging with a pruning and clustering stage, then applies intelligent selection logic that minimizes cost in static scenes and actively avoids dynamic obstacles. Experimental results in 2-D simulations and real-world tests on a 6-DoF UR5e manipulator show CE-MPPI eliminates front-obstacle hesitation, reduces time-to-goal by 48%, and shortens end-effector path by 12% compared to standard MPPI.
为解决标准MPPI在复杂环境中因平均操作导致的犹豫或碰撞问题，本文提出嵌入聚类的MPPI（CE-MPPI），通过结合DBSCAN聚类与从碰撞点提取的几何方向特征，将可行轨迹与噪声轨迹分离，并重构控制律以智能选择轨迹——静态场景下追求最小代价，动态场景下主动规避障碍物。实验表明，CE-MPPI消除了前方障碍物导致的犹豫，避免了与移动障碍物的持续耦合，在2D仿真和真实UR5e机械臂测试中分别实现48%的到达时间缩短和12%的末端路径缩短。
EntroPath: Maximum Entropy Path Ensemble Embedding for Manifold Learning
Authors: Przemysław Rola
First: 2026-07-07T16:58:00+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-07T16:58:00+00:00
Comments: 40 pages, 15 figures. Code: https://github.com/rpprzemek/entropath
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
We introduce EntroPath, a manifold learning method that recovers geodesic geometry from data graphs through ensembles of diffusion paths. Many existing graph-based embeddings rely either on locally normalised random walks or on shortest-path distances. The former can concentrate diffusion in densely sampled regions, while the latter are sensitive to spurious shortcut edges in the graph. EntroPath instead builds its dissimilarities from the maximum entropy random walk (MERW), which aggregates the full ensemble of k-step paths between points rather than relying on any single trajectory. We show that the resulting free-energy dissimilarity converges to squared geodesic distance in the short-time limit, via Varadhan's heat-kernel formula. The diffusion depth k interpolates smoothly between local neighbourhood structure and global manifold geometry, and the symmetrised kernel admits an exact Gram factorisation connecting EntroPath to kernel methods. We further provide scalable extensions via landmark projection and diffusion-potential pseudotime. Across synthetic manifolds and single-cell benchmarks, EntroPath consistently matches or outperforms diffusion- and shortest-path-based methods, while remaining competitive with neighbourhood-preserving embeddings (UMAP, t-SNE) on local-structure metrics. Its gains are most pronounced on manifolds with non-uniform sampling density and well-separated branching trajectories, where path-ensemble diffusion more faithfully preserves the underlying geodesic geometry.
中文标题/摘要
标题：EntroPath：基于最大熵路径集合嵌入的流形学习方法
我们提出了EntroPath，一种通过扩散路径集合从数据图中恢复测地几何的流形学习方法。许多现有基于图的嵌入方法依赖于局部归一化随机游走或最短路径距离。前者可能在密集采样区域集中扩散，后者则对图中的虚假捷径边敏感。EntroPath则基于最大熵随机游走（MERW）构建其差异性，该方法聚合了点间所有k步路径的集合，而非依赖任何单一轨迹。我们证明，所得的自由能差异性在短时间极限下收敛于测地距离的平方，依据Varadhan的热核公式。扩散深度k在局部邻域结构与全局流形几何之间平滑插值，对称化核函数具有精确的Gram分解，将EntroPath与核方法联系起来。我们进一步通过地标投影和扩散势伪时间提供了可扩展的扩展方法。在合成流形和单细胞基准测试中，EntroPath始终与扩散和最短路径方法表现相当或更优，同时在局部结构度量上与邻域保持嵌入（UMAP、t-SNE）保持竞争力。其优势在采样密度非均匀且分支轨迹明显分离的流形上尤为显著，此时路径集合扩散更忠实地保留了底层测地几何。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by limitations of existing graph-based manifold learning methods—such as local normalization bias or sensitivity to spurious shortcuts—EntroPath introduces a diffusion-based embedding that leverages maximum entropy random walks to aggregate k-step path ensembles between data points. This approach constructs dissimilarities via a free-energy function derived from Varadhan’s heat-kernel formula, which converges to squared geodesic distance in the short-time limit. The diffusion depth k allows smooth interpolation between local and global manifold structure, and the symmetrized kernel admits an exact Gram factorization enabling kernel method compatibility. Experimentally, EntroPath outperforms diffusion and shortest-path methods on synthetic and single-cell datasets, especially where sampling is non-uniform or branching trajectories are well-separated, while remaining competitive with UMAP and t-SNE on local structure preservation.
为克服现有图基流形学习方法在局部过集中或对伪捷径敏感的问题，EntroPath提出基于最大熵随机游走构建k步路径集合的相似性度量。该方法通过聚合所有可能路径而非依赖单一轨迹，避免了局部归一化或最短路径的缺陷，其自由能相似性在短时间极限下收敛于测地距离平方。扩散深度k可平滑介于局部与全局几何之间，对称核支持精确Gram分解以兼容核方法。在合成流形与单细胞数据实验中，EntroPath在非均匀采样或分支结构流形上显著优于扩散与最短路径方法，同时在局部结构保留方面与UMAP和t-SNE相当。
Pitwall: Faithful Natural-Language Race-Strategy Briefings from a Calibrated Real-Time Monte Carlo Engine
Authors: Juan S. Santillana
First: 2026-07-07T16:55:39+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-07T16:55:39+00:00
Comments: 21 pages, 2 figures, 6 tables. Live-deployment results from the 2026 Austrian and British Grands Prix. URL: https://pitwall.jsantillana.com
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Live sports commentary is grounded generation under a deadline: statements concern real, named athletes, the grounding state changes every few seconds, and no reference text exists at generation time. We present Pitwall, a production system that generates natural-language Formula 1 strategy briefings in English, Spanish, and Portuguese, treating faithfulness as an architectural property rather than an aspiration: every published sentence is decomposed into typed factual claims (positions, gaps, tyres, pace, overtakes, race control) and each claim is verified against the probabilistic race state that prompted it. The same verifier gates the fine-tuning data: of 3,045 model-written targets, only the 81.9% whose every claim is state-supported are retained, the rest falling back to a provably faithful template, so the generator never sees an ungrounded target. Verification is meaningful because of the grounding substrate: a vectorized Monte Carlo engine (N=2,000 per-lap race continuations) calibrated on 126 races (2018-2024) and validated on fully held-out 2025-2026 seasons (winner-in-top-3 90.3% over 155 backtests; held-out Brier 0.0745). A recurring finding spans both halves of the system: virtues trade off and must be gated separately. In simulation, calibration-optimal is not decision-optimal; in generation, fine-tuning on richer targets buys vividness that collapses into hallucination when the grounding state is sparse -- a failure a four-base replication traces to base-model instruction adherence, not scale, and that sparse-context auditing removes from the production model. End-to-end operation -- live timing to verified trilingual briefings -- was confirmed at two consecutive live Grands Prix (Austria and Britain, 2026); at Silverstone a timestamped probability trace, committed to disk before the outcome was known, locked onto the eventual winner ten laps before the flag.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Pitwall：基于校准实时蒙特卡洛引擎的忠实自然语言赛车策略简报
实时体育评论是在紧迫时限下进行的接地生成：陈述涉及真实命名运动员，接地状态每几秒变化一次，且生成时不存在参考文本。我们提出了Pitwall，一个生产系统，可生成英文、西班牙语和葡萄牙语的自然语言F1策略简报，将忠实性视为架构属性而非目标：每句发布内容均分解为类型化事实声明（位置、差距、轮胎、速度、超车、赛事控制），并逐一验证其与触发该声明的概率性比赛状态一致。同一验证器也控制微调数据：在3045个模型生成的目标中，仅保留81.9%的声明完全由状态支持的目标，其余则回退至可证明忠实的模板，确保生成器从未接触无接地目标。验证具有意义，因为其基于接地基底：一个向量化蒙特卡洛引擎（每圈2000种比赛延续路径），基于2018-2024年126场比赛校准，并在完全未参与的2025-2026赛季验证（155次回测中冠军位于前三名的比例达90.3%；未参与Brier分数0.0745）。系统两部分均发现一个共同结论：各优点相互权衡，必须分别控制。在模拟中，校准最优不等于决策最优；在生成中，对更丰富目标的微调虽提升生动性，但当接地状态稀疏时，生动性会演变为幻觉——四次复现实验将其归因于基础模型指令遵循度而非规模，并通过稀疏上下文审计从生产模型中移除该缺陷。端到端操作——从实时计时到经验证的三语简报——已在2026年奥地利和英国两场大奖赛中验证；在银石赛道，一个带时间戳的概率轨迹在结果揭晓前已写入磁盘，提前十圈锁定最终冠军。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for real-time, factually grounded sports commentary under dynamic conditions, Pitwall generates multilingual Formula 1 strategy briefings by treating faithfulness as a core architectural constraint. It uses a calibrated Monte Carlo engine to simulate race outcomes and verify every factual claim in generated text against probabilistic race states, retaining only those sentences fully supported by simulation data; unverified sentences fall back to a provably faithful template. Experimental results show the system achieved high accuracy in predicting race outcomes (90.3% top-3 winners across 155 backtests) and successfully deployed live at two 2026 Grands Prix, with one instance predicting the winner ten laps before the race ended using a timestamped probability trace.
为解决在严格时限下生成真实、可验证体育赛事解说的难题，作者构建了Pitwall系统，通过将每一条自然语言陈述与基于蒙特卡洛模拟的实时赛车状态对齐，生成多语种F1策略简报。该方法在训练和生成阶段均强制验证：仅当所有陈述均被概率性赛车状态支持时，才作为训练目标；否则回退至可证明忠实的模板。实验表明，该系统在155次回测中预测冠军准确率达90.3%，并在2026年英国大奖赛中成功在终点前十圈锁定最终胜者，证明了忠实性与生动性之间存在权衡，必须分别控制以确保实时部署的可靠性。
Universal Algorithm-Implicit Learning
Authors: Stefano Woerner, Seong Joon Oh, Christian F. Baumgartner
Venue: ICML 2026
First: 2026-02-16T14:05:07+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-07T16:55:06+00:00
Comments: Accepted at ICML 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Current meta-learning methods are constrained to narrow task distributions with fixed feature and label spaces, limiting applicability. Moreover, the current meta-learning literature uses key terms like "universal" and "general-purpose" inconsistently and lacks precise definitions, hindering comparability. We introduce a theoretical framework for meta-learning which formally defines practical universality and introduces a distinction between algorithm-explicit and algorithm-implicit learning, providing a principled vocabulary for reasoning about universal meta-learning methods. Guided by this framework, we present TAIL, a transformer-based algorithm-implicit meta-learner that functions across tasks with varying domains, modalities, and label configurations. TAIL features three innovations over prior transformer-based meta-learners: random projections for cross-modal feature encoding, random injection label embeddings that extrapolate to larger label spaces, and efficient inline query processing. TAIL achieves state-of-the-art performance on standard few-shot benchmarks while generalizing to unseen domains. Unlike other meta-learning methods, it also generalizes to unseen modalities, solving text classification tasks despite training exclusively on images, handles tasks with up to 20$\times$ more classes than seen during training, and provides orders-of-magnitude computational savings over prior transformer-based approaches.
中文标题/摘要
标题：通用算法隐式学习
当前元学习方法受限于狭窄的任务分布和固定的特征与标签空间，限制了其适用性。此外，现有元学习文献对“通用”和“通用目的”等术语使用不一致且缺乏精确定义，阻碍了可比性。我们提出了一种元学习的理论框架，正式定义了实用通用性，并引入了算法显式与算法隐式学习的区别，为推理通用元学习方法提供了原则性术语。基于该框架，我们提出了TAIL，一种基于Transformer的算法隐式元学习器，可在不同领域、模态和标签配置的任务间运作。TAIL相比先前的Transformer元学习器有三项创新：用于跨模态特征编码的随机投影、可外推至更大标签空间的随机注入标签嵌入，以及高效的内联查询处理。TAIL在标准少样本基准上达到最先进性能，同时能泛化到未见领域。与其他元学习方法不同，它还能泛化到未见模态，即使仅在图像上训练，也能解决文本分类任务；支持训练时未见类别的任务，最多可处理训练时类数的20倍；相比先前的Transformer方法，计算效率提升数个数量级。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitations of current meta-learning methods in handling diverse task distributions and the lack of precise terminology for universality, this paper introduces a theoretical framework that formally defines algorithm-implicit learning and distinguishes it from algorithm-explicit approaches. Guided by this framework, the authors propose TAIL, a transformer-based meta-learner that adapts to tasks with varying domains, modalities, and label spaces through innovations including random projections for cross-modal encoding, random injection label embeddings for extrapolation, and efficient inline query processing. Socially-Aware Autonomous Doorway Traversal and Payload Delivery for Emergency Assistance
Authors: Andrew Snowdy, Ananya Trivedi, Sarvesh Prajapati, Lorena Maria Genua, Taskin Padir
First: 2026-07-06T16:56:00+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-07T16:49:51+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
In this work, we focus on the scenario of a robot-assisted emergency evacuation. We consider two capabilities relevant to such a setting. The first is opening doors ahead of the people being evacuated, so that their path toward an exit stays clear. The second is retrieving rescue equipment and delivering it to the emergency responders carrying out the evacuation. From a systems perspective, this involves several tasks at once. The robot must locate ADA-compliant door buttons and the rescue equipment it needs to retrieve. Additionally, it must remain aware of the people around it and adapt its behavior to them, so that it supports the evacuation rather than getting in the way. We address these demands with a behavior tree at the core of our framework. This structure is chosen for its ability to select high-level tasks based on environmental triggers, and to extend to new situations as they arise. We evaluate the system in 105 trials on the Toyota Human Support Robot, across five hardware and three simulation scenarios. These trials capture the decisions the robot must make in this setting: whether to press a door button, yield to a nearby person, walk through a door someone else is holding, or first retrieve rescue equipment before traversing the door. Overall, the system completes 97 of the 105 trials successfully. These results suggest our framework provides a practical basis for robotic assistance in broader emergency response tasks. Code and video demonstrations are available at https://github.com/AndrewSnowdy/hsr_mm_control.

Multi-Agent Deep Reinforcement Learning for Multi Objective Battery Management in Dairy Farms
Authors: Marcos Eduardo Cruz Victorio, Karl Mason
First: 2026-07-07T16:48:27+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-07T16:48:27+00:00
Comments: 8 pages, 2 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The dairy industry in Ireland has a large potential for the integration of renewable energy and the reduction of carbon emissions. However, researchers of distributed generation control are mainly focused on residential and commercial applications. To contribute to the effective integration of renewable energy in the dairy sector, this paper presents a multi-objective optimisation control system based on differential evolution and multi agent Deep Reinforcement Learning. The proposed control is organised in two layers: the upper layer uses dynamic pricing, and the lower layer is based on multi-agent reinforcement learning for battery management. This paper also simulates the electrical response of the proposed control system in a rural distribution circuit. The simulation results show that the proposed control framework can improve profits from energy arbitrage up to 18% compared to using Rule-based models, increase the use of distributed generation without significantly increasing cost, and comply with the Irish grid code in terms of voltage variation.

标题：面向乳制品农场多目标电池管理的多智能体深度强化学习

Motivated by the need to integrate renewable energy and reduce emissions in Ireland’s dairy sector, this study proposes a two-layer control system combining dynamic pricing with multi-agent Deep Reinforcement Learning for battery management.
