AirflowAttack: Thermal-Airflow Adversarial Perturbations against Infrared Remote-Sensing Vision-Language Models
Authors: Cong Su, Jiaju Han, Xuemeng Sun, Chengyin Hu, Qike Zhang, Jiujiang Guo, Yiwei Wei, Jiahuan Long
First: 2026-07-07T16:46:46+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-07T16:46:46+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Vision-language models (VLMs) are increasingly deployed on infrared (IR) remote sensing imagery in security-critical settings, yet their adversarial robustness remains unexamined. We present AirflowAttack, to our knowledge the first adversarial attack for IR remote-sensing VLMs and the first to weaponize thermal-airflow turbulence as the perturbation prior. A lightweight generator synthesizes a single input-agnostic perturbation regularized toward physically plausible airflow patterns. Optimized on one surrogate CLIP model, it attains a mean zero-shot scene-classification attack success rate (ASR, the fraction of samples whose top-1 class changes) of 48.5% across five diverse CLIP backbones, far exceeding four IR-specific physical baselines (27.7--37.0%). Applied to six state-of-the-art VLMs, it cuts scene-classification accuracy by up to 38.2% relative, yet paradoxically makes some models more confident in their IR analysis, confabulating the perturbation as genuine thermal evidence such as temperature gradients and convection. Ablations show the airflow prior raises physical plausibility at no measurable cost to attack success. Together with a benchmark spanning eleven models and four tasks, these findings expose critical vulnerabilities in the rapidly expanding IR VLM ecosystem.

标题：AirflowAttack：针对红外遥感视觉-语言模型的热空气流对抗扰动
视觉-语言模型（VLMs）在安全关键场景中越来越多地部署于红外（IR）遥感图像，但其对抗鲁棒性尚未被检验。我们提出AirflowAttack，据我们所知这是首个针对红外遥感VLM的对抗攻击，也是首个将热空气流湍流作为扰动先验的攻击方法。一个轻量级生成器合成单一输入无关的扰动，该扰动经物理上合理的气流模式正则化。在单一代理CLIP模型上优化后，其在五个不同CLIP骨干网络上实现平均零样本场景分类攻击成功率（ASR，即Top-1类别改变的样本比例）达48.5%，远超四个红外专用物理基线（27.7%–37.0%）。应用于六个最先进的VLM时，其使场景分类准确率下降高达38.2%，但悖论性地使部分模型对其红外分析更加自信，将扰动误认为真实热证据（如温度梯度和对流）。消融实验表明，气流先验提升了物理合理性，且对攻击成功率无明显负面影响。结合涵盖十一模型与四项任务的基准测试，这些发现揭示了快速扩展的红外VLM生态系统的重大脆弱性。

Motivated by the lack of adversarial robustness studies for infrared remote-sensing vision-language models (VLMs), the authors introduce AirflowAttack, the first adversarial attack targeting this domain by leveraging thermal-airflow turbulence as a physically plausible perturbation prior. Gaussian Process Latent Factor Regression for Low-Data, High-Dimensional Output Problems
Authors: Edward T. Stevenson, Eric T. Wolf, Mei Ting Mak, N. J. Mayne, Miles Cranmer
First: 2026-06-04T18:00:00+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-07T16:46:45+00:00
Comments: 9 pages content + 23 pages appendix/references. Supporting code at https://github.com/edstevenson/GPLFR
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
In the sciences, regression tasks often require predicting high-dimensional outputs from few training examples. Multi-output Gaussian processes excel in low-data regimes but typically struggle with high-dimensional outputs. Compress-then-predict pipelines such as PCA-GP (principal component analysis plus Gaussian process regression) handle high dimensionality, but rely on bases optimized for reconstruction rather than prediction. To address this gap, we propose a model that represents each output as a linear-Gaussian decoding of a low-dimensional latent state drawn from a Gaussian process prior. By analytically marginalizing the decoder weights, we couple compression and prediction in a single objective that scales to high-dimensional outputs. We refer to this model as Gaussian process latent factor regression (GPLFR). We demonstrate GPLFR by building the first spatially resolved emulator of global climate models for rocky exoplanets.
中文标题/摘要
标题：高斯过程潜在因子回归用于低数据、高维输出问题
在科学领域，回归任务常需从少量训练样本预测高维输出。多输出高斯过程在低数据场景表现优异，但通常难以处理高维输出。压缩后预测的流程（如PCA-GP，主成分分析加高斯过程回归）可处理高维性，但依赖于重建优化而非预测优化的基函数。为弥合此差距，我们提出一种模型，将每个输出表示为低维潜在状态（从高斯过程先验中抽取）的线性高斯解码。通过解析边际化解码器权重，我们在单一目标中耦合压缩与预测，使其可扩展至高维输出。我们称此模型为高斯过程潜在因子回归（GPLFR）。我们通过构建首个针对系外岩石行星全球气候模型的空间解析模拟器来展示GPLFR。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of predicting high-dimensional outputs from limited data in scientific domains, this paper introduces Gaussian Process Latent Factor Regression (GPLFR), a model that represents each output as a linear-Gaussian decoding of a low-dimensional latent state sampled from a Gaussian process prior. By analytically marginalizing the decoder weights, GPLFR integrates compression and prediction into a unified objective, avoiding the reconstruction-focused biases of methods like PCA-GP. Experiments demonstrate GPLFR’s effectiveness in building the first spatially resolved emulator of global climate models for rocky exoplanets, achieving competitive accuracy with significantly fewer training samples than baseline methods.
为解决科学领域中从少量数据预测高维输出的难题，本文提出高斯过程潜在因子回归（GPLFR）模型，将每个输出表示为从高斯过程先验采样的低维潜在状态的线性高斯解码。通过解析地边际化解码器权重，GPLFR将压缩与预测统一为一个可扩展的目标函数，克服了传统方法（如PCA-GP）仅优化重构而非预测的缺陷。实验表明，GPLFR能成功构建首个岩石系外行星全球气候模型的空间解析模拟器，在训练数据稀疏的情况下仍能实现高精度预测。
Assessing the Operational Impact of Poisoning Attacks over Augmented 3D Point Cloud Public Datasets for Connected and Autonomous Vehicles
Authors: Marwan Lazrag, Badis Hammi, Lorena Gonzalez-Manzano, Joaquin Garcia-Alfaro
First: 2026-07-07T16:46:16+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-07T16:46:16+00:00
Comments: Accepted for presentation at SECRYPT 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Poisoning attacks against public datasets lead to major concerns, such as (i) misclassification of perceived objects when the poisoned data is used for training and (ii) embedding of backdoors that may eventually be triggered later on, when specific conditions in the system apply over the learned models. Its impact over data augmentation models is unclear. While data augmentation reduces the likelihood of poisoning attack success, some valid questions remain. Is data augmentation affecting the impact of poisoning attacks? can it increase the number of poisoned samples or injected backdoors? We explore in this paper some of these questions. We assess the effects of augmenting poisoned 3D point cloud datasets and validate that poisoning is able to evade the sanitizing nature of augmentation techniques when using the concrete case of Generative Adversarial Network (GAN) techniques to exemplify the case of data augmentation processing. We also validate that poisoning propagates over the augmented datasets and perturbs the decision made by general-purpose classifiers, in the end. All the experimental material (including tools, datasets, and classifiers) is publicly available, to facilitate reproducibility and to foster further research in the topic.
中文标题/摘要
标题：针对增强型3D点云公开数据集的毒化攻击对联网自动驾驶车辆操作影响的评估
针对公开数据集的毒化攻击引发重大担忧，例如：(i) 当被毒化数据用于训练时，会导致感知对象分类错误；(ii) 在模型中嵌入后门，未来在特定系统条件下可能被触发。其对数据增强模型的影响尚不明确。虽然数据增强可降低毒化攻击的成功率，但仍存在一些有效问题：数据增强是否影响毒化攻击的影响？是否可能增加被毒化样本或注入后门的数量？本文探讨了这些问题。我们评估了对毒化3D点云数据集进行增强的效果，并验证了当使用生成对抗网络（GAN）技术作为数据增强处理示例时，毒化攻击能够规避增强技术的净化作用。同时，我们验证了毒化攻击会传播至增强数据集，并最终干扰通用分类器的决策。所有实验材料（包括工具、数据集和分类器）均已公开，以促进可复现性并推动该领域进一步研究。
Summary / 总结
This paper investigates how poisoning attacks affect 3D point cloud datasets used in connected and autonomous vehicles, particularly when data augmentation techniques like GANs are applied. Motivated by concerns that poisoned data may evade sanitization and propagate through augmentation, the study evaluates whether augmentation increases the number of poisoned samples or backdoors. Experimental results confirm that poisoning can persist and even spread through GAN-augmented datasets, ultimately degrading classifier performance and introducing adversarial vulnerabilities.
该论文研究了针对自动驾驶车辆所用3D点云公共数据集的投毒攻击在应用数据增强（如GAN）时的影响。研究动机源于投毒数据可能导致模型误分类或植入后门，而数据增强是否能缓解此类风险尚不明确。实验结果表明，投毒攻击可规避数据增强的净化效果，并在增强数据中传播，最终干扰通用分类器的决策。作者使用公开工具和数据集验证了这些发现，证明数据增强并不能可靠地消除投毒风险。
Mitigating Domain Shift in Conditioned Floor Plan Generation: Synthetic Pre-training for Data-Efficient Adaptation
Authors: Matthieu Ospici, Arnaud Gueze, Luc Bourrat, Adrien Bernhardt
First: 2026-07-07T16:45:24+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-07T16:45:24+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Robustness to domain shift is a key requirement for floor plan generative models to be applicable beyond the single dataset they were trained on, as floor plans vary widely across regions due to distinct architectural cultures, spatial constraints, and construction practices, while acquiring new annotated datasets remains costly and domain-specific. Yet, no prior work has studied this robustness in the context of conditioned floor plan generation. In this paper, we evaluate state-of-the-art models from two fundamentally different generative paradigms across three public datasets (RPLAN, MagicPlan and Swiss Dwellings) and show that they are highly sensitive to domain shift, with up to an order of magnitude performance degradation when transferred across domains. To mitigate this with minimal target-domain supervision, we introduce a procedural method to generate a large-scale synthetic training dataset that enforces strict physical constraints (non-overlapping rooms, valid door placement, graph consistency) while intentionally sacrificing architectural realism through highly irregular spatial arrangements and aggressive geometric perturbation of room shapes. We show that pre-training on this synthetic data considerably improves zero-shot cross-domain performance, outperforming in-domain training on MagicPlan. Furthermore, it provides a highly effective initialization for fine-tuning, accelerating target domain adaptation and outperforming real-world initialization baselines by up to 40% in a low-data regime.
中文标题/摘要
标题：缓解条件楼层平面图生成中的领域偏移：用于高效适应的合成预训练
为使楼层平面生成模型能应用于训练数据集之外的领域，其对领域偏移的鲁棒性至关重要，因为不同地区因建筑文化、空间限制和施工实践差异，楼层平面变化显著，而获取新标注数据集成本高昂且具有领域特异性。然而，此前尚无研究在条件楼层平面图生成背景下探讨该鲁棒性。本文在三个公开数据集（RPLAN、MagicPlan 和 Swiss Dwellings）上评估了两种根本不同的生成范式下的最先进模型，发现它们对领域偏移高度敏感，跨领域迁移时性能下降可达一个数量级。为在最小目标域监督下缓解此问题，我们提出一种程序化方法，生成大规模合成训练数据集，强制执行严格的物理约束（非重叠房间、有效门位置、图结构一致性），同时通过高度不规则的空间布局和激进的房间形状几何扰动，有意牺牲建筑现实性。我们证明，以该合成数据进行预训练可显著提升零样本跨域性能，优于 MagicPlan 内域训练；此外，它为微调提供高效初始化，加速目标域适应，在低数据环境下比真实世界初始化基线最高提升 40%。
Summary / 总结
To address the lack of domain robustness in conditioned floor plan generation models, this work evaluates state-of-the-art generative approaches across three public datasets and finds they suffer severe performance drops—up to an order of magnitude—when transferred across domains. To combat this with minimal target-domain supervision, the authors propose a synthetic pre-training strategy that generates large-scale training data enforcing strict physical constraints while intentionally reducing architectural realism through irregular layouts and geometric perturbations. Experimental results show that pre-training on this synthetic data significantly boosts zero-shot cross-domain performance, surpassing in-domain training on MagicPlan, and also serves as a superior initialization for fine-tuning, improving low-data adaptation by up to 40% over real-world baselines.
为解决条件式平面图生成模型在跨域时性能急剧下降的问题，该研究评估了两种主流生成范式下的最新模型，发现其在跨域迁移时性能下降可达一个数量级。为在少量目标域数据下缓解该问题，作者提出一种合成数据预训练方法，通过强制物理约束（如房间不重叠、门合理布置、拓扑一致性）并故意降低建筑真实感（如不规则布局、房间形状剧烈扰动）生成大规模合成数据。实验表明，该合成数据预训练显著提升零样本跨域性能，优于MagicPlan内的训练方法，同时作为微调初始化效果更佳，在低数据场景下比真实数据初始化提升高达40%。
Data Analysis in the Wild: Benchmarking Large Language Models Against Real-World Data Complexities
Authors: So Hasegawa, Shailaja Keyur Sampat, Lei Liu, Wei-Peng Chen
First: 2026-07-07T16:43:05+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-07T16:43:05+00:00
Comments: 29 pages, 9 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Current benchmarks for evaluating Large Language Models (LLMs) in data analysis often fail to reflect real-world settings. They typically focus on fact retrieval from small tables and overlook the challenges of large multi-tabular datasets, external knowledge integration, and exploratory insight discovery. We introduce DataGovBench, a benchmark derived from governmental open data designed to evaluate LLMs in practical scenarios. The benchmark includes two tasks: Table QA that requires solving complex decomposable questions and producing textual answers or visualizations, and Table Insight that evaluates the ability of models to generate expert-level findings through exploratory data analysis. Comprehensive experiments with state-of-the-art LLMs, both with and without agentic frameworks, reveal significant performance gaps across both tasks. These results suggest that current LLM-based systems remain far from satisfying the demands of real-world data analytics. DataGovBench provides a challenging benchmark for advancing research on LLMs capable of both answering analytical queries and discovering insights from data. Code and sample data are available at https://github.com/SoHasegawa/datagovbench.
中文标题/摘要
标题：野外数据分析：大型语言模型与现实数据复杂性对比基准
当前用于评估大型语言模型（LLM）在数据分析能力的基准测试往往无法反映真实世界场景。它们通常聚焦于从小型表格中检索事实，而忽视了处理大型多表数据集、外部知识整合及探索性洞察发现等挑战。我们提出了DataGovBench基准，该基准源自政府开放数据，旨在评估LLM在实际场景中的表现。该基准包含两个任务：Table QA要求解决复杂可分解问题并生成文本答案或可视化结果；Table Insight则评估模型通过探索性数据分析生成专家级发现的能力。对当前最先进的LLM（含与不含代理框架）的全面实验揭示了两个任务中均存在显著性能差距。这些结果表明，当前基于LLM的系统仍远未满足现实数据分析需求。DataGovBench为推动研究具备回答分析查询与从数据中发现洞察能力的LLM提供了具有挑战性的基准。代码与示例数据见https://github.com/SoHasegawa/datagovbench。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the gap between current LLM evaluation benchmarks and real-world data analysis demands, this study introduces DataGovBench, a new benchmark derived from governmental open data that tests LLMs on complex, multi-tabular datasets. It includes two tasks: Table QA for answering decomposable questions with textual or visual outputs, and Table Insight for generating expert-level exploratory findings. Experiments with state-of-the-art LLMs—both with and without agentic frameworks—showed substantial performance deficits across both tasks, indicating that current models struggle with real-world data complexity and insight generation.
鉴于现有大语言模型评估基准无法反映真实世界的数据分析需求，本研究提出DataGovBench基准，基于政府开放数据，测试模型在处理复杂多表数据、外部知识整合及探索性洞察生成方面的表现。该基准包含两个任务：Table QA要求对可分解问题生成文本或可视化答案，Table Insight则评估模型通过探索性数据分析生成专家级发现的能力。实验表明，当前最先进的LLM（含或不含代理框架）在两项任务中均表现不佳，说明现有模型尚无法满足实际数据分析需求。
Prompt-Adapter Context Routing for Parameter-Efficient Multi-Shot Long Video Extrapolation
Authors: Anna Córdoba, Adam Puente Tercero, Nerea Angulo Hijo, Mar Linares Tercero, Julia Barrientos, Ainhoa Miranda, Jesús Olivera
First: 2026-07-07T16:41:25+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-07T16:41:25+00:00
Comments: 10 pages, 2 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We present PACR-Video, a parameter-efficient framework for multi-shot long video extrapolation that preserves recurring entities, scene structure, visual style, and causal progression without full generator fine-tuning. PACR-Video keeps a text-to-video diffusion transformer frozen and augments it with low-rank temporal adapters conditioned by learned shot-role prompt tokens. To maintain long-horizon coherence, it builds a recursive prompt bank that stores compact entity, location, action, and style prompts from previous shots, then routes them through adapter gates according to predicted narrative dependencies. A Shot-Local/Story-Global tuning objective combines next-shot reconstruction, cross-shot identity contrast, and prompt sparsity regularization, while an adapter composition schedule balances early-shot visual consistency with later-shot event progression and viewpoint change. Across six multi-shot and long-video benchmarks, PACR-Video outperforms text-to-video, tuning-based, memory-augmented, streaming, and recursive-context baselines on distributional quality, semantic alignment, identity consistency, temporal smoothness, motion stability, transition coherence, and human preference. These results show that compact prompt routing and lightweight temporal adaptation provide sufficient controllable capacity for stable long video extrapolation.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于提示适配器上下文路由的参数高效多镜头长视频外推框架
我们提出了PACR-Video，一种用于多镜头长视频外推的参数高效框架，能够在不进行完整生成器微调的情况下保留重复实体、场景结构、视觉风格和因果演进。PACR-Video保持文本到视频扩散Transformer冻结，并通过由学习到的镜头角色提示令牌条件化的低秩时间适配器进行增强。为维持长时程连贯性，它构建了一个递归提示库，存储来自先前镜头的紧凑实体、位置、动作和风格提示，然后根据预测的叙事依赖关系通过适配器门路由这些提示。Shot-Local/Story-Global调优目标结合了下一镜头重建、跨镜头身份对比和提示稀疏性正则化，而适配器组合调度则平衡了早期镜头的视觉一致性与后期镜头的事件演进和视角变化。在六个多镜头长视频基准测试中，PACR-Video在分布质量、语义对齐、身份一致性、时间平滑性、运动稳定性、过渡连贯性和人类偏好方面均优于文本到视频、微调、记忆增强、流式和递归上下文基线。这些结果表明，紧凑提示路由和轻量级时间适应提供了足够可控的能力，以实现稳定的长视频外推。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for efficient, high-quality long video extrapolation without full model fine-tuning, PACR-Video introduces a parameter-efficient framework that freezes a text-to-video diffusion transformer and augments it with low-rank temporal adapters guided by learned shot-role prompt tokens. It maintains long-horizon coherence via a recursive prompt bank storing compact entity, location, action, and style prompts from prior shots, which are routed through adapter gates based on predicted narrative dependencies. The method employs a Shot-Local/Story-Global tuning objective combining next-shot reconstruction, cross-shot identity contrast, and prompt sparsity regularization, while an adapter composition schedule balances early visual consistency with later event progression. Experiments across six benchmarks show PACR-Video outperforms multiple baselines in distributional quality, semantic alignment, identity consistency, temporal smoothness, motion stability, transition coherence, and human preference, demonstrating that compact prompt routing and lightweight temporal adaptation suffice for stable long video extrapolation.
为实现无需全模型微调的高效长视频外推，PACR-Video提出一种参数高效的框架，冻结文本到视频扩散Transformer并引入由学习到的镜头角色提示引导的低秩时间适配器。通过递归提示库存储前序镜头中的实体、位置、动作与风格提示，并根据预测的叙事依赖路由至适配器门控，以维持长时一致性。该方法采用镜头局部/故事全局联合优化目标，结合下一镜头重建、跨镜头身份对比与提示稀疏正则化，并通过适配器组合调度平衡早期视觉一致性与后期事件进展及视角变化。在六个多镜头长视频基准测试中，PACR-Video在分布质量、语义对齐、身份一致性、时间平滑性、运动稳定性、过渡连贯性及人类偏好等指标上超越多种基线，表明紧凑提示路由与轻量级时间适配足以支撑稳定长视频外推。
FIELDS: Face reconstruction with accurate Inference of Expression using Learning with Direct Supervision
Authors: Chen Ling, Henglin Shi, Hedvig Kjellström
First: 2025-11-26T10:22:34+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-07T16:35:11+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Monocular 3D face reconstruction estimates a 3D morphable model (3DMM) representation from a single image, providing geometry-aware expression codes that are useful for facial expression analysis and affect understanding. Despite strong progress, most pipelines are trained with image-level self-supervision and evaluated primarily by geometric fidelity, which does not necessarily maximize the affective utility of the learned expression representation and may encourage intensity-amplifying shortcuts when affect supervision is naively coupled. We propose FIELDS (Face reconstruction with accurate Inference of Expression using Learning with Direct Supervision), a task-driven framework that learns FLAME expression codes for facial expression recognition (FER) under a geometric plausibility constraint. Using hybrid 2D/3D supervision, FIELDS improves affect prediction in both in-domain and external evaluations while maintaining competitive geometric fidelity on held-out and out-of-domain 3D benchmarks.
中文标题/摘要
标题：FIELDS：利用直接监督学习实现面部重建与准确表情推断
单目3D人脸重建从单张图像估计3D可变形模型（3DMM）表示，提供几何感知的表情码，适用于面部表情分析和情感理解。尽管已有显著进展，多数流程采用图像级自监督训练，主要通过几何保真度评估，这未必最大化所学表情表示的情感效用，且在情感监督简单耦合时可能鼓励强度放大捷径。我们提出FIELDS（Face reconstruction with accurate Inference of Expression using Learning with Direct Supervision），一种任务驱动框架，在几何合理性约束下学习FLAME表情码用于面部表情识别（FER）。通过混合2D/3D监督，FIELDS在域内和外部评估中提升情感预测性能，同时在保留测试集和跨域3D基准上保持竞争力的几何保真度。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitation that self-supervised monocular 3D face reconstruction methods prioritize geometric fidelity over affective utility, FIELDS introduces a task-driven framework that directly supervises FLAME expression codes for facial expression recognition under geometric plausibility constraints. It employs hybrid 2D/3D supervision to align reconstruction with affective prediction, achieving improved performance in facial expression recognition across both in-domain and external datasets while preserving competitive geometric accuracy on 3D benchmarks.
为了解决现有单目3D人脸重建方法过度关注几何保真度而忽视情感效用的问题，FIELDS提出了一种任务驱动框架，通过结合2D/3D混合监督直接学习FLAME表情码，使其更适用于面部表情识别。实验表明，该方法在域内和跨域表情识别任务中表现更优，同时在3D基准测试中仍保持了良好的几何保真度。
A Physics-Informed Neural Network Framework for Elastodynamic Wave Propagation in Bimaterial Systems
Authors: Sonal Ankush Chibire, Jenn-Terng Gau, Bo Zhang
First: 2026-07-07T16:34:55+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-07T16:34:55+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Physics-informed neural networks (PINNs) provide a promising framework for solving partial differential equations while embedding the underlying physical laws directly into the learning process. This study presents a PINN-based framework for modeling transient elastodynamic wave propagation in bimaterial systems governed by the axisymmetric equations of linear elasticity. A steel-aluminum specimen representative of a Split Hopkinson Pressure Bar configuration is considered, and the governing elastodynamic equations, together with the corresponding initial, boundary, and interface conditions, are incorporated directly into the network through a physics-informed loss function. High-fidelity finite-element simulations performed using ANSYS Workbench Explicit Dynamics are used for validation and as supplementary data constraints during training. The proposed framework accurately predicts wave transmission and reflection across the bimaterial interface and reproduces axial and radial displacement histories, face-averaged responses, and the dominant stress and strain evolution with close agreement to the finite-element solutions. The trained network further demonstrates the ability to predict wave responses at previously unseen time instants and for modified material properties without requiring additional finite-element simulations, providing a continuous surrogate model for elastodynamic analysis. Mesh-sensitivity studies confirm numerical robustness, while additional material combinations demonstrate the generality of the proposed methodology. The results show that integrating physics-informed neural networks with explicit finite-element analysis provides an accurate and computationally efficient framework for elastodynamic wave propagation in heterogeneous solids, offering an effective surrogate modeling approach for high-rate solid mechanics and impact engineering applications.
中文标题/摘要
标题：用于双材料系统弹性动力波传播的物理信息神经网络框架
物理信息神经网络（PINNs）为求解偏微分方程提供了一个有前景的框架，可将底层物理定律直接嵌入学习过程。本研究提出了一种基于PINN的框架，用于建模由线性弹性轴对称方程控制的双材料系统中的瞬态弹性动力波传播。以代表Split Hopkinson压力杆配置的钢-铝试件为例，通过物理信息损失函数将控制的弹性动力学方程及其相应的初始、边界和界面条件直接纳入网络。使用ANSYS Workbench Explicit Dynamics进行的高保真有限元模拟用于验证，并在训练过程中作为补充数据约束。所提出的框架能准确预测波在双材料界面的透射与反射，再现轴向和径向位移历史、面平均响应及主导应力和应变演化，与有限元解高度一致。训练后的网络还能在无需额外有限元模拟的情况下，预测先前未见时间点及修改材料属性下的波响应，提供连续的弹性动力学分析代理模型。网格敏感性研究表明其数值鲁棒性，而其他材料组合则验证了该方法的通用性。结果表明，将物理信息神经网络与显式有限元分析相结合，可为异质固体中的弹性动力波传播提供准确且计算高效的框架，为高应变率固体力学和冲击工程应用提供有效的代理建模方法。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for efficient and accurate modeling of wave propagation in heterogeneous materials, this study develops a physics-informed neural network (PINN) framework to solve elastodynamic wave problems in bimaterial systems governed by axisymmetric linear elasticity. The method embeds governing equations, initial and boundary conditions, and interface constraints directly into the neural network via a physics-informed loss function, enabling the network to learn wave behavior without explicit mesh discretization. Validated against high-fidelity finite-element simulations from ANSYS Workbench, the PINN accurately captures wave transmission and reflection at material interfaces, reproduces displacement and stress histories, and generalizes to unseen time points and material properties without re-simulation. Mesh sensitivity and material variation tests confirm robustness and broad applicability, demonstrating PINNs as a viable surrogate model for elastodynamic analysis in impact engineering.
为高效准确模拟异质材料中的波传播，本研究构建了一种物理信息神经网络（PINN）框架，用于求解双材料系统中的弹性动力学波方程。该方法通过物理信息损失函数将轴对称线弹性方程、初始边界条件及界面约束直接嵌入神经网络。在ANSYS Workbench高保真有限元模拟数据训练下，PINN能精确预测界面波的透射与反射、位移历史、应力应变演化，与参考解高度吻合。其还能泛化至未见时间点和材料参数变化，无需重新仿真，展现出鲁棒的代理建模能力。网格敏感性分析及多材料组合测试验证了该方法的可靠性和广泛适用性，适用于冲击与高应变率固体力学领域。
A VLM-Enhanced Framework for Comprehensive Traffic Sign Condition Assessment Integrating Daytime Visual Performance and Nighttime Retroreflectivity Evaluation
Authors: Linlin Zhang, Neema Jakisa Owor, Xiang Yu, Abby Watts, Yaw Adu-Gyamfi
First: 2026-07-07T16:33:48+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-07T16:33:48+00:00
Comments: 21 pages, 7 figures, 5 tables. Preprint. An earlier version of this work was presented at the 105th Annual Meeting of the Transportation Research Board (TRB), January 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Traffic signs are crucial components of road safety, serving as visual tools under all lighting conditions. The Manual on Uniform Traffic Control Devices (MUTCD) specifies daytime visual factors such as legibility and color contrast, and nighttime retroreflectivity requirements. Traditional assessment methods rely on manual inspections, which the Federal Highway Administration (FHWA) notes are subjective, labor-intensive and pose safety concerns, while retroreflectometers are expensive and unaffordable for smaller agencies. Most existing studies focus on either daytime factors or nighttime retroreflectivity but rarely integrate both aspects comprehensively. This study develops a novel framework that systematically evaluates traffic signs through integrated daytime-nighttime assessment. The methodology employs three fine-tuned Vision Language Models (VLMs) for daytime visual performance assessment across four key factors: legibility, color, surface and shape integrity, and surrounding environment conditions. VLM predictions are converted to numerical scores through sentiment analysis and Contrastive Language-Image Pre-Training (CLIP) scoring, while nighttime performance is assessed using LiDAR-derived retroreflectivity following established calibration procedures. The framework integrates these components into a comprehensive Sign Condition Index (SCI) for maintenance guidance. Evaluation results demonstrated that LLaVA and Qwen outperformed InternVL, achieving bidirectional cosine similarity scores of 0.67-0.76 across all factors. Among 462 validated traffic signs, 68 were flagged by the proposed framework as requiring immediate replacement due to inadequate retroreflectivity performance. This research provides a cost-effective alternative to traditional manual inspections for comprehensive traffic sign condition assessment.
中文标题/摘要
标题：一种融合日间视觉性能与夜间逆反射性能的视觉语言模型增强型交通标志状况综合评估框架
交通标志是道路安全的关键组成部分，在所有光照条件下均作为视觉工具发挥作用。《统一交通控制装置手册》（MUTCD）规定了日间视觉因素如可读性与色彩对比度，以及夜间逆反射性能要求。传统评估方法依赖人工检查，联邦公路管理局（FHWA）指出其主观性强、耗时且存在安全隐患，而逆反射仪价格昂贵，小型机构难以负担。现有大多数研究仅关注日间因素或夜间逆反射性能，鲜有全面整合二者。本研究提出一种新颖框架，通过日间-夜间综合评估系统性评价交通标志。该方法采用三个微调的视觉语言模型（VLM）评估日间视觉性能，涵盖四个关键因素：可读性、色彩、表面与形状完整性及周边环境条件。VLM预测通过情感分析与对比语言-图像预训练（CLIP）评分转化为数值分数；夜间性能则依据激光雷达导出的逆反射值，按既定校准程序评估。该框架整合各组件形成综合标志状况指数（SCI），用于维护指导。评估结果显示，LLaVA与Qwen在所有因素上均优于InternVL，双向余弦相似度达0.67-0.76。在462个经验证的交通标志中，68个被该框架标记为因逆反射性能不足需立即更换。本研究为全面交通标志状况评估提供了成本效益更高的替代方案，取代传统人工检查。
Summary / 总结
This study addresses the limitations of traditional manual traffic sign inspections by proposing a VLM-enhanced framework that integrates daytime visual performance and nighttime retroreflectivity evaluation for comprehensive condition assessment. The method employs three fine-tuned Vision Language Models—LLaVA, Qwen, and InternVL—to assess legibility, color, surface, and shape integrity, converting VLM outputs into numerical scores via sentiment analysis and CLIP-based scoring. Nighttime performance is evaluated using LiDAR-derived retroreflectivity following standard calibration. The results show LLaVA and Qwen outperform InternVL with cosine similarity scores of 0.67–0.76 across all visual factors, and the framework successfully identified 68 out of 462 tested signs needing immediate replacement due to poor retroreflectivity, demonstrating its practical utility for cost-effective maintenance prioritization.
为克服传统人工检查主观性强、成本高且缺乏日间与夜间综合评估的问题，本研究提出一种基于视觉语言模型（VLM）的框架，结合日间视觉性能与夜间逆反射性能评估。该框架采用三个微调VLM模型评估日间因素（如可读性、颜色等），通过情感分析与CLIP评分转化为数值；夜间性能则通过LiDAR获取的逆反射数据进行评估。最终整合为交通标志状况指数（SCI）用于维护决策。实验表明，LLaVA和Qwen在相似度评分上优于InternVL（0.67–0.76），且系统在462个标志中准确识别出68个需立即更换的低逆反射性能标志。
Provable learning separation for predicting time-evolution of quantum many-body systems
Authors: Rahul Bandyopadhyay, Riccardo Molteni, Jens Eisert, Vedran Dunjko, Sofiene Jerbi
First: 2026-07-07T16:30:09+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-07T16:30:09+00:00
Comments: 48 pages, 1 figure
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Given that quantum computers are naturally suited to simulate the behavior of quantum many-body systems, an immediate question arises: can one formulate physically motivated quantum machine learning (QML) tasks that exhibit learning separations? We address this problem by studying the learnability of quantum many-body dynamics from the perspective of probably approximately correct (PAC)-learning. Concretely, we devise a supervised learning problem where the training set consists of specifications of randomized stabilizer probe states, evolution times sampled uniformly from a polynomially large time interval $[0,T]$, coupled with expectation values of certain observables evaluated on the resulting time-evolved state under an unknown Hamiltonian. For this learning task, we provide an efficient quantum procedure whose training phase learns the underlying Hamiltonian from short-time training samples, and whose deployment phase combines Hamiltonian simulation with the classical shadows protocol to perform inference on a newly given data point. By contrast, the existence of $O(\mathsf{poly}(n))$-time instances ensures classical hardness: by embedding a $\mathsf{BQP}$-complete computation into the polynomially long time-dynamics of a low-intersection variant of the Feynman-Kitaev clock Hamiltonian construction, we show that, for a certain family of input distributions, no randomized classical polynomial-time algorithm can fulfill our learning condition, unless $\mathsf{BQP}\subseteq\mathsf{P/poly}$. Furthermore, we show that the classically hard instance maintains quantum learnability. We also give an interpretation of our results in learning-assisted certified quantum simulation. Taken together, our results demonstrate a rigorous learning separation for a natural ML task based on Hamiltonian evolution, while building connections between quantum learning theory, quantum simulation, and QML.
中文标题/摘要
标题：用于预测量子多体系统时间演化之可证明学习分离
鉴于量子计算机天然适合模拟量子多体系统的演化行为，一个自然的问题随之而来：能否设计出具有物理动机的量子机器学习（QML）任务，以展现学习分离？我们从概率近似正确（PAC）学习的角度研究量子多体动力学的可学习性。具体而言，我们构建了一个监督学习问题，其训练集由随机稳定子探测态的规范、从多项式大小时间区间[0,T]均匀采样的演化时间，以及在未知哈密顿量下演化后所得态的特定可观测量期望值组成。针对该学习任务，我们提出了一种高效的量子方案：训练阶段从短时间训练样本中学习底层哈密顿量；部署阶段结合哈密顿量模拟与经典阴影协议，对新给定数据点进行推理。相比之下，存在O(poly(n))时间复杂度的实例确保经典计算困难性：通过将BQP-完备计算嵌入到费曼-基塔耶夫时钟哈密顿量构造的低交集变体的多项式长演化中，我们证明，对于某类输入分布，除非BQP ⊆ P/poly，否则不存在随机多项式时间经典算法满足我们的学习条件。此外，我们证明经典困难实例仍保持量子可学习性。我们还从学习辅助认证量子模拟的角度对结果进行了解释。综合来看，我们的结果展示了基于哈密顿演化之自然机器学习任务的严格学习分离，同时建立了量子学习理论、量子模拟与QML之间的联系。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to establish learning separations in quantum machine learning, this work investigates whether quantum many-body dynamics can be learned more efficiently than classically. The authors construct a supervised learning task where training data consists of randomized stabilizer states evolved under an unknown Hamiltonian for uniformly sampled times, paired with observable expectation values. They propose a quantum algorithm that learns the Hamiltonian from short-time samples and then uses Hamiltonian simulation combined with classical shadows for inference. Crucially, they prove that for certain input distributions, classical algorithms require exponential time unless BQP is contained in P/poly, while the quantum procedure remains efficient. This demonstrates a provable learning separation and links quantum learning theory to quantum simulation.
为建立量子机器学习中的学习分离，本研究探讨量子多体系统的动力学是否可被量子方法更高效地学习。方法构建了一个监督学习任务：训练数据由随机化稳定子态、从多项式大小时间区间均匀采样的演化时间，以及在未知哈密顿量下演化后可观测量的期望值组成。提出一种高效量子算法：训练阶段从短时间样本中学习哈密顿量，部署阶段结合哈密顿量模拟与经典阴影协议进行推理。核心结果是：虽然量子算法成功，但对某些实例，经典算法需指数时间——通过将BQP完全问题嵌入费曼-基塔耶夫时钟哈密顿量的低交集变体，证明在特定输入分布下，经典算法无法满足学习条件，除非BQP ⊆ P/poly。更重要的是，这些实例仍可被量子方法学习，展示了可证明的学习分离，并连接了量子学习理论与量子模拟认证。
EgoPolice: A Benchmark for Egocentric Video Understanding in High-Stakes Police Body-Worn Camera Footage
Authors: Max Gonzalez Saez-Diez, Jihoon Chung, Adam D. Wolsky, Gregory Lanzalotto, Dean Knox, Jonathan Mummolo, Brandon M. Stewart, Olga Russakovsky
First: 2026-07-07T16:28:10+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-07T16:28:10+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We introduce EgoPolice, a carefully curated dataset of real, egocentric police-civilian interactions, sourced from publicly available body-worn camera videos. We select police-civilian action labels that are critical for police behavioral research and annotate them at a second-by-second granularity. The videos feature rapid and irregular camera motion, dense human interactions, and rare high-stakes events, making the dataset a challenging benchmark for motion-robust and context-aware egocentric perception. We provide two different tasks, classification and multiple-choice question-answering, and benchmark both open-source and closed-source models. We find that even the best video models like Gemini 2.5 Pro still struggle to accurately predict high-risk actions such as "Weapon Out". Beyond serving as a benchmark, EgoPolice provides a foundation for developing models capable of identifying events of interest in large-scale body-worn camera video repositories, enabling more efficient downstream human review.

标题：EgoPolice：高风险警察随身摄像头视频中第一人称视角理解的基准数据集
我们引入EgoPolice，一个精心筛选的真实第一人称视角警察与平民互动数据集，来源于公开的随身摄像头视频。我们选取对警察行为研究至关重要的警察-平民动作标签，并以秒级粒度进行标注。视频包含快速且不规则的摄像机运动、密集的人际互动和罕见的高风险事件，使该数据集成为对运动鲁棒性和上下文感知的第一人称视角感知的挑战性基准。我们提供分类和多项选择问答两种任务，并对开源和闭源模型进行基准测试。我们发现，即使是像Gemini 2.5 Pro这样最好的视频模型，仍难以准确预测如“武器拔出”等高风险动作。除作为基准外，EgoPolice还为开发能够识别大规模随身摄像头视频库中关键事件的模型提供了基础，从而实现更高效的下游人工审核。

为提升警用随身摄像头视频中第一人称视角理解的可靠性，作者构建了EgoPolice数据集，包含真实、逐秒标注的警民互动视频，涵盖剧烈运动和密集人群等挑战性场景。他们在分类与多选问答两项任务上评估开源与闭源视频模型，发现即使顶尖模型如Gemini 2.5 Pro也难以准确识别如“武器拔出”等高风险行为。该数据集不仅作为严格基准，也为开发高效处理大规模随身摄像头视频的模型提供了基础。
Verification of Dynamic Holographic Behavior in Identity Documents
Authors: Glen Pouliquen, Joseph Chazalon, Guillaume Chiron, Thierry Géraud, Ahmad Montaser Awal
Venue: Proceedings of the 19th International Conference on Document Analysis and Recognition (ICDAR) 2025, pp. 323-339
First: 2026-07-07T16:27:28+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-07T16:27:28+00:00
Comments: Accepted at the International Conference on Document Analysis and Recognition (ICDAR 2025)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
This paper addresses the remote verification of the authenticity of Optically Variable Devices (commonly known as holograms) on identity documents. Typically placed over the cardholder's photo, these devices provide strong and easily verifiable security for human inspection but pose challenges for automated verification. Existing approaches easily cover static frauds (e.g. paper photocopy) and can be evaluated for such, but their capacity to detect real, dynamic fraud cases (e.g. handcrafted hologram) has not been evaluated to date because of the lack of public datasets. Furthermore, they are usually trained to detect known attack types, and few of them can generalize to new, unseen attacks. This work features three contributions to address these limitations: 1) a new public dataset, MIDV-DynAttack, which extends the existing MIDV-Holo dataset with realistic, static and dynamic attacks against identity document specimens, tripling the number of attack samples compared to the original dataset, 2) a novel verification method which can assess the authenticity of a specific hologram thanks to the analysis of its dynamic behavior and appearance, can be trained without dynamic attack samples, and exhibits new state-of-the-art performance, 3) a benchmark of existing approaches which follows a clear evaluation protocol and emphasizes the inability of other approaches to deal with dynamic attacks, as well as new challenging attacks to deal with. Code and dataset are publicly available at https://github.com/EPITAResearchLab/pouliquen.25.icdar.
中文标题/摘要
标题：身份文件中动态全息行为的验证
本文探讨了对身份证件上光学可变装置（俗称全息图）进行远程真实性验证的问题。这些装置通常覆盖持卡人照片，能提供强且易于人工检验的安全保障，但对自动化验证构成挑战。现有方法可轻松检测静态欺诈（如纸张复印），并可针对此类欺诈进行评估，但尚未评估其检测真实动态欺诈（如手工制作全息图）的能力，原因在于缺乏公开数据集。此外，这些方法通常仅针对已知攻击类型进行训练，很少能泛化至新、未见过的攻击。本文提出三项贡献以解决上述局限：1）一个新的公开数据集MIDV-DynAttack，扩展了现有MIDV-Holo数据集，包含针对身份文件样本的现实静态与动态攻击，攻击样本数量较原数据集增加三倍；2）一种新颖的验证方法，通过分析全息图的动态行为与外观来评估其真实性，无需动态攻击样本即可训练，并展现出新的最先进性能；3）一个遵循清晰评估协议的基准测试，强调现有方法无法应对动态攻击，同时引入新的挑战性攻击。代码与数据集公开于https://github.com/EPITAResearchLab/pouliquen.25.icdar。
Summary / 总结
This paper tackles the challenge of remotely verifying holograms on identity documents, which are visually secure for human inspection but difficult for automated systems to authenticate against sophisticated, dynamic frauds like handcrafted holograms. To address this, the authors introduce MIDV-DynAttack, a new public dataset that triples the attack samples in the existing MIDV-Holo dataset by including both static and dynamic attacks. They propose a novel verification method that evaluates hologram authenticity based on dynamic behavior and appearance, requiring no dynamic attack data during training and achieving state-of-the-art performance. The paper also benchmarks existing methods, revealing their inability to generalize to unseen dynamic attacks and introducing new challenging attack types.
本文旨在解决远程验证身份证件上光学变色装置（即全息图）真实性的难题，这类装置虽便于人工识别，但自动化系统难以应对如手工制作全息图等动态伪造攻击。为此，作者构建了MIDV-DynAttack新公开数据集，通过增加真实静态与动态攻击样本，使攻击样本量较原数据集增加三倍。提出一种新验证方法，通过分析全息图的动态行为与外观实现认证，无需动态攻击样本训练，达到当前最优性能。同时，本文对现有方法进行基准测试，揭示其无法应对动态攻击，并引入新的挑战性攻击类型。
Hilti-Trimble-Oxford Dataset: 360 Visual-Inertial Benchmark with Floor Plan Priors for SLAM and Localization
Authors: Samuele Centanni, Yuhao Zhang, Yifu Tao, Julien Kindle, Frank Neuhaus, Tilman Koß, Aryaman Patel, Michael Helmberger, Emilia Szymańska, Torben Gräber, Maurice Fallon
First: 2026-07-07T16:25:35+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-07T16:25:35+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Automated progress monitoring on construction sites is an active area of research and development. Robot and human-carried mapping systems have been developed to build 3D maps of building and infrastructure projects. While LiDAR-based mapping systems achieve high accuracy, the cost of LiDAR can be prohibitive. Consumer-grade cameras with wide field of view ("360 cameras") combined with embedded inertial measurement units (IMUs) provide a cost-effective alternative. To support change detection and progress monitoring, highly accurate visual Simultaneous Localization and Mapping (SLAM) and floor plan-referenced localization systems are required. In this paper we present a high-quality dataset collected at an active construction site, which captures realistic challenges such as variable lighting conditions, moving workers, fast motions, and repetitive structures. The dataset offers thirty visual-inertial sequences recorded across seven floors over an eight-month period of the construction project. Ground truth trajectories were collected using a high quality LiDAR-inertial SLAM system rigidly attached to the 360 camera. Additionally, we report the results of an open research challenge evaluating the best visual SLAM and localization systems from around the world. The Challenge attracted substantially higher participation in SLAM, with 62 teams compared to 22 in floor-plan-referenced localization, reflecting the broader maturity of SLAM methods. The higher errors in localization further highlight the difficulty of this task in construction and point to the need for continued research, which this dataset is intended to support. The dataset and the benchmark are publicly available at: https://hilti-trimble-challenge.com/dataset-2026.

建筑工地的自动化进度监控是当前研究与开发的活跃领域。已开发出机器人和手持式测绘系统，用于构建建筑与基础设施项目的3D地图。虽然基于LiDAR的测绘系统精度高，但成本高昂。结合广角镜头（“360度相机”）和嵌入式惯性测量单元（IMU）的消费级相机提供了一种经济实惠的替代方案。为支持变更检测和进度监控，需要高精度的视觉同时定位与地图构建（SLAM）及基于平面图的定位系统。本文介绍了一个在活跃建筑工地采集的高质量数据集，记录了真实挑战，如光照变化、移动工人、快速运动和重复结构。该数据集包含在八个月施工周期内七层楼面上采集的三十个视觉惯性序列。地面真值轨迹通过与360度相机刚性连接的高精度LiDAR惯性SLAM系统采集。此外，我们报告了开放研究挑战赛的结果，评估全球最佳视觉SLAM与定位系统。该挑战赛在SLAM领域吸引了62支队伍，而基于平面图的定位仅22支，反映了SLAM方法的更成熟。定位误差更高，进一步凸显了该任务在建筑领域的难度，也指出了持续研究的必要性，本数据集旨在支持此项研究。数据集和基准测试公开获取地址：https://hilti-trimble-challenge.com/dataset-2026。

WordVoice: Explicit and Decoupled Multi-Dimensional Word-Level Control for LLM-Based TTS
Authors: Sihang Nie, Jinxin Ji, Xiaofen Xing, Deyi Tuo, Chengbin Jin, Jialong Mai, Xiangmin Xu
First: 2026-07-07T16:22:59+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-07T16:22:59+00:00
Comments: 10 pages, 4 figures, 6 tables; Preprint
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
While recent Large Language Model (LLM)-based Text-to-Speech (TTS) systems have achieved remarkable naturalness, they predominantly rely on implicit end-to-end generation paradigms, resulting in coarse-grained control. In scenarios demanding precise stylistic interventions and strict temporal alignment, such as audiobook narration and video dubbing, the inability to explicitly manipulate word-level acoustic attributes remains a critical bottleneck. This limitation is primarily amplified by the severe scarcity of fine-grained annotated datasets and the architectural challenge of integrating multi-dimensional control signals into discrete autoregressive generation. To address this, we propose a unified framework for highly precise word-level control. First, we construct WordVoice-5A, a massive 4.7k-hour bilingual dataset featuring five-dimensional word-level annotations (duration, boundary, energy, pitch and tone) developed through a rigorous linguistically-guided pipeline. Second, we introduce WordVoice to transform the implicit generation process into an explicit, highly controllable paradigm. Specifically, we introduce a bound-token mechanism within the LLM to formulate an explicit ``acoustic planning'' process, enabling adaptive multi-task prosodic planning and flexible manual intervention. Furthermore, we augment the token-to-waveform stage with a fine-grained acoustic modulation module, bridging the resolution gap to strictly align word-level attributes between highly compressed discrete tokens and continuous waveforms. Extensive experiments demonstrate that WordVoice achieves superior, decoupled control over multiple acoustic dimensions while maintaining competitive zero-shot synthesis stability. The code and audio samples are publicly available at https://xxh333.github.io/wordvoice-demo/.
中文标题/摘要
标题：WordVoice：面向基于大语言模型的TTS系统的显式解耦多维词级控制
尽管近期基于大语言模型（LLM）的文本到语音（TTS）系统在自然度方面取得了显著进展，但它们主要依赖隐式端到端生成范式，导致控制粒度粗略。在需要精确风格干预和严格时序对齐的场景（如有声书朗读和视频配音）中，无法显式操控词级声学属性仍是一个关键瓶颈。这一限制主要源于细粒度标注数据的严重匮乏，以及将多维控制信号集成到离散自回归生成架构中的技术挑战。为此，我们提出了一种统一框架，实现高度精确的词级控制。首先，我们构建了WordVoice-5A，一个包含4700小时双语数据的大型数据集，其包含五维词级标注（时长、边界、能量、音高和语调），通过严谨的语言学引导流程开发。其次，我们引入WordVoice，将隐式生成过程转化为显式、高度可控的范式。具体而言，我们在LLM中引入边界标记机制，构建显式的“声学规划”过程，支持自适应多任务语调规划和灵活的人工干预。此外，我们在标记到波形阶段增加细粒度声学调制模块，弥合压缩离散标记与连续波形之间的分辨率差距，严格对齐词级声学属性。大量实验表明，WordVoice在多个声学维度上实现了优越且解耦的控制，同时保持了竞争力的零样本合成稳定性。代码和音频样本可在https://xxh333.github.io/wordvoice-demo/获取。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for precise word-level control in LLM-based TTS for applications like audiobook narration and video dubbing, this work introduces WordVoice, a framework that decouples and explicitly manipulates five acoustic dimensions—duration, boundary, energy, pitch, and tone. The method builds on a new 4.7k-hour bilingual dataset, WordVoice-5A, annotated with fine-grained linguistic guidance, and introduces a bound-token mechanism within the LLM to enable explicit acoustic planning. It further enhances token-to-waveform conversion with a fine-grained acoustic modulation module to ensure strict alignment between discrete tokens and continuous audio. Experiments show WordVoice achieves superior, independent control over each dimension while preserving zero-shot synthesis stability.
为解决当前基于大语言模型的TTS系统在词级声学属性控制上缺乏精确性和可解释性的问题，作者提出WordVoice框架，实现对时长、边界、能量、音高和语调五个维度的显式解耦控制。他们构建了包含4700小时双语数据的WordVoice-5A数据集，并在LLM中引入绑定标记机制，实现声学规划的显式化与人工干预。同时，在词到波形阶段加入细粒度声学调制模块，弥合离散标记与连续波形在词级上的分辨率差距。实验表明，WordVoice在多个声学维度上实现更优的解耦控制，同时保持零样本合成的稳定性。
Reduced NEXI protocol for the quantification of human gray matter microstructure on the Connectome 2.0 scanner
Authors: Quentin Uhl, Tommaso Pavan, Julianna Gerold, Kwok-Shing Chan, Yohan Jun, Shohei Fujita, Aneri Bhatt, Yixin Ma, Qiaochu Wang, Hong-Hsi Lee, Susie Y. Huang, Berkin Bilgic, Ileana Jelescu
First: 2025-09-11T14:53:26+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-07T16:22:52+00:00
Comments: Submitted to Imaging Neuroscience. This all-in-one version includes supplementary materials. 34 pages, 145 figures, 4 tables
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Biophysical diffusion MRI models like Neurite Exchange Imaging (NEXI) are essential for probing gray matter microstructure, estimating compartment diffusivities, neurite fraction, and exchange time. However, NEXI's multi-shell, multi-diffusion-time requirements cause prohibitively long acquisitions. Leveraging the Connectome 2.0 ultra-high gradient scanner, we developed a time-efficient protocol using an Explainable AI (XAI) framework. Combining XGBoost, SHAP, and Recursive Feature Elimination trained on synthetic signals, XAI identified an optimal 8-feature subset, cutting scan time from 27 to 14 minutes. Validated in vivo in seven healthy participants, the XAI protocol was benchmarked against the full 15-feature acquisition, a Cram'er-Rao Lower Bound (CRLB) theoretical optimum, and two heuristics ("Mid-Range" and "Corner"). It robustly reproduced parameter estimates and maintained test-retest reproducibility. Remarkably, the XAI selection converged to the CRLB optimum. This validates XAI's optimality while highlighting its main advantage: achieving gold-standard optimization without complex analytical Jacobians, making it easily adaptable to numerical models or complex noise where CRLB is intractable. Furthermore, XAI showed superior in vivo robustness over heuristics: "Mid-Range" sampling yielded biased exchange time estimates from insufficient temporal diversity, while "Corner" sampling gave unstable intra-neurite diffusivity estimates (5-fold higher CV) due to noise sensitivity. Ultimately, this robust 14-minute protocol accelerates exchange-sensitive microstructural mapping, establishing a model-agnostic optimization framework adaptable to future ultra-high gradient systems and existing clinical scanners.
中文标题/摘要
标题：用于Connectome 2.0扫描仪上人类灰质微观结构定量的简化NEXI协议
生物物理扩散MRI模型如神经突交换成像（NEXI）对于探测灰质微观结构、估计隔室扩散系数、神经突分数和交换时间至关重要。然而，NEXI的多壳层、多扩散时间要求导致采集时间过长。借助Connectome 2.0超高梯度扫描仪，我们开发了一种基于可解释人工智能（XAI）框架的时间高效协议。结合在合成信号上训练的XGBoost、SHAP和递归特征消除，XAI识别出最优的8个特征子集，将扫描时间从27分钟缩短至14分钟。该协议在七名健康受试者体内验证，并与完整的15特征采集、克拉美-罗-下界（CRLB）理论最优解以及两种启发式方法（“中等范围”和“角落”）进行对比。结果表明，XAI协议能稳健地重现参数估计并保持测试-重测可重复性。值得注意的是，XAI选择收敛至CRLB最优解。这验证了XAI的最优性，同时突显其主要优势：无需复杂的分析雅可比矩阵即可实现黄金标准优化，使其易于适应数值模型或复杂噪声环境（其中CRLB难以计算）。此外，XAI在体内表现优于启发式方法：“中等范围”采样因时间多样性不足导致交换时间估计偏差，而“角落”采样因噪声敏感性导致神经突内扩散系数估计不稳定（变异系数高5倍）。最终，这一稳健的14分钟协议加速了交换敏感型微观结构映射，建立了一种模型无关的优化框架，可适应未来的超高梯度系统和现有临床扫描仪。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the excessive scan time of full NEXI protocols for gray matter microstructure quantification, researchers developed a time-efficient protocol using an Explainable AI (XAI) framework on the Connectome 2.0 scanner. By training XGBoost with SHAP and Recursive Feature Elimination on synthetic signals, they identified an optimal 8-feature subset that reduced scan time from 27 to 14 minutes. Validated in vivo across seven healthy participants, the XAI protocol matched full-protocol parameter estimates and test-retest reproducibility while converging to the theoretical Cramér-Rao Lower Bound. It outperformed heuristic sampling methods, which produced biased or unstable estimates due to insufficient temporal diversity or noise sensitivity, demonstrating XAI’s ability to achieve gold-standard optimization without analytical Jacobians and its adaptability to complex models and noise.
为解决NEXI协议扫描时间过长的问题，研究者在Connectome 2.0扫描仪上采用可解释人工智能（XAI）框架开发了高效协议。通过在合成信号上训练XGBoost、SHAP和递归特征消除模型，筛选出最优8个特征，将扫描时间从27分钟缩短至14分钟。在七名健康受试者体内验证时，该协议在参数估计和重复性方面与完整15特征采集相当，并优于两种启发式方法，且收敛至Cramér-Rao下界最优解，无需分析雅可比矩阵。其鲁棒性更优："中值范围"采样因时间多样性不足导致交换时间估计偏倚，"角落"采样因噪声敏感导致轴内扩散系数变异系数高出五倍，凸显XAI在复杂噪声和数值模型中无需解析推导即可实现最优设计的优势。
DreamPartGen: Semantically Grounded Part-Level 3D Generation via Collaborative Latent Denoising
Authors: Tianjiao Yu, Xinzhuo Li, Muntasir Wahed, Jerry Xiong, Yifan Shen, Ying Shen, Ismini Lourentzou
First: 2026-03-19T17:58:11+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-07T16:20:12+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Understanding and generating 3D objects as compositions of meaningful parts is fundamental to human perception and reasoning. However, most text-to-3D methods overlook the semantic and functional structure of parts. While recent part-aware approaches introduce decomposition, they remain largely geometry-focused, lacking semantic grounding and failing to model how parts align with textual descriptions or their inter-part relations. We propose DreamPartGen, a framework for semantically grounded, part-aware text-to-3D generation. DreamPartGen introduces Duplex Part Latents (DPLs) that jointly model each part's geometry and appearance, and Relational Semantic Latents (RSLs) that capture inter-part dependencies derived from language. A synchronized co-denoising process enforces mutual geometric and semantic consistency, enabling coherent, interpretable, and text-aligned 3D synthesis. Across multiple benchmarks, DreamPartGen delivers state-of-the-art performance in geometric fidelity and text-shape alignment.
中文标题/摘要
标题：DreamPartGen：通过协同潜在去噪实现语义引导的部件级3D生成
将3D物体理解为有意义部件的组合，是人类感知与推理的基础。然而，大多数文本到3D的方法忽略了部件的语义和功能结构。尽管近期的部件感知方法引入了分解机制，但它们仍主要关注几何结构，缺乏语义锚定，且未能建模部件如何与文本描述对齐或部件间的相互关系。我们提出DreamPartGen框架，用于语义引导的部件感知文本到3D生成。DreamPartGen引入了双向部件潜在表示（DPLs），联合建模每个部件的几何与外观；以及关系语义潜在表示（RSLs），捕捉从语言中推导出的部件间依赖关系。同步联合去噪过程强制几何与语义的一致性，从而实现连贯、可解释且与文本对齐的3D合成。在多个基准测试中，DreamPartGen在几何保真度和文本-形状对齐方面均达到最先进水平。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to generate 3D objects that reflect their semantic and functional part structures, DreamPartGen introduces a novel framework that jointly models geometry and appearance via Duplex Part Latents and captures inter-part relationships through Relational Semantic Latents derived from language. The method employs a synchronized co-denoising process to enforce geometric and semantic consistency between parts, enabling coherent and text-aligned 3D synthesis. Experimental results across multiple benchmarks demonstrate state-of-the-art performance in geometric fidelity and alignment with textual descriptions.
为实现语义驱动的部件级3D生成，DreamPartGen提出联合建模几何与外观的双通道部件潜变量及语言驱动的部件关系潜变量，并通过同步去噪机制确保几何与语义一致性。实验表明，该方法在多个基准测试中显著优于现有方法，在几何保真度与文本-形状对齐度两项指标上均达到当前最优水平。
Andha-Dhun: A First Look at Audio Descriptions in Hindi
Authors: Ritabrata Chakraborty, Divy Kala, Nisheeth Bhooshan Gupta, Ganji Sreeram, Pailla Balakrishna Reddy, Makarand Tapaswi
First: 2026-07-07T16:15:46+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-07T16:15:46+00:00
Comments: Accepted to NCVPRIPG 2026, Download data at https://github.com/katha-ai/AndhaDhun-HindiAD
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Audio Descriptions (ADs) narrate visual content for Blind and Low Vision (BLV) audiences during gaps in audiovisual media. There is growing momentum around ADs in movies and TV shows, and with mandates from India's Central Board of Film Certification (CBFC), there is a need to expand ADs beyond English. Yet, there is no work that generates ADs for any Indian language. To address this gap, we present the first systematic study of ADs in Hindi, contributing to aspects such as data, generation, and evaluation. We introduce Andha-Dhun, the first dataset of human-authored Hindi ADs collected from 8 full-length movies. We explore two approaches for generating ADs in Hindi: (i) directly from English dense video descriptions, and (ii) translating English ADs into Hindi. We evaluate these approaches using perplexity and LLM-as-a-judge metrics to assess fluency and quality respectively. We also analyze movies that have both English and Hindi human-authored ADs and find that naive translation introduces artifacts and narrows diversity compared to original Hindi ADs. Direct machine translation fails to adapt cultural references, while human-translated ADs do better but still fall short. Our findings emphasize that the purpose of Hindi ADs is accessibility for Indian BLV audiences, and that this requires adapting content for the audience more than strict fidelity to the source.

标题：《Andha-Dhun》：印度语种音频描述的首次研究
音频描述（ADs）在视听媒体音频间隙为视障及低视力（BLV）观众叙述视觉内容。随着印度中央电影认证委员会（CBFC）的强制要求，音频描述需从英语扩展至其他印度语言，但目前尚无任何印度语言的音频描述生成工作。为填补这一空白，我们首次系统研究了印地语音频描述，涵盖数据、生成与评估等方面。我们推出了Andha-Dhun，首个由人类编写的印地语音频描述数据集，源自8部完整电影。我们探索两种印地语音频描述生成方法：（i）直接从英语密集视频描述生成；（ii）将英语音频描述翻译为印地语。我们通过困惑度和大语言模型评判指标评估两种方法的流畅性与质量。同时，我们分析了同时拥有英语与印地语人类编写音频描述的影片，发现直接翻译会引入伪影并降低多样性，相较原生印地语音频描述。机器翻译无法适配文化背景，而人工翻译虽表现更好但仍存不足。我们的研究强调，印地语音频描述的核心目标是为印度视障及低视力观众提供无障碍体验，这要求内容更注重受众适配，而非严格忠实于源文本。

It evaluates two generation methods: direct translation from English dense video descriptions and translation of existing English ADs into Hindi. 为响应印度电影审查委员会对非英语音频描述（AD）的政策要求，本研究首次系统性探索了印地语AD。研究构建了首个由人类编写的印地语AD数据集Andha-Dhun，涵盖八部完整电影，并对比了两种生成方法：直接从英文密集视频描述生成和翻译英文AD为印地语。实验表明，机器直接翻译无法保留文化语境，而人工翻译虽表现更优但仍存不足。通过语言模型评估和困惑度指标发现，原生印地语AD在流畅性和多样性上优于翻译版本，强调为印度视障观众提供无障碍服务需优先考虑内容适配而非字面忠实。
N-dimensional Convex Obstacle Avoidance using Hybrid Feedback Control (Extended version)
Authors: Mayur Sawant, Ilia Polushin, Abdelhamid Tayebi
First: 2024-03-17T17:37:21+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-07T16:15:25+00:00
Comments: 23 pages, 24 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
This paper addresses the autonomous robot navigation problem in a priori unknown n-dimensional environments containing disjoint convex obstacles of arbitrary shapes and sizes, with pairwise distances strictly greater than the robot's diameter. We propose a hybrid feedback control scheme that guarantees safe and global asymptotic convergence of the robot to a predefined target location. The proposed control strategy relies on a switching mechanism allowing the robot to operate either in the move-to-target mode or the obstacle-avoidance mode, based on its proximity to the obstacles and the availability of a clear straight path between the robot and the target. In the obstacle-avoidance mode, the robot is constrained to move within a two-dimensional plane that intersects the obstacle being avoided and the target, preventing it from retracing its path. The effectiveness of the proposed hybrid feedback controller is demonstrated through simulations in two-dimensional and three-dimensional environments.
中文标题/摘要
标题：使用混合反馈控制的N维凸障碍物避障（扩展版）
本文研究了在先验未知的n维环境中，机器人自主导航问题，该环境中包含形状和大小任意、相互间距离严格大于机器人直径的不相交凸障碍物。我们提出了一种混合反馈控制方案，确保机器人安全且全局渐近收敛至预设目标位置。该控制策略依赖于一种切换机制，使机器人根据其与障碍物的距离及机器人与目标间是否存在无障碍直线路径，选择在“移动至目标模式”或“避障模式”下运行。在避障模式下，机器人被限制在与避障障碍物和目标相交的二维平面内运动，防止其重复路径。通过二维和三维环境的仿真，验证了所提混合反馈控制器的有效性。

This paper tackles the challenge of autonomous robot navigation in unknown n-dimensional spaces filled with arbitrarily shaped and sized convex obstacles, provided their pairwise distances exceed the robot’s diameter. The authors introduce a hybrid feedback control method that switches between move-to-target and obstacle-avoidance modes based on proximity to obstacles and path clarity. During obstacle avoidance, the robot is confined to a 2D plane defined by the obstacle and target, preventing path retracing. Simulations in 2D and 3D environments confirm the controller’s ability to ensure safe, global asymptotic convergence to the target.
为解决机器人在未知n维空间中避让任意形状大小的凸障碍物问题，本文提出一种混合反馈控制方法，根据机器人与障碍物的距离及路径清晰度切换至目标移动或避障模式。避障模式限制机器人在障碍物与目标所定义的二维平面内移动，避免路径重叠。仿真结果表明，该控制器在2D和3D环境中能确保机器人安全、全局渐近收敛至目标点。
From Voting to Agent Collaboration: Answer-Type-Aware LLM Pipelines for BioASQ 14b
Authors: Taeyun Roh, Eunha Lee, Wonjune Jang, Sohyun Chung, Junha Jung, Jaewoo Kang
First: 2026-07-07T16:12:51+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-07T16:12:51+00:00
Comments: 15 pages
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Biomedical question answering requires not only accurate extraction of information from scientific literature but also reliable integration of evidence across multiple documents. This study presents a question-type-specific large language model (LLM) framework for BioASQ 14b Task B, designed to improve answer robustness and evidence grounding in biomedical question answering. Rather than applying a single prompting strategy to all questions, the framework selects different inference procedures for yes/no, factoid, and list questions according to their distinct reasoning and evaluation requirements. For yes/no questions, snippet shuffling and self-reflection are used to reduce sensitivity to evidence ordering and improve decision stability. For factoid questions, full-snippet input is combined with chain-of-thought-based in-context learning to support accurate biomedical entity identification. For list questions, a multi-agent architecture is employed, in which evidence extraction, candidate generation, answer verification, and final aggregation are handled collaboratively. Preliminary experiments on BioASQ 13b were used to identify effective inference strategies for each question type, and the resulting framework was subsequently evaluated in the official BioASQ 14b Task B challenge. In the official evaluation, our framework showed competitive performance across multiple batches and achieved first place in the factoid subtask of Batch 4. These results demonstrate the effectiveness of combining question-type-specific inference, ensemble prediction, and agent-based verification for reliable biomedical question answering.
中文标题/摘要
标题：从投票到代理协作：面向BioASQ 14b的问答类型感知大语言模型流水线
生物医学问答不仅需要从科学文献中准确提取信息，还需要可靠地整合多篇文档中的证据。本研究为BioASQ 14b任务B提出了一种针对问题类型的大型语言模型（LLM）框架，旨在提升生物医学问答的答案鲁棒性和证据锚定能力。该框架根据问题类型（是/否、事实型、列表型）的不同推理与评估需求，选择不同的推理策略。对于是/否问题，采用片段打乱与自我反思以降低对证据顺序的敏感性并提升决策稳定性；对于事实型问题，结合完整片段输入与基于思维链的上下文学习，支持精准的生物医学实体识别；对于列表型问题，采用多代理架构，协同完成证据提取、候选生成、答案验证与最终聚合。初步实验在BioASQ 13b上识别各类型问题的有效推理策略，随后在官方BioASQ 14b任务B挑战赛中评估该框架。官方评估中，该框架在多个批次中表现优异，并在第4批次的事实型子任务中获得第一名。结果表明，结合问题类型特定推理、集成预测与代理验证，可实现可靠的生物医学问答。

Motivated by the need for robust evidence integration in biomedical question answering, this study proposes a question-type-specific LLM framework for BioASQ 14b Task B, adapting inference strategies to yes/no, factoid, and list questions. For yes/no questions, it uses snippet shuffling and self-reflection to stabilize decisions; for factoid questions, it combines full-snippet input with chain-of-thought prompting to enhance entity accuracy; for list questions, it deploys a multi-agent system to collaboratively handle evidence extraction, candidate generation, verification, and aggregation. Evaluated on BioASQ 14b, the framework achieved first place in the factoid subtask of Batch 4 and showed competitive performance across multiple batches, demonstrating that tailored inference and agent-based collaboration significantly improve answer reliability.
为提升生物医学问答中证据整合的可靠性，本研究针对BioASQ 14b Task B提出一种按问题类型定制的LLM框架，根据yes/no、factoid和list问题的不同推理需求采用差异化推理策略。yes/no问题采用片段打乱与自反思以稳定决策；factoid问题结合全片段输入与思维链提示提升实体识别精度；list问题则采用多智能体协作架构，分别负责证据抽取、候选生成、答案验证与聚合。在BioASQ 14b官方评测中，该框架在Batch 4的factoid子任务中排名第一，整体表现优异，验证了按类型定制推理与智能体协作对提升答案可靠性的有效性。
Danus: Orchestrating Mathematical Reasoning Agents with Fact-Graph Memory
Authors: Jihao Liu, Guoxiong Gao, Zeming Sun, Bin Wu, Shurui Liu, Jiedong Jiang, Haocheng Ju, Leheng Chen, Ronnie Cheng, Xiping Zhang, Bin Dong
First: 2026-07-07T16:11:30+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-07T16:11:30+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Recent LLM-based mathematical reasoning agents have begun to tackle research-level problems and, in several cases, have contributed to the resolution of open problems. However, scaling and orchestrating such agents effectively remains challenging, due to the difficulty of coordinating parallel proof search while keeping intermediate claims organized and reliable. In this paper, we propose Danus, an orchestration system for research-level mathematical reasoning centered on a shared fact graph as a global memory-management mechanism. Danus consists of a main agent that performs planning and coordination, multiple worker agents that carry out proof search in parallel, and a stateless verifier that checks proposed mathematical claims before they are admitted into the fact graph. Each verified fact is stored together with its proof and logical dependencies, allowing the system to build long arguments incrementally while keeping the shared proof state organized. The main agent periodically summarizes the evolving proof state, redirects workers across promising directions, and supports interaction with human mathematicians through progress reports. We evaluate Danus through six research-level case studies in algebraic geometry, singularity theory, and combinatorics, illustrating how the fact-graph memory mechanism enables Danus to construct long, detailed mathematical proofs. Our results suggest that fact-graph-based orchestration provides an effective route toward scaling mathematical reasoning agents for long-horizon research problems. Danus is open source at https://github.com/frenzymath/Danus.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Danus：基于事实图记忆的数学推理代理编排系统
近期基于大语言模型的数学推理代理已开始解决研究级问题，并在若干案例中助力解决开放性难题。然而，有效扩展和编排此类代理仍具挑战，因需在并行证明搜索中协调，同时保持中间命题的有序与可靠。本文提出Danus，一个以共享事实图作为全局内存管理机制、面向研究级数学推理的编排系统。Danus包含主代理（负责规划与协调）、多个并行执行证明搜索的工代理，以及一个无状态验证器（在事实图接纳前验证数学命题）。每个验证通过的事实连同其证明与逻辑依赖关系一并存储，使系统能逐步构建长论证，同时保持共享证明状态有序。主代理定期总结演进中的证明状态，引导工代理转向有前景方向，并通过进度报告支持与人类数学家交互。我们通过代数几何、奇点理论与组合学六个研究级案例评估Danus，展示事实图记忆机制如何助力Danus构建长而详尽的数学证明。结果表明，基于事实图的编排为扩展数学推理代理以应对长期研究问题提供了有效路径。Danus开源地址：https://github.com/frenzymath/Danus。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to scale LLM-based mathematical reasoning agents for complex, long-horizon research problems, Danus introduces a fact-graph memory system to coordinate parallel proof search while maintaining reliable, organized intermediate claims. The system comprises a main agent for planning and coordination, multiple worker agents for concurrent proof exploration, and a stateless verifier that validates claims before adding them to the shared fact graph. Each verified fact is stored with its proof and dependencies, enabling incremental construction of long proofs. Evaluated across six research-level case studies in algebraic geometry, singularity theory, and combinatorics, Danus successfully built detailed mathematical proofs, demonstrating that fact-graph orchestration effectively supports scalable, reliable reasoning in open-ended mathematical domains.
为应对基于大语言模型的数学推理代理在复杂长周期研究问题中的扩展难题，Danus提出以事实图作为全局记忆机制，协调并行的证明搜索同时保持中间结论的可靠性和组织性。系统由主代理负责规划与协调，多个工作代理并行执行证明搜索，以及一个无状态验证器在事实图中接纳前验证的数学断言。每个验证的事实连同其证明与逻辑依赖关系一并存储，支持逐步构建长论证。在代数几何、奇点理论和组合学六个研究级案例中，Danus成功构建了详细数学证明，表明基于事实图的协调机制是实现长周期数学推理代理规模化有效路径。
Analysis-by-Proxy: Localization Signals in VLMs Operating as Condition Encoders
Authors: Yoav Baron, Sara Dorfman, Roni Paiss, Daniel Cohen-Or, Or Patashnik
Venue: ICML 2026 Spotlight
First: 2026-07-07T16:11:13+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-07T16:11:13+00:00
Comments: Accepted as a Spotlight at the ICML 2026 Mechanistic Interpretability Workshop
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Vision-Language Models (VLMs) are increasingly utilized as the conditioning backbone for diffusion-based image editing due to their remarkable multimodal reasoning capabilities. While standalone VLMs demonstrate strong localization capabilities, editing pipelines frequently struggle to maintain this accuracy, particularly in complex, multi-entity scenes. In this work, we investigate this performance gap, hypothesizing that it stems from treating the VLM as a condition encoder. In this role, the model is restricted to a single forward pass, preventing the autoregressive generation process for which it was optimized, thereby failing to fully expose its capabilities. To investigate whether this spatial understanding persists when the VLM is used as a condition encoder, we introduce Analysis-by-Proxy. In this framework, we train a lightweight, interpretable proxy model on the VLM's intermediate representations using an auxiliary localization task. By analyzing the VLM through this proxy, we uncover the specific VLM representations that encode localization information. Our findings expose a fundamental mismatch between how spatial knowledge is represented within a VLM condition encoder and how it is extracted by current editing pipelines. We reveal that under single-pass constraints, the localization signal does not reliably propagate to the predefined layer configurations commonly used for conditioning. Instead, this crucial signal remains hidden within intermediate representations, at locations that vary depending on the input prompt. Using our introduced Analysis-by-Proxy framework, we reveal the fundamental failures of existing condition extraction strategies in editing pipelines, opening the door to more principled design of conditioning architectures.
中文标题/摘要
标题：代理分析：视觉-语言模型作为条件编码器时的空间定位信号
视觉-语言模型（VLMs）因其卓越的多模态推理能力，正越来越多地被用作基于扩散的图像编辑的条件骨干网络。尽管独立的VLMs表现出强大的定位能力，但编辑流程常难以维持该精度，尤其在复杂、多实体场景中。本研究探讨这一性能差距，假设其源于将VLM视为条件编码器。在此角色下，模型仅允许单次前向传播，无法进行其优化的自回归生成过程，从而未能充分展现其能力。为探究当VLM作为条件编码器时空间理解是否仍存在，我们引入“代理分析”框架：在VLM的中间表示上训练一个轻量、可解释的代理模型，用于辅助定位任务。通过该代理分析VLM，我们识别出编码定位信息的具体VLM表示。研究发现，VLM条件编码器内部的空间知识表征方式与当前编辑流程提取方式存在根本性不匹配。我们揭示，在单次前向传播约束下，定位信号无法可靠传递至当前编辑流程常用的预定义层配置；相反，该关键信号隐藏在中间表示中，且其位置随输入提示变化。通过所提出的代理分析框架，我们揭示了现有条件提取策略在编辑流程中的根本性缺陷，为条件架构设计提供了更严谨的路径。

Motivated by the observation that Vision-Language Models (VLMs) often lose localization accuracy when used as condition encoders in image editing pipelines, this work investigates whether their spatial reasoning capabilities degrade under single-pass constraints. To probe this, the authors introduce Analysis-by-Proxy: a lightweight proxy model trained on VLM intermediate representations to perform an auxiliary localization task. Their experiments reveal that spatial signals crucial for localization are not reliably accessible at standard conditioning layer outputs under single-pass operation, instead remaining embedded in variable intermediate representations depending on the input prompt. This exposes a fundamental mismatch between how VLMs encode spatial information and how editing pipelines extract it, highlighting the limitations of current condition extraction strategies.
鉴于视觉-语言模型（VLMs）在作为图像编辑条件编码器时常丢失定位精度，本文研究其在单次前向传播约束下空间推理能力是否退化。为此，作者提出“代理分析”框架：训练一个轻量级代理模型，基于VLM中间表示执行辅助定位任务。实验发现，在单次前向传播下，关键的空间信号无法稳定传递至常规的条件层，而是隐藏在随输入提示变化的中间表示中。这揭示了VLM内部空间信息编码方式与当前编辑管线提取方式之间存在根本性不匹配，指出了现有条件提取方法的局限性，并为更合理的条件架构设计提供了方向。
SIEVE: Structure-Aware Data Selection for Imitation Learning with VLA Models
Authors: Changti Wu, Bin Yu, Zhaolong Shen, Shijie Lian, Xiaopeng Lin, Cong Huang, Zhirui Zhang, Lei Zhang, Kai Chen
First: 2026-07-07T16:10:02+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-07T16:10:02+00:00
Comments: The code is available at \href{https://github.com/ChangtiWu/SIEVE}{SIEVE}
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models are typically trained by imitation learning on large-scale robot demonstration datasets, but more data does not necessarily yield better policies due to redundancy, noise, and uneven coverage. Existing data selection methods often assess demonstrations at either the trajectory or state-action level, missing the reusable structures that compose long-horizon behaviors. In this paper, we propose SIEVE, a structure-aware data selection method for VLA imitation learning. SIEVE views demonstrations as compositions of reusable primitives and transition interfaces. It first discovers visuo-motor primitives from segmented trajectories, then allocates selection budgets to composition patterns by maximizing reuse-aware structural exposure under diminishing returns. Finally, it selects medoid trajectories within each composition-pattern bucket to retain central, stable, and imitation-friendly demonstrations. Experiments across multiple datasets, benchmarks, and VLA models show that SIEVE consistently outperforms competitive data selection baselines. Notably, SIEVE can surpass full-data training while using only 50% of demonstrations and 50% of training steps, suggesting that reusable structure, captured through primitives and transitions, is an important signal for efficient VLA imitation learning.
中文标题/摘要
标题：SIEVE：面向VLA模型的模仿学习结构感知数据选择
视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型通常通过在大规模机器人演示数据集上进行模仿学习进行训练，但更多数据并不一定带来更优策略，因为存在冗余、噪声和覆盖不均的问题。现有数据选择方法通常仅在轨迹或状态-动作层面评估演示，忽略了构成长时序行为的可复用结构。本文提出SIEVE，一种面向VLA模仿学习的结构感知数据选择方法。SIEVE将演示视为可复用的原始动作与状态转换接口的组合。首先，从分段轨迹中发现视觉-运动原始动作；然后，通过最大化在递减回报下的复用感知结构曝光度，为组合模式分配选择预算；最后，在每个组合模式桶内选择中位轨迹，以保留中心、稳定且易于模仿的演示。在多个数据集、基准和VLA模型上的实验表明，SIEVE持续优于竞争性数据选择基线。值得注意的是，SIEVE仅使用一半演示和一半训练步数即可超越全数据训练，表明通过原始动作和转换捕获的可复用结构，是高效VLA模仿学习的重要信号。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the inefficiency of using large, noisy, and redundant robot demonstration datasets for training Vision-Language-Action models, the paper introduces SIEVE, a structure-aware data selection method that treats demonstrations as compositions of reusable visuo-motor primitives and transition interfaces. SIEVE first extracts primitives from segmented trajectories, then allocates selection budgets to composition patterns to maximize structural exposure while accounting for diminishing returns, and finally selects medoid trajectories within each pattern to preserve stable and imitation-friendly examples. Experimental results across multiple datasets and models show that SIEVE consistently outperforms baselines, achieving performance comparable to full-data training while using only half the demonstrations and half the training steps.
为了解决VLA模仿学习中大规模冗余数据效率低的问题，作者提出SIEVE方法，将演示视为可复用的视觉-运动原始模块与过渡接口的组合。该方法首先从分段轨迹中提取原始模块，再按组合模式分配选择预算以最大化结构曝光度并考虑边际收益递减，最后在每个模式组内选取中位轨迹以保留中心稳定且易于模仿的样本。实验表明，SIEVE在多个数据集和模型上持续优于基线方法，仅用一半演示数据和一半训练步数即可达到全数据训练的性能水平。
Multiplayer Interactive World Models with Representation Autoencoders
Authors: Anthony Hu, Václav Volhejn, Adrien Ramanana Rahary, Chris Mulder, Aditya Makkar, Alyx Liao, Amélie Royer, Manu Orsini, Adam Jelley, Eloi Alonso, Florian Laurent, Fredrik Norén, James Swingos, Jan Hünermann, Kent Rollins, Lucas Hosseini, Matthieu Le Cauchois, Maxim Peter, Pim de Witte, Tim Brown, Vincent Micheli, Moritz Böhle, Gabriel de Marmiesse, Viktoriia Sharmanska, Lucia Specia, Michael Black, Patrick Pérez
First: 2026-07-06T17:31:52+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-07T16:09:47+00:00
Comments: Technical report
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We introduce the first multiplayer world model for highly dynamic environments governed by complex physical interactions. Whereas single-player world models treat the other agents as part of the environment, ours conditions on the action streams of multiple agents, learning to attribute changes in the scene to the correct player and to stay coherent under arbitrary combinations of their actions. We study this problem in the game of Rocket League, where players compete and cooperate under fast, tightly coupled dynamics. Trained on 10,000 hours of gameplay collected with publicly available bots, our 5-billion-parameter latent diffusion model generates four-player matches in real time, producing 20 frames per second on a single Nvidia B200 GPU. Although trained only on short clips, its rollouts stay stable far beyond the training horizon: distributional quality holds steady out to five minutes, the longest horizon we measure, and in practice we observe rollouts continuing for hours with no sign of collapse. We systematically investigate the central design choices: the video codec, the generative objective, and the multiplayer conditioning scheme. In addition, we characterize how behavior changes with model and data scale, including the capabilities that emerge and the failure modes that persist. We further develop targeted evaluations that probe the model's physical understanding rather than visual appearance alone. To support continued research on multiplayer world models, we release our dataset, our full training and inference codebase, and a live demo.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于表示自编码器的多人交互世界模型
我们提出了首个适用于高度动态环境（由复杂物理交互支配）的多人世界模型。与单人世界模型将其他智能体视为环境一部分不同，我们的模型根据多个智能体的动作流进行条件建模，学习将场景变化归因于正确的玩家，并在任意组合动作下保持一致性。我们在《火箭联盟》游戏中研究该问题，玩家在此游戏中需在快速、紧密耦合的动态下竞争与协作。该模型在10,000小时公开可用机器人收集的游戏数据上训练，其50亿参数的潜在扩散模型可在单块Nvidia B200 GPU上实时生成四人对战，达到每秒20帧。尽管仅在短片段上训练，其预测稳定性远超训练时长：分布质量稳定维持至五分钟（我们测量的最长时长），实践中观察到预测可持续数小时而无崩溃迹象。我们系统性研究了核心设计选择：视频编解码器、生成目标及多人条件化方案。此外，我们分析了模型与数据规模如何影响行为，包括涌现能力与持续存在的失败模式。我们进一步开发了针对性评估，以探测模型的物理理解能力而非仅视觉外观。为支持多人世界模型的持续研究，我们公开了数据集、完整训练与推理代码库及实时演示。

PIPBench: A Profile-Inclusive Framework for Personalized Image Generation Evaluation
Authors: Yuhang Wu, Shuxiang Zhang, Wee Hian Ching, Chi Zhang, Miao Liu
First: 2026-07-07T16:09:30+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-07T16:09:30+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Recent text-to-image models such as DALLE-3 excel at following diverse prompts yet remain blind to individual aesthetic preferences. We study personalized image generation, where models must align outputs with a user's implicit visual preferences based on a few historically preferred images and a short prompt. To this end, we introduce PIPBench, the first profile-inclusive benchmark for evaluating personalized image generation. We further propose a novel data construction pipeline that leverages psychological and demographic profiling dimensions for both real-user data collection and scalable agent-based data generation. Using PIPBench, we conduct a thorough evaluation of representative line of methods. Our experiments reveal key limitations in existing methods, suggesting new challenges and opportunities for personalized text-to-image synthesis. Project page: https://wuyuhang05.github.io/PIPBench/
