WristMimic: Full-Body Humanoid Control with Wrist-Guided Manipulation
Authors: Wongyun Yu, Youngwoon Kim, Minsu Cho
Venue: ECCV 2026
First: 2026-07-07T16:06:43+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-07T16:06:43+00:00
Comments: Accepted to ECCV 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Retargeting human object interaction demonstrations to physics based simulation requires reproducing not only body motion but also the object motion and contacts that make manipulation succeed. However, position only hand trajectories do not specify the contact forces needed to manipulate objects, and directly tracking them can overconstrain contact rich finger behavior. We introduce WristMimic, a wrist guided whole body control framework that explicitly separates contact free body motion from contact rich hand manipulation. The contact free body and wrist are guided by kinematic pose targets, whereas the fingers are not directly supervised by human hand pose. Instead, they learn grasping and manipulation behaviors from object tracking and contact outcomes. Our key insight is that the wrist is the natural gate between these two regimes. It is largely free from contact and can be tracked kinematically, yet it determines the global hand configuration and places the fingers within reachable grasp affordances. To ensure reliable wrist placement during interaction, we introduce wrist specific reset constraints and reward prioritization. Experiments show that WristMimic matches or surpasses methods using full finger pose supervision while enabling finger agnostic retargeting across diverse hand embodiments.
中文标题/摘要
标题：WristMimic：基于手腕引导的全身人形控制与操作
将人类操作物体的演示重新定向到基于物理的仿真，不仅需要复现身体运动，还需再现使操作成功的物体运动和接触。然而，仅位置的手部轨迹无法指定操作物体所需的接触力，直接跟踪这些力会过度约束富含接触的指部行为。我们提出WristMimic，一种基于手腕引导的全身控制框架，明确分离无接触的身体运动与富含接触的手部操作。无接触的身体和手腕由运动学位姿目标引导，而手指不直接由人类手部位姿监督。相反，它们从物体跟踪和接触结果中学习抓取与操作行为。我们的核心见解是，手腕是这两个模式之间的自然接口：它基本无接触且可被运动学跟踪，但决定全局手部构型并将手指置于可触及的抓取范围内。为确保交互期间手腕位置的可靠性，我们引入了针对手腕的重置约束和奖励优先级。实验表明，WristMimic在匹配或超越使用全指位姿监督的方法的同时，支持跨多样手部形态的指部无关重定向。
Summary / 总结
WristMimic addresses the challenge of retargeting human object manipulation demonstrations into physics-based simulations by separating body motion from finger manipulation, as raw hand trajectories lack contact force information and direct finger supervision overconstrains natural grasping. The method uses kinematic wrist pose targets to guide body and wrist motion while letting fingers learn grasping behaviors from object tracking and contact outcomes, leveraging the wrist as a natural interface between contact-free motion and contact-rich manipulation. Experiments demonstrate that WristMimic achieves performance matching or exceeding full-finger-supervised methods while enabling finger-agnostic retargeting across diverse hand morphologies.
WristMimic 解决了将人类操作演示迁移到物理仿真中的难题，通过分离身体运动与手指接触动力学。它利用运动学腕部姿态目标引导全身运动和腕部定位，而手指则通过物体追踪和接触结果间接学习抓取与操作行为，避免直接监督手指姿态。该方法支持跨不同手部形态的无手指依赖重定向，并通过腕部专用重置约束和奖励优先级确保交互稳定性，其性能可媲美或超越需全手指姿态监督的方法。
Finding H. pylori in the Fine Print: Evidence-Linked Multi-Agent Case Finding from Gastric Biopsy Reports
Authors: Yufan Wang, Anit Kumar Sahu, Yan Fei Ng, Daniel Kang, Shayan Vassef, Soorya Ram Shimgekar, Koustuv Saha, Piyum Zonooz, Navin Kumar, Chee Leong Cheng, Li Yan Khor
First: 2026-07-07T16:05:00+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-07T16:05:00+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Data from Singapore indicated that about 31% of the population had evidence of Helicobacter pylori infection. Persistent H. pylori infection is associated with chronic active gastritis and peptic ulcer disease, and its eradication is key to gastric cancer prevention. However, evidence supporting \textit{H. pylori} positivity and H. pylori-associated gastritis may be distributed across heterogeneous coded and free-text report fields and may require contextual interpretation of assertion and negation, limiting keyword search, and making manual review difficult to scale. We conducted a retrospective pilot evaluation of the Nimblemind Multi-Agent System (nMAS), a field-name-driven, evidence-linked extraction workflow, using 54 de-identified gastric biopsy pathology reports from a large healthcare system in Singapore. Four clinician-scoped binary fields were evaluated: gastric/stomach biopsy, biopsy status, H. pylori positivity, and H. pylori-associated gastritis. Across 216 feature-case decisions, nMAS correctly classified 213, corresponding to 98.61% overall accuracy. A separately implemented UMA-style MiniMax M2.5 comparator produced similar aggregate and per-field classification metrics. Although predictive performance was similar, nMAS maintained unified report-level outputs with supporting source sentences; the demonstrated contribution is therefore workflow integration and traceability rather than predictive superiority. Under an illustrative, unmeasured scenario, reviewing 1,000 reports at five minutes per manual review versus five seconds per evidence-linked verification would reduce review time from 83.3 to 1.4 staff-hours, corresponding to 81.9 staff-hours and about USD~6,100 in potential staff-time value. Larger multi-institutional studies should evaluate evidence-span correctness, clinician verification time, and generalizability.
中文标题/摘要
标题：在胃活检报告的细枝末节中寻找幽门螺杆菌：基于证据的多智能体病例发现
新加坡数据显示约31%人口存在幽门螺杆菌感染证据。持续性幽门螺杆菌感染与慢性活动性胃炎和消化性溃疡病相关，其根除是胃癌预防的关键。然而，支持幽门螺杆菌阳性及幽门螺杆菌相关胃炎的证据可能分散在异质化的编码和自由文本字段中，需结合断言与否定语境进行解释，限制关键词搜索，使人工审核难以规模化。我们对新加坡一家大型医疗系统提供的54份匿名胃活检病理报告，回顾性评估了Nimblemind多智能体系统（nMAS）——一种基于字段名称驱动、证据关联的提取工作流。评估了四个临床限定的二元字段：胃活检、活检状态、幽门螺杆菌阳性、幽门螺杆菌相关胃炎。在216个特征病例决策中，nMAS正确分类213例，整体准确率达98.61%。独立实现的UMA风格MiniMax M2.5比较器产生相似的总体及各字段分类指标。尽管预测性能相似，nMAS保持统一的报告级输出并附带支持句源；其贡献在于工作流整合与可追溯性，而非预测优越性。在示例性未测量场景下，若人工审核每份报告需5分钟，而证据关联验证仅需5秒，则1000份报告的审核时间将从83.3小时降至1.4小时，节省81.9小时人工时间，约值6100美元。更大规模的多机构研究应评估证据覆盖正确性、临床验证时间及泛化能力。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the high prevalence of H. pylori infection in Singapore and the clinical need for efficient detection in gastric biopsy reports, the study evaluated the Nimblemind Multi-Agent System (nMAS), a field-name-driven, evidence-linked extraction method designed to handle heterogeneous coded and free-text data. The system was tested on 54 de-identified reports across four binary clinical fields, achieving 98.61% accuracy in classifying 216 feature-case decisions. While a UMA-style MiniMax M2.5 comparator yielded similar performance, nMAS uniquely provided traceable, source-sentence-supported outputs, enhancing workflow integration. The study estimated that evidence-linked verification could reduce manual review time for 1,000 reports from 83.3 to 1.4 staff-hours, representing a potential cost savings of about USD 6,100.
鉴于新加坡人群中H. pylori感染率较高，且及时检测有助于预防胃癌，本研究评估了名为nMAS的多智能体系统，该系统通过字段驱动的方式从胃活检报告中提取并关联证据。在54份匿名报告中，系统对四个关键二元字段进行分类，216个案例决策准确率达98.61%。虽然对比系统性能相近，但nMAS能提供带源句支持的可追溯输出，提升临床工作流程整合能力。研究估算，若每份报告人工审核需5分钟、自动化验证仅需5秒，则1000份报告的审核时间可从83.3小时降至1.4小时，节省约6100美元人工成本，凸显其在可扩展性和效率方面的价值。
TILDE: TILt-based Distributional Erasure for Concept Unlearning
Authors: Naveen George, Naoki Murata, Yuhta Takida, Konda Reddy Mopuri, Yuki Mitsufuji
First: 2026-07-07T15:59:59+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-07T15:59:59+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Concept unlearning in text-to-image diffusion models is critical for safe and practical deployment: with rising privacy concerns, copyright disputes, trademark constraints, and safety regulations, deployed systems must be able to suppress unwanted concepts after training. Existing methods often remove the target concept effectively, but practical unlearning also requires an equally fundamental property: the unlearned model should retain quality, diversity, and semantic coverage on benign generation. The gold standard is a retain-only model trained from scratch without the unwanted data. However, common erasure objectives do not specify which post-unlearning distribution should approximate this reference, leaving retention as an implicit consequence of the update rule. We propose TILDE, TILt-based Distributional Erasure, which formulates concept unlearning as a distributional alignment problem: the desired target is the minimum-deviation conditional distribution from the pretrained model under a forgetting constraint. This energy-tilted, anchor-free target suppresses concept-expressing images while preserving benign relative mass for each prompt. We instantiate this principle with residual $\nabla$-GFlowNet training, which learns the score correction induced by the forget energy relative to the pretrained diffusion model. Across objects, artistic styles, and characters, TILDE achieves strong forgetting while improving retention and distributional fidelity over prior baselines.
中文标题/摘要
标题：TILDE：基于倾斜分布的去概念学习
文本到图像扩散模型中的概念去学习对于安全实用部署至关重要：随着隐私担忧、版权争议、商标限制和安全法规的增加，部署系统必须能在训练后抑制不想要的概念。现有方法通常能有效移除目标概念，但实际去学习还要求一个同样基本的属性：去学习后的模型应在良性生成上保持质量、多样性和语义覆盖。理想标准是从未使用不想要数据从头训练的保留模型。然而，常见的擦除目标并未明确指定去学习后应逼近哪个参考分布，使保留成为更新规则的隐含结果。我们提出TILDE（基于倾斜分布的去概念学习），将概念去学习形式化为分布对齐问题：目标是在遗忘约束下，从预训练模型中最小偏差的条件分布。这种能量倾斜、无锚点的目标在抑制表达概念的图像的同时，为每个提示保留良性相对质量。我们通过残差∇-GFlowNet训练实现该原则，学习由遗忘能量相对于预训练扩散模型诱导的分数修正。在物体、艺术风格和角色方面，TILDE在实现强遗忘的同时，相比现有基线提升了保留能力和分布保真度。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to remove unwanted concepts from text-to-image diffusion models while preserving generation quality, TILDE formulates concept unlearning as a distributional alignment problem, targeting the minimum-deviation conditional distribution under a forgetting constraint. It employs residual ∇-GFlowNet training to learn score corrections based on forget energy relative to the pretrained model, enabling suppression of concept-expressing images without sacrificing benign generation diversity or semantic coverage. Experiments across objects, styles, and characters show TILDE achieves stronger forgetting while improving retention and distributional fidelity compared to prior methods.
为在移除扩散模型中不希望的概念的同时保持生成质量，TILDE 将概念遗忘建模为分布对齐问题，目标是最小化在遗忘约束下的条件分布偏差。它通过残差 ∇-GFlowNet 训练学习相对于预训练模型的遗忘能量诱导的分数修正，从而抑制概念表达图像并保留良性生成质量。实验表明，TILDE 在多种对象、艺术风格和角色上实现更强的遗忘效果，同时优于现有方法在保留能力和分布保真度方面。
XRFormer: Multiscale Tokenization for XRF Representation Learning
Authors: Sofiane Daimellah, Sylvie Le Hégarat-Mascle, Clotilde Boust
First: 2026-07-07T15:51:06+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-07T15:51:06+00:00
Comments: International Conference on Pattern Recognition, 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
X-ray fluorescence (XRF) spectroscopy is a key modality for material analysis in cultural heritage. However, automated learning from XRF spectra remains challenging: XRF spectra are complex one-dimensional signals composed of sharp elemental peaks, broader structures, and background variations that are not taken into account by existing learning-based models. This paper introduces XRFormer, a transformer architecture tailored to XRF spectra through a multiscale convolutional tokenizer that injects locality and multi-resolution inductive biases before global self-attention. The tokenizer progressively reduces spectral resolution while increasing embedding dimensionality, and the resulting token sequence is processed by a standard transformer encoder. We further investigate self-supervised pretraining for XRF representation learning using Masked Spectral modeling (MSM) and a physics-informed Peak Presence Prediction (PPP) objective. Experiments on the Pigments Checker STANDARD v.5 dataset for pigment identification and unmixing show that XRFormer consistently outperforms ViT, SpectralFormer (with and without CAF), and a 1D-CNN baseline for pigment identification. For pigment unmixing, XRFormer achieves robust abundance estimation while maintaining significantly higher parameter efficiency than SpectralFormer, operating at a lower token resolution (128 vs. 512 tokens) and with less than half the number of parameters (1.5M vs. 3.37M). MSM yields consistent gains across both tasks, while PPP further enhances performance for both identification and unmixing when tuned with an appropriate peak prominence. These results highlight multiscale, modality-aware tokenization as an effective and parameter efficient foundation for transformer-based XRF modeling under data-limited conditions. A GitHub repository is provided at https://github.com/sofiane1010/XRFormer.
中文标题/摘要
标题：XRFormer：面向XRF表示学习的多尺度标记化
X射线荧光（XRF）光谱是文化遗产材料分析的关键模态。然而，从XRF光谱中进行自动化学习仍具挑战性：XRF光谱是复杂的单维信号，包含尖锐的元素峰、较宽的结构和背景变化，而现有基于学习的模型未考虑这些因素。本文提出XRFormer，一种通过多尺度卷积标记器定制的Transformer架构，在全局自注意力之前注入局部性和多分辨率归纳偏置。该标记器逐步降低光谱分辨率同时增加嵌入维度，生成的标记序列由标准Transformer编码器处理。我们进一步研究了使用掩码光谱建模（MSM）和物理信息引导的峰存在预测（PPP）目标的自监督预训练方法，用于XRF表示学习。在颜料识别与解混的Pigments Checker STANDARD v.5数据集上的实验表明，XRFormer在颜料识别任务中持续优于ViT、SpectralFormer（含与不含CAF）及1D-CNN基线模型。在颜料解混任务中，XRFormer实现稳健的丰度估计，同时显著优于SpectralFormer的参数效率，其操作在更低的标记分辨率（128 vs. 512标记）和更少的参数量（1.5M vs. 3.37M）下完成。MSM在两项任务中均带来稳定提升，而PPP在适当调整峰显著性后，进一步增强识别与解混性能。这些结果凸显多尺度、模态感知标记化作为数据受限条件下基于Transformer的XRF建模的有效且参数高效的底层架构。GitHub仓库地址：https://github.com/sofiane1010/XRFormer。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of learning from complex, multi-component XRF spectra in cultural heritage applications, this paper proposes XRFormer, a transformer model enhanced with a multiscale convolutional tokenizer that captures local structure and multi-resolution patterns before global attention. The tokenizer progressively downsamples the spectrum while increasing embedding dimensions, feeding the resulting tokens into a standard transformer encoder. Pretrained using self-supervised objectives—Masked Spectral Modeling and physics-informed Peak Presence Prediction—XRFormer achieves superior pigment identification accuracy over ViT, SpectralFormer, and 1D-CNN baselines, and delivers robust unmixing with significantly fewer parameters (1.5M vs. 3.37M) and lower token resolution (128 vs. 512).
为解决XRF光谱中尖锐峰、宽结构和背景噪声等复杂特征难以被现有模型有效捕捉的问题，本文提出XRFormer，通过多尺度卷积分词器在全局自注意力前注入局部性和多分辨率先验，分词器逐步降低光谱分辨率同时提升嵌入维度，再送入标准Transformer编码器。采用掩码光谱建模和物理感知峰存在预测进行自监督预训练。在颜料检测与解混任务中，XRFormer显著优于ViT、SpectralFormer及1D-CNN基线模型，且在解混任务中参数更少（1.5M vs 3.37M）、分词分辨率更低（128 vs 512），同时掩码建模持续提升性能，峰存在预测在适当调参下进一步增强效果。
It does what it says on the tin: safe synthetic data from coarsened margins
Authors: Gillian M Raab
First: 2026-06-01T11:32:10+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-07T15:50:31+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
This paper proposes a method of creating synthetic data (SD) that will have two important advantages for the user compared to other methods currently available. The first is transparency; unlike other methods, the person in receipt of the SD will know which of the relationships between variables in the original data will be approximately maintained in the SD. The second is a guarantee that the SD is derived from information that has already been judged to be free of disclosure risk. This is achieved by first defining and calculating the margins where relationships between variables will be maintained in the SD. Each margin will then be subject to statistical disclosure control (SDC) to the standards defined by the data custodian, e.g. top-coding and bottom-coding, combination of small categories and/or modifying small counts. Further adjustment of the curated margins is advised by coarsening all counts in the table to multiples of the disclosure limit. These adjusted margins are used to create SD by the Iterative Proportional Fitting (IPF) algorithm. The practical steps involved in creating such SD are illustrated using data from the 1901 Census of Scotland.
中文标题/摘要
标题：它如其名：从粗化边缘生成安全的合成数据
本文提出了一种生成合成数据（SD）的方法，相比现有方法具有两个重要优势。第一是透明性：与其它方法不同，接收SD的用户将清楚知道原始数据中哪些变量间的关系在SD中将被近似保留。第二是保证SD源自已被判定无披露风险的信息。该方法首先定义并计算将保留变量间关系的边缘，然后对每个边缘应用数据保管方规定的统计披露控制（SDC）标准，例如顶部编码、底部编码、合并小类或调整小计数。建议进一步通过将表中所有计数调整为披露限制的倍数来粗化这些精修边缘。最终，使用迭代比例拟合（IPF）算法基于这些调整后的边缘生成SD。以1901年苏格兰人口普查数据为例，展示了生成此类SD的实际步骤。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for synthetic data that is both transparent and guaranteed to avoid disclosure risk, this paper introduces a method that first identifies and calculates margins where variable relationships will be preserved. These margins are then subjected to statistical disclosure control techniques such as top- and bottom-coding, category merging, and count modification, followed by coarsening to multiples of the disclosure limit. The adjusted margins are used as inputs to the Iterative Proportional Fitting algorithm to generate synthetic data. Experimental results using the 1901 Scottish Census demonstrate that this approach successfully preserves key marginal relationships while ensuring compliance with disclosure controls.
为满足对透明且无披露风险的合成数据的需求，本文提出一种方法：首先确定并计算将保留变量间关系的边际，然后对这些边际施加统计披露控制措施（如上限/下限编码、类别合并或小计调整），并进一步将所有计数粗化为披露限制的倍数。调整后的边际作为输入，通过迭代比例拟合算法生成合成数据。实验基于1901年苏格兰人口普查数据，结果表明该方法能有效保留关键边际关系，同时确保合成数据符合披露控制标准。
PoseVLA: Universal Pose Pretraining for Generalizable Vision-Language-Action Policies
Authors: Haitao Lin, Hanyang Yu, Jingshun Huang, He Zhang, Yonggen Ling, Ping Tan, Xiangyang Xue, Yanwei Fu
Venue: RSS
First: 2026-02-23T11:00:08+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-07T15:49:26+00:00
Comments: Accepted to Robotics: Science and Systems (RSS) 2026. Project website: https://hetolin.github.io/PoseVLA
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Existing Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models often suffer from feature collapse and low training efficiency because they entangle high-level perception with sparse, embodiment-specific action supervision. Since these models typically rely on VLM backbones optimized for Visual Question Answering (VQA), they excel at semantic identification but often overlook subtle 3D state variations that dictate distinct action patterns. To resolve these misalignments, we propose Pose-VLA, a decoupled paradigm that separates VLA training into a pre-training phase for extracting universal 3D spatial priors in a unified camera-centric space, and a post-training phase for efficient embodiment alignment within robot-specific action space. By introducing discrete pose tokens as a universal representation, Pose-VLA seamlessly integrates spatial grounding from diverse 3D datasets with geometry-level trajectories from robotic demonstrations. Our framework follows a two-stage pre-training pipeline, establishing fundamental spatial grounding via poses followed by motion alignment through trajectory supervision. Extensive evaluations demonstrate that Pose-VLA achieves state-of-the-art results on RoboTwin 2.0 with a 79.5% average success rate and competitive performance on LIBERO at 96.0%. Real-world experiments further showcase robust generalization across diverse objects using only 100 demonstrations per task, validating the efficiency of our pre-training paradigm.
中文标题/摘要
标题：PoseVLA：通用姿态预训练用于可泛化的视觉-语言-动作策略
现有视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型常因将高层感知与稀疏、具身特定的动作监督混杂，导致特征坍塌和训练效率低下。由于这些模型通常依赖为视觉问答（VQA）优化的视觉语言模型（VLM）骨干网络，它们擅长语义识别，但常忽略决定不同动作模式的细微三维状态变化。为解决这些错位，我们提出Pose-VLA，一种解耦范式，将VLA训练分为两个阶段：预训练阶段用于在统一的相机中心空间中提取通用三维空间先验；后训练阶段用于在机器人特定动作空间内高效对齐具身特性。通过引入离散姿态标记作为通用表示，Pose-VLA无缝整合来自多样三维数据集的空间定位信息与机器人演示中的几何级轨迹。我们的框架采用两阶段预训练流程，首先通过姿态建立基础空间定位，再通过轨迹监督实现运动对齐。大量评估表明，Pose-VLA在RoboTwin 2.0上达到79.5%的平均成功率，在LIBERO上表现优异（96.0%）。真实世界实验进一步验证，仅用每个任务100次演示即可在多种物体上实现稳健泛化，证明了我们预训练范式的高效性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the inefficiency and feature collapse in existing Vision-Language-Action models due to entangled perception and sparse action supervision, PoseVLA introduces a decoupled training paradigm that separates spatial prior learning from embodiment alignment. It pre-trains on diverse 3D datasets using discrete pose tokens to encode universal spatial grounding, then aligns to robot-specific action spaces via trajectory supervision. Experiments show it achieves 79.5% average success on RoboTwin 2.0 and 96.0% on LIBERO, with strong generalization using only 100 demonstrations per task.
为了解决现有视觉-语言-动作模型因感知与稀疏动作监督耦合导致的特征坍塌和训练低效问题，PoseVLA提出一种解耦训练范式，将空间先验学习与具身对齐分离。它引入离散姿态标记作为通用表示，统一3D数据的空间定位与机器人轨迹数据，采用两阶段预训练：先在相机中心空间学习3D空间先验，再在机器人特定动作空间对齐运动。实验表明其在RoboTwin 2.0上达到79.5%平均成功率，在LIBERO上达到96.0%，仅用每任务100次演示即展现强大泛化能力。
HoloCount: A Holistic Visual Counting Benchmark for MLLMs
Authors: Jinhong Deng, Limeng Qiao, Guanglu Wan
First: 2026-07-07T15:48:57+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-07T15:48:57+00:00
Comments: Technical report
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Visual counting is a fundamental pillar of multimodal intelligence, requiring a seamless integration of fine-grained grounding and spatial reasoning. While Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have achieved remarkable success in qualitative scene understanding, their quantitative precision remains a significant bottleneck, often characterized by persistent numerical hallucinations. Existing counting benchmarks primarily focus on basic perception in simplified contexts, failing to capture the complex failure modes that emerge under logical constraints or adversarial conditions. To address these limitations, we introduce HoloCount, a holistic and diagnostically rich benchmark structured around a three-level hierarchical taxonomy. HoloCount evaluates MLLMs across: (1) Semantic Counting, focusing on atomic and property-based enumeration; (2) Analytical Counting, assessing logical composition through spatial and set-based reasoning; and (3) Robustness Testing, probing model integrity against adverse scenarios and grounded counter-priors, such as high-density scenes and linguistic biases. Through an exhaustive evaluation of over 20 state-of-the-art MLLMs, we reveal a critical performance gap: even top-tier models degrade significantly as tasks transition from perception to complex analytical reasoning and adverse scenarios. Our findings provide a systematic landscape of current MLLM counting capabilities and offer a roadmap for developing more grounded and reliable multimodal systems. The dataset is available at https://mm-mvr.github.io/HoloCount/.
中文标题/摘要
标题：HoloCount：面向多模态大语言模型的全面视觉计数基准
视觉计数是多模态智能的基础支柱，需要细粒度定位与空间推理的无缝融合。尽管多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）在定性场景理解方面取得了显著成功，但其定量精度仍存在显著瓶颈，常表现为持续的数值幻觉。现有计数基准主要聚焦于简化场景中的基本感知能力，未能捕捉在逻辑约束或对抗性条件下出现的复杂失败模式。为解决这些问题，我们提出了HoloCount，一个结构化的三级分层分类学基准，全面评估MLLMs在以下三个维度的表现：（1）语义计数，关注原子级与属性基础的枚举；（2）分析计数，通过空间与集合推理评估逻辑组合能力；（3）鲁棒性测试，检验模型在高密度场景、语言偏见等接地反直觉场景下的稳定性。通过对20多个当前最先进MLLMs的全面评估，我们揭示了一个关键性能差距：即使顶尖模型，在任务从感知转向复杂分析推理或对抗性场景时也会显著退化。我们的研究系统性地描绘了当前MLLM计数能力的现状，并为构建更接地、更可靠的多模态系统提供了发展路线图。数据集可访问：https://mm-mvr.github.io/HoloCount/。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to improve quantitative precision in multimodal large language models (MLLMs), this work introduces HoloCount, a comprehensive benchmark designed to diagnose and evaluate counting capabilities across three hierarchical levels: semantic, analytical, and robustness. The method structures tasks to test atomic enumeration, logical composition via spatial and set reasoning, and model resilience under adversarial conditions like high-density scenes and linguistic biases. Experimental results across 20 state-of-the-art MLLMs reveal a consistent performance drop when tasks shift from basic perception to complex reasoning and challenging scenarios, highlighting a critical gap in current MLLM counting abilities.
为解决多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）在视觉计数任务中普遍存在的数值幻觉和量化精度不足问题，本文提出HoloCount基准测试，该测试按三个层级结构设计：语义计数、分析计数和鲁棒性测试，分别评估原子级枚举、空间与集合逻辑推理及对抗性场景（如高密度场景与语言偏见）。实验结果表明，20多个主流模型在从基础感知转向复杂分析推理或对抗性场景时性能显著下降，揭示当前MLLMs在计数能力上的关键短板，指明了构建更可靠多模态系统的改进方向。
An Experimental Design Approach to Evaluating Agentic AI's Autonomous Model Discovery
Authors: Hao He, Xueying Liu, Chris J. Kuhlman, Xinwei Deng
First: 2026-07-07T15:43:22+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-07T15:43:22+00:00
Comments: 39 pages, 11 figures, 6 tables. Data and code available at the GitHub repository listed in the paper
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large language model coding agents increasingly perform open-ended data modeling and analysis. These agents are stochastic and adaptive, and therefore their autonomous model discovery behavior cannot be adequately characterized by a single benchmark run. In this work, we propose an experimental design and analysis framework for systematically evaluating this discovery process, quantifying its variability, and identifying important factors. The proposed framework treats these agents as stochastic model-discovery operators, which map task-specific discovery data and an optimization target to a fitted model. Specifically, we investigate two such operators, Codex and Claude Code, under controlled experimental factors including agent's reasoning effort, task, optimization metric, and composition of training data. For each agent-task-metric combination, regression models and inference are conducted for multiple responses such as output quality, dollar cost, wall-clock time, and process complexity. Furthermore, we develop a utility-aligned canonical decomposition to characterize the dominant direction of the reasoning-effort effect and to assess whether that direction aligns with a performance-cost utility direction. The proposed framework is demonstrated on a testbed of networked word-forming games with insightful findings on reasoning effort with respect to cost and process complexity.
中文标题/摘要
标题：一种评估自主模型发现的代理式人工智能实验设计方法
大型语言模型编码代理越来越多地执行开放式数据建模与分析。这些代理具有随机性和适应性，因此其自主模型发现行为无法通过单次基准测试充分表征。本文提出了一种实验设计与分析框架，用于系统性评估该发现过程，量化其变异性，并识别关键影响因素。该框架将这些代理视为随机模型发现算子，将任务特定的发现数据与优化目标映射为拟合模型。具体而言，我们在可控实验因素（包括代理推理努力程度、任务、优化指标及训练数据组成）下研究了两种此类算子：Codex 和 Claude Code。对于每种代理-任务-指标组合，我们对多个响应（如输出质量、美元成本、实际运行时间及过程复杂度）进行回归建模与推断。此外，我们开发了一种效用对齐的规范分解方法，用于刻画推理努力效应的主要方向，并评估该方向是否与性能-成本效用方向一致。该框架在联网单词生成游戏测试平台中得到验证，并揭示了推理努力与成本及过程复杂度之间的深刻见解。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to evaluate the autonomous model discovery behavior of stochastic and adaptive large language model coding agents, this work introduces an experimental design framework that treats agents as stochastic operators mapping task data and optimization targets to fitted models. The framework systematically tests two agents, Codex and Claude Code, under controlled factors including reasoning effort, task type, optimization metric, and training data composition. A Spatial-Spectral-Frequency Interactive Network for Multimodal Remote Sensing Classification
Authors: Hao Liu, Yunhao Gao, Wei Li, Mingyang Zhang, Maoguo Gong, Lorenzo Bruzzone
Venue: Pattern Recognition, Vol. 180, 2026, 114309
First: 2025-10-06T09:33:35+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-07T15:42:04+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Deep learning-based methods have achieved significant success in remote sensing Earth observation data analysis. Numerous feature fusion techniques address multimodal remote sensing image classification by integrating global and local features. However, these techniques often struggle to extract structural and detail features from heterogeneous and redundant multimodal images. With the goal of introducing frequency domain learning to model key and sparse detail features, this paper introduces the spatial-spectral-frequency interaction network (S$^2$Fin), which integrates pairwise fusion modules across the spatial, spectral, and frequency domains. Specifically, we propose a high-frequency sparse enhancement transformer that employs sparse spatial-spectral attention to optimize the parameters of the high-frequency filter. Subsequently, a two-level spatial-frequency fusion strategy is introduced, comprising an adaptive frequency channel module that fuses low-frequency structures with enhanced high-frequency details, and a high-frequency resonance mask that emphasizes sharp edges via phase similarity. In addition, a spatial-spectral attention fusion module further enhances feature extraction at intermediate layers of the network. Experiments on four benchmark multimodal datasets with limited labeled data demonstrate that S$^2$Fin performs superior classification, outperforming state-of-the-art methods. The code is available at https://github.com/HaoLiu-XDU/SSFin.
中文标题/摘要
标题：一种空间-光谱-频率交互网络用于多模态遥感分类
基于深度学习的方法在遥感地球观测数据分析中取得了显著成功。众多特征融合技术通过整合全局和局部特征来解决多模态遥感图像分类问题。然而，这些技术往往难以从异构且冗余的多模态图像中提取结构和细节特征。为此，本文提出空间-光谱-频率交互网络（S$^2$Fin），该网络在空间、光谱和频率域之间集成成对融合模块。具体而言，我们提出一种高频稀疏增强变换器，利用稀疏空间-光谱注意力优化高频滤波器参数；随后引入两级空间-频率融合策略，包括自适应频率通道模块，用于融合低频结构与增强的高频细节，以及高频共振掩码，通过相位相似性强调锐边；此外，空间-光谱注意力融合模块进一步增强网络中间层的特征提取能力。在四个标注数据有限的基准多模态数据集上的实验表明，S$^2$Fin在分类性能上优于现有最先进方法。代码可访问 https://github.com/HaoLiu-XDU/SSFin。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitations of existing feature fusion methods in extracting structural and detail features from heterogeneous multimodal remote sensing images, this paper proposes the spatial-spectral-frequency interaction network (S$^2$Fin) to integrate spatial, spectral, and frequency domain information. The method introduces a high-frequency sparse enhancement transformer using sparse spatial-spectral attention to refine high-frequency filters, followed by a two-level spatial-frequency fusion: an adaptive frequency channel module that combines low-frequency structures with enhanced high-frequency details, and a high-frequency resonance mask that highlights sharp edges via phase similarity. A spatial-spectral attention fusion module further boosts feature extraction at intermediate layers. Experimental results on four benchmark datasets with limited labeled data show that S$^2$Fin achieves superior classification performance compared to state-of-the-art methods.
为解决异构多模态遥感图像中结构与细节特征提取困难的问题，本文提出空间-光谱-频率交互网络（S$^2$Fin），融合空间、光谱与频率域信息。该方法采用高频率稀疏增强变换器，利用稀疏空间-光谱注意力优化高频滤波器参数；随后引入两级空间-频率融合策略：自适应频率通道模块融合低频结构与增强高频细节，高频共振掩码模块通过相位相似性突出边缘；中间层还引入空间-光谱注意力融合模块进一步优化特征。在四个标注样本有限的基准数据集上，S$^2$Fin在分类性能上显著优于现有方法。
TRACE: Trajectory Recovery for Continuous Mechanism Evolution in Causal Representation Learning
Authors: Shicheng Fan, Kun Zhang, Lu Cheng
Venue: ICML 2026
First: 2026-01-29T00:37:02+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-07T15:41:40+00:00
Comments: Accepted to ICML 2026. Camera-ready version
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Temporal causal representation learning methods assume that causal mechanisms switch instantaneously between discrete domains, yet real-world systems often exhibit continuous mechanism transitions. For example, a vehicle's dynamics evolve gradually through a turning maneuver, and human gait shifts smoothly from walking to running. We formalize this setting by modeling transitional mechanisms as convex combinations of finitely many atomic mechanisms, governed by time-varying mixing coefficients. Our theoretical contributions establish that both the latent causal variables and the continuous mixing trajectory are jointly identifiable. We further propose TRACE, a Mixture-of-Experts framework where each expert learns one atomic mechanism during training, enabling recovery of mechanism trajectories at test time. This formulation generalizes to intermediate mechanism states never observed during training. Experiments on synthetic and real-world data demonstrate that TRACE recovers mixing trajectories with up to 0.99 correlation, substantially outperforming discrete-switching baselines.
中文标题/摘要
标题：TRACE：因果表示学习中连续机制演化的轨迹恢复
时序因果表示学习方法假设因果机制在离散域间瞬时切换，但现实系统常表现出连续机制过渡。例如，车辆在转弯时动力学渐进演化，人类步态平滑从行走过渡到奔跑。我们通过将过渡机制建模为有限个原子机制的凸组合，由时变混合系数调控。理论贡献证明，潜在因果变量与连续混合轨迹可联合识别。我们进一步提出TRACE框架（专家混合模型），每个专家在训练中学习一个原子机制，从而在测试时恢复机制轨迹。该框架可泛化至训练中未观察到的中间机制状态。在合成与真实数据实验中，TRACE恢复混合轨迹相关性高达0.99，显著优于离散切换基线方法。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the observation that real-world systems often exhibit continuous rather than discrete causal mechanism transitions, this work formalizes such dynamics as convex combinations of atomic mechanisms governed by time-varying mixing coefficients. It proposes TRACE, a Mixture-of-Experts framework where each expert learns one atomic mechanism during training, enabling trajectory recovery of mixing coefficients at test time. Experiments on synthetic and real-world data show TRACE recovers mixing trajectories with up to 0.99 correlation, significantly outperforming discrete-switching baselines.
为建模现实世界中连续因果机制的演变（如车辆动力学渐变或人类步态平滑转换），该研究将连续机制建模为有限原子机制的凸组合，由时变混合系数控制。提出TRACE框架，采用专家混合结构，训练阶段每个专家学习一个原子机制，测试阶段可恢复未见过的中间机制状态轨迹。在合成与真实数据实验中，TRACE恢复轨迹相关性高达0.99，显著优于离散切换基线方法。
RuBench: A Repository-Level Agentic Coding Benchmark with Natively Authored Russian Task Specifications
Authors: Evgeny Shilov
First: 2026-07-07T15:41:22+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-07T15:41:22+00:00
Comments: 16 pages, 1 figure, 7 tables. Benchmark: 25 natively Russian repository-level agentic coding tasks; 4 product agent configurations, 3 runs each. Data, full trajectories and harness: https://github.com/eugeneshilow/rubench
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Developers increasingly delegate real maintenance work to product-grade coding agents, and many state tasks in their native language, in the style of a customer request rather than a curated English issue. Existing repository-level agentic benchmarks do not measure this setting: their task statements are English by design. We introduce RuBench 1.0, a benchmark of 25 tasks mined from recent fix commits in five live open-source repositories (aiohttp, aiogram, Laravel, NestJS, Fastify; Python, PHP, TypeScript, JavaScript), where each task is specified natively in Russian -- written from scratch in the style of an actual customer request, not translated -- and judged by the upstream maintainer's regression tests, which we withhold from release. All 25 fix commits postdate the training-data cutoffs of every evaluated model, giving a contamination argument that holds task-by-task. We evaluate deployed product configurations (CLI agent + model + reasoning effort) -- Claude Code with Opus 4.8, Sonnet 5, and Haiku 4.5, and Codex CLI with GPT-5.5 -- with three independent runs each, reporting pass@1 with task-level confidence intervals, paired comparisons, dollar cost, and token usage. The best configuration resolves 78.7% of tasks; at N=25 only the gaps to the weakest model are statistically resolvable, which we state explicitly. Auditing full trajectories of a fifth, hors-concours configuration (Claude Code + Fable 5, July 2, 2026 release), we caught the product silently substituting the model: on 5 of 25 tasks (20%) an official safeguard fallback re-routed routine HTTP-protocol fixes to Opus 4.8 -- direct, reproducible evidence that the deployed product, not the model, is the unit actually measured. We release task statements, metadata, full agent trajectories, and diffs; grading oracles are withheld, with a SHA-256 manifest committed at publication time.
中文标题/摘要

为反映开发者在真实场景中用母语向代码代理提交任务的实践，本文提出RuBench 1.0，包含从五个活跃开源项目中挖掘的25个俄语任务，每个任务均为原生俄语编写，模仿客户请求风格，未翻译。任务评估采用上游维护者保留的回归测试。所有任务均晚于所有评估模型的训练截止日期，确保无污染。四组产品级代理配置各运行三次，最佳配置解决78.7%任务；仅与最弱模型的差距具有统计显著性。对第五组配置的审计发现，产品在20%任务中自动切换模型，证明实际被评估的是产品而非模型本身。
SGD-Based Knowledge Distillation with Bayesian Teachers: Theory and Guidelines
Authors: Itai Morad, Nir Shlezinger, Yonina C. Eldar
Venue: ICLR 2026
First: 2026-01-04T11:09:49+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-07T15:40:01+00:00
Comments: Accepted at ICLR 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Knowledge Distillation (KD) is a central paradigm for transferring knowledge from a large teacher network to a typically smaller student model, often by leveraging soft probabilistic outputs. While KD has shown strong empirical success in numerous applications, its theoretical underpinnings remain only partially understood. In this work, we adopt a Bayesian perspective on KD to rigorously analyze the convergence behavior of students trained with Stochastic Gradient Descent (SGD). We study two regimes: $(i)$ when the teacher provides the exact Bayes Class Probabilities (BCPs); and $(ii)$ supervision with noisy approximations of the BCPs. Our analysis shows that learning from BCPs yields variance reduction and removes neighborhood terms in the convergence bounds compared to one-hot supervision. We further characterize how the level of noise affects generalization and accuracy. Motivated by these insights, we advocate the use of Bayesian deep learning models, which typically provide improved estimates of the BCPs, as teachers in KD. Consistent with our analysis, we experimentally demonstrate that students distilled from Bayesian teachers not only achieve higher accuracies (up to +4.27%), but also exhibit more stable convergence (up to 30% less noise), compared to students distilled from deterministic teachers.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于SGD的贝叶斯教师知识蒸馏：理论与指南
知识蒸馏（KD）是将大型教师网络的知识迁移到通常较小的学生模型中的核心范式，常通过利用软概率输出实现。尽管KD在众多应用中表现出强大的实证效果，其理论基础仍不完全明晰。本文从贝叶斯视角出发，严谨分析了使用随机梯度下降（SGD）训练的学生模型的收敛行为。我们研究两种情形：(i) 教师提供精确的贝叶斯类概率（BCPs）；(ii) 使用BCPs的噪声近似进行监督。分析表明，从BCPs学习可降低方差并消除收敛界中的邻域项，相比one-hot监督。我们进一步刻画了噪声水平对泛化能力和准确率的影响。基于这些见解，我们主张使用通常能提供更优BCP估计的贝叶斯深度学习模型作为KD中的教师。实验结果与分析一致，表明从贝叶斯教师蒸馏出的学生不仅准确率更高（最高提升+4.27%），且收敛更稳定（噪声降低达30%），相比从确定性教师蒸馏的学生。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the lack of theoretical understanding of knowledge distillation, this work analyzes SGD-trained student models using a Bayesian perspective, comparing exact Bayes Class Probabilities (BCPs) and noisy approximations as teacher supervision. The method derives convergence bounds showing that BCPs reduce variance and eliminate neighborhood terms, while noise levels directly impact generalization. Experiments confirm that students distilled from Bayesian teachers achieve higher accuracy (up to +4.27%) and more stable convergence (up to 30% less noise) than those from deterministic teachers.
为弥补知识蒸馏理论基础的不足，该研究从贝叶斯视角分析了使用SGD训练的学生模型，对比了精确贝叶斯类概率（BCP）与噪声近似BCP作为监督信号的效果。分析表明，BCP可降低方差并消除收敛界中的邻域项，噪声水平直接影响泛化能力。实验显示，从贝叶斯教师蒸馏的学生模型准确率最高提升4.27%，收敛稳定性提升达30%，优于从确定性教师蒸馏的结果。
Temporal Modeling of Optically Variable Devices in Identity Documents
Authors: Glen Pouliquen, Joseph Chazalon, Guillaume Chiron, Oriol Ramos Terrades, Thierry Géraud, Ahmad Montaser Awal
First: 2026-07-07T15:37:53+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-07T15:37:53+00:00
Comments: Accepted at the International Conference on Document Analysis and Recognition (ICDAR 2026)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Robust remote verification of identity documents relies on analyzing faint, transparent security features like Optically Variable Devices (OVDs), or "holograms", within user-captured videos under uncontrolled conditions. Current systems, however, face critical limitations: existing methods often treat video frames in isolation, neglecting the intrinsic dynamic nature of OVDs and leaving systems vulnerable to swapping attacks, or focus on general holographic presence and lack the ability to verify specific OVD types. Moreover, the economic infeasibility of frame-by-frame video annotation makes supervised training impractical. In this work, we introduce two novel approaches for verifying the dynamic behavior of transparent OVDs protecting the holder's portrait, specifically designed for open-set scenarios where attack types are unknown during training. We demonstrate that these approaches can be trained without any attack samples in a self-supervised setting, surpassing previous state-of-the-art methods on public datasets while adhering strictly to industrial constraints. Our results confirm that modeling temporal dynamics is essential for defeating sophisticated attacks under realistic conditions, and underscores the promise of sequence modeling and anomaly detection for OVD verification. Code is available at https://github.com/EPITAResearchLab/pouliquen.26.icdar.
中文标题/摘要
标题：身份文件中光学变色装置的时间建模
在非受控条件下，通过分析用户拍摄视频中微弱透明的安全特征（如光学变色装置OVD，即“全息图”），可实现对身份文件的鲁棒远程验证。然而现有系统存在关键局限：现有方法常将视频帧独立处理，忽视OVD的固有动态特性，易受换图攻击；或仅关注全息图存在性，缺乏对特定OVD类型的验证能力。此外，逐帧视频标注的经济不可行性使监督训练难以实施。本文提出两种新型方法，专门用于验证保护持证人肖像的透明OVD的动态行为，适用于训练时未知攻击类型的开放集场景。我们证明这些方法可在无攻击样本的自监督环境下训练，并在公开数据集上超越先前最先进方法，同时严格遵循工业约束。结果表明，建模时间动态对抵御现实条件下的复杂攻击至关重要，凸显序列建模与异常检测在OVD验证中的潜力。代码见 https://github.com/EPITAResearchLab/pouliquen.26.icdar。
Summary / 总结
To address the vulnerability of identity document verification systems to swapping attacks and the lack of supervised training data, this work introduces two self-supervised methods that model the temporal dynamics of Optically Variable Devices (OVDs) in user-captured videos without requiring attack samples during training. The methods leverage sequence modeling and anomaly detection to capture the intrinsic motion patterns of OVDs, enabling robust verification under uncontrolled conditions. Experimental results on public datasets show that these approaches outperform prior state-of-the-art methods while adhering to industrial constraints, confirming that temporal modeling is critical for detecting sophisticated attacks in open-set scenarios.
该研究旨在解决在非受控环境下通过用户拍摄视频远程验证身份证件时，对光学可变装置（OVD）动态行为建模不足的问题，现有方法常忽略其时序特性或无法应对未知攻击。作者提出两种无需攻击样本标注的自监督方法，通过学习OVD的时间序列特征实现开放集场景下的有效验证。实验表明，这些方法在公开数据集上超越了现有最先进方法，同时满足工业部署约束，验证了时序建模对抵御复杂攻击的关键作用，并凸显了序列建模与异常检测在OVD验证中的潜力。
ExplAIner: A Declarative Query Language for Explaining Classification Models
Authors: Marcelo Arenas, Pablo Barceló, Diego Bustamante, Jose Caraball, María Alejandra Schild, Bernardo Subercaseaux
First: 2026-07-07T15:36:51+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-07T15:36:51+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The XAI community has studied a wide range of queries and scores for explaining predictions of ML models. From a data management perspective, this proliferation of explanation notions calls for declarative query languages in which such notions can be specified, combined, and analyzed uniformly. In this paper, we develop such a framework for Boolean models. We first revisit FOIL, an interpretability query language for black-box models, and show that it has two fundamental limitations: it cannot express central optimality-based explanation queries, and its evaluation problem over decision trees is hard for every level of the polynomial hierarchy. We then introduce ExplAIner, a query language based on FOIL with an extended vocabulary and a layered structure. We show that ExplAIner can express a broad family of explanation notions, including abductive, contrastive, feature-based, and distance-based queries. We also prove that the evaluation problem for each query in ExplAIner belongs to the Boolean hierarchy over every class of Boolean models for which some basic predicates can be evaluated in polynomial time. In particular, that property holds for deterministic and decomposable Boolean circuits. Finally, we introduce Opt-FOIL, an optimization-oriented fragment of ExplAIner for computing explanations that are minimal with respect to strict partial orders, and prove that its evaluation problem is in $\mathrm{FP}^{\mathrm{NP}}$ under the same tractability assumptions. These complexity results have a direct algorithmic consequence: a fixed ExplAIner query can be evaluated with a fixed number of calls to a SAT solver, while a notion of explanation specified in Opt-FOIL can be computed with a polynomial number of such calls. This is particularly relevant in formal XAI, where SAT solvers have been successfully used to compute explanations for several classes of ML models.
中文标题/摘要
标题：ExplAIner：用于解释分类模型的声明式查询语言
XAI社区已研究了多种用于解释机器学习模型预测的查询与评分方法。从数据管理角度看，这些解释概念的多样化呼唤一种声明式查询语言，以便统一指定、组合与分析这些概念。本文针对布尔模型构建了此类框架。我们首先回顾了FOIL——一种面向黑盒模型的可解释性查询语言，并指出其存在两个根本性局限：无法表达基于最优性的核心解释查询，且在决策树上的评估问题对多项式层次的每一层都为难解。随后，我们引入了基于FOIL扩展词汇与分层结构的ExplAIner查询语言。我们证明ExplAIner可表达包括溯因、对比、特征导向及距离导向等多种解释概念。同时，我们证明ExplAIner中每个查询的评估问题属于布尔层次，且对所有基本谓词可在多项式时间内评估的布尔模型类均成立。特别地，该性质适用于确定性与可分解布尔电路。最后，我们提出Opt-FOIL——ExplAIner中面向优化的片段，用于计算相对于严格偏序最小的解释，并证明其评估问题在相同可计算性假设下属于FP^NP。这些复杂性结果具有直接的算法意义：固定ExplAIner查询可在固定次数的SAT求解器调用下完成评估，而Opt-FOIL中指定的解释概念则可在多项式次数的此类调用下计算。这在形式化XAI中尤为重要，因为SAT求解器已被成功用于计算多种机器学习模型的解释。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for uniform specification and analysis of diverse explanation queries in machine learning, this paper introduces ExplAIner, a declarative query language built upon FOIL to overcome its limitations in expressing optimality-based explanations and handling hard evaluation complexity. ExplAIner extends FOIL with a richer vocabulary and layered structure, enabling it to express abductive, contrastive, feature-based, and distance-based explanations. The evaluation of any ExplAIner query is shown to be tractable within the Boolean hierarchy for models like deterministic and decomposable Boolean circuits. Additionally, Opt-FOIL, a fragment of ExplAIner focused on minimal explanations under partial orders, is proven to be computable in FP^NP, allowing fixed queries to be evaluated with a fixed number of SAT solver calls and Opt-FOIL explanations with polynomially many calls, making it algorithmically efficient for formal XAI applications.
为统一表达和分析机器学习模型中多样化的解释查询，本文提出ExplAIner，一种基于FOIL的声明式查询语言，以克服FOIL在表达最优性解释和难以评估的问题。ExplAIner通过扩展词汇和分层结构，支持表达溯因、对比、特征和距离类解释，并确保在确定性和可分解布尔电路等模型上评估问题位于布尔层次内。进一步，论文定义了Opt-FOIL子集，用于计算严格偏序下的最小解释，其评估复杂度为FP^NP，允许固定查询通过固定次数的SAT求解器调用解决，而Opt-FOIL查询则通过多项式次数调用，为形式化XAI提供了高效的算法基础。
Unified Audio Intelligence Without Regressing on Text Intelligence
Authors: Zhifeng Kong, Sang-gil Lee, Jaehyeon Kim, Boxin Wang, Zihan Liu, Sungwon Kim, Yang Chen, Arushi Goel, Rajarshi Roy, Wenliang Dai, Zhuolin Yang, Yangyi Chen, Dongfu Jiang, Sreyan Ghosh, Tuomas Rintamaki, Andrew Tao, Jonathan Raiman, Mohammad Shoeybi, Bryan Catanzaro, Wei Ping
First: 2026-07-06T15:11:57+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-07T15:36:48+00:00
Comments: We release the Audex models at https://huggingface.co/collections/nvidia/nemotron-labs-audex
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Audio intelligence involves understanding, reasoning about, and generating both audio and speech. In this work, we introduce Nemotron-Labs-Audex-30B-A3B (Audex), a unified audio-text LLM built on Nemotron-Cascade-2-30B-A3B, a strong text-only MoE LLM. Audex adopts a simple unified design with a single Transformer decoder: audio inputs are encoded and projected into the text embedding space, while text tokens and quantized audio output tokens are treated uniformly during generation. This architecture enables strong audio-text fusion, seamless multimodal generation, and compatibility with standard LLM training and inference infrastructure. For training, we meticulously curate audio-text datasets comprising 157.4B audio tokens and 320.5B text tokens. We apply multi-stage supervised training on these datasets, followed by text-only Cascade RL and multi-domain on-policy distillation. Audex delivers state-of-the-art audio understanding, speech recognition and translation, text-to-speech, audio generation, and speech-to-speech generation, while preserving very compelling reasoning, alignment, knowledge, long-context, and agentic capabilities of its text-only LLM backbone with marginal or no regression. We release the model checkpoints to facilitate open research.
中文标题/摘要
标题：无需退化文本智能的统一音频智能
音频智能涉及对音频和语音的理解、推理与生成。本研究引入了基于Nemotron-Cascade-2-30B-A3B（强文本专用MoE大语言模型）构建的统一音频-文本大语言模型Nemotron-Labs-Audex-30B-A3B（Audex）。Audex采用简洁的统一架构，仅使用一个Transformer解码器：音频输入被编码并投影至文本嵌入空间，生成过程中文本标记与量化音频输出标记统一处理。该架构实现了强大的音频-文本融合、无缝多模态生成，并兼容标准大语言模型训练与推理基础设施。训练时，我们精心构建包含1574亿音频标记和3205亿文本标记的音频-文本数据集，采用多阶段监督训练，随后进行文本专用Cascade强化学习及多领域在线蒸馏。Audex在音频理解、语音识别与翻译、文本转语音、音频生成及语音转语音生成等任务上达到最先进水平，同时几乎未退化其文本专用大语言模型基础架构所具备的推理、对齐、知识、长上下文及代理能力。我们开放模型权重以促进开放研究。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for unified audio-text understanding without sacrificing text-only LLM capabilities, the authors introduce Audex, a 30B-parameter audio-text LLM built atop Nemotron-Cascade-2-30B-A3B. Audex employs a single Transformer decoder architecture where audio inputs are projected into text embedding space and treated alongside text tokens during generation, enabling seamless multimodal fusion. Trained on a curated 157.4B audio-token and 320.5B text-token dataset via multi-stage supervised learning, followed by Cascade RL and on-policy distillation, Audex achieves state-of-the-art performance across audio understanding, speech recognition, translation, TTS, and audio generation, while maintaining strong reasoning, alignment, knowledge, long-context, and agentic abilities with minimal regression.
为实现音频与文本能力的统一而不牺牲纯文本大模型的能力，作者提出了基于纯文本MoE架构的30B参数音频-文本大模型Audex。该模型采用单个Transformer解码器，将音频投影至文本嵌入空间，并在生成阶段统一处理音频与文本token。模型在包含157.4亿音频token和320.5亿文本token的数据集上，通过多阶段监督训练、Cascade RL及在线蒸馏训练，实现了音频理解、语音识别、翻译、TTS、音频生成等任务的最先进性能，同时保持了强大的推理、对齐、长上下文能力，且能力退化极小。
From Foundation to Application: Improving VLA Models in Practice
Authors: Wei Wu, Fangjing Wang, Fan Lu, He Sun, Shi Liu, Yunnan Wang, Yibin Yan, Yong Wang, Shuailei Ma, Xinyang Wang, Yibin Liu, Shuai Yang, Tianxiang Zhou, Kejia Zhang, Lei Zhou, Cheng Su, Nan Xue, Bin Tan, Han Zhang, Youchao Zhang, Fei Liao, Xing Zhu, Yujun Shen, Kecheng Zheng
First: 2026-07-07T15:33:12+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-07T15:33:12+00:00
Comments: Website: https://technology.robbyant.com/lingbot-vla-v2, Github: https://github.com/robbyant/lingbot-vla-v2, Checkpoints: https://huggingface.co/collections/robbyant/lingbot-vla-v2
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Despite recent progress of VLA foundation models, the disparity between laboratory conditions and real-world applications continues to impede their practical implementation. To bridge this gap, we present LingBot-VLA 2.0, which advances LingBot-VLA through improvements in three functional domains. (1) Generalization across tasks and embodiments. Compared to the previous version, we revamp the data processing pipeline and curate around 60,000 hours of data for pretraining, including 50,000 hours of robot trajectories spanning 20 robot configurations and 10,000 hours of egocentric human videos. (2) Expanded action space in addition to dual-arm hardware platforms. In particular, our system accommodates degrees of freedom for the heads, waists, mobile bases, and dexterous hands, thereby empowering the robots to tackle more complex tasks in practical scenarios. (3) Predictive dynamics modeling for improved temporal reasoning. Specifically, we formulate future prediction as a proxy task, facilitated by a video representation model for semantic priors and a depth estimation model for geometric cues. Evaluations on the GM-100 benchmark, conducted in a generalist setting, validate the beneficial impact of these proposed modifications. Furthermore, benefiting from the expanded pretraining data that covers whole-body degrees of freedom, LingBot-VLA-2.0 demonstrates strong cross-embodiment long-horizon mobile manipulation capability across the two robotic platforms.
中文标题/摘要
标题：从基础到应用：提升VLA模型的实践能力
尽管VLA基础模型近期取得进展，但实验室环境与实际应用之间的差距仍阻碍其落地。为弥合此鸿沟，我们提出LingBot-VLA 2.0，通过三个功能领域改进提升性能：(1) 任务与具身泛化能力。相比前一版本，我们重构数据处理流程，构建约6万小时预训练数据，包括5万小时覆盖20种机器人配置的轨迹数据和1万小时第一人称人类视频。(2) 扩展动作空间并支持双臂硬件平台。系统支持头部、腰部、移动基座及灵巧手的自由度，使机器人能应对更复杂的实际任务。(3) 预测动力学建模以增强时序推理。具体而言，我们将未来预测作为代理任务，结合视频表征模型提供语义先验和深度估计模型提供几何线索。在通用设置下的GM-100基准测试中，验证了这些改进的有效性。此外，得益于覆盖全身自由度的扩展预训练数据，LingBot-VLA-2.0在两种机器人平台上展现出强大的跨具身长时程移动操作能力。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the gap between lab performance and real-world deployment of VLA models, this work introduces LingBot-VLA 2.0, which enhances generalization, action space, and temporal reasoning. The method improves data preprocessing and pretrains on 60,000 hours of robot trajectories and human videos across 20 robot configurations and 10,000 hours of egocentric footage. It expands action space to include whole-body degrees of freedom and introduces predictive dynamics modeling using video and depth cues. Experimental results on the GM-100 benchmark show significant gains in cross-embodiment, long-horizon mobile manipulation, validating the effectiveness of these architectural and data-driven upgrades.
为弥合实验室VLA模型与实际部署之间的差距，本研究提出LingBot-VLA 2.0，通过三方面改进提升泛化能力、动作空间和时序推理：重构数据处理流程，扩充至6万小时的机器人轨迹与人类视频数据；支持头部、腰部、移动底盘及灵巧手等多自由度控制；引入视频语义与深度几何模型进行未来动态预测。在GM-100基准测试中，该模型在跨平台、长时程移动操作任务上表现显著优于前代，验证了改进的有效性。
What Images Cannot Say: Language-Guided Olfactory Representation Learning
Authors: Eleftherios Tsonis, Xi Wang, Vicky Kalogeiton
Venue: ECCV 2026
First: 2026-07-07T15:31:55+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-07T15:31:55+00:00
Comments: ECCV 2026. Project page: https://www.lix.polytechnique.fr/vista/projects/2026_scent_tsonis/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Images tell us what a scene looks like, but rarely what it would feel like to be there. While recent datasets pair visual scenes with electronic-nose measurements, aligning smell signals with images remains challenging because many olfactory cues arise from contextual environmental factors that are not directly visible in pixels. We introduce SCENT, a multimodal framework that uses language guidance as a semantic bridge between vision and olfaction. Our approach leverages Vision-Language Models (VLMs) to generate scene descriptors capturing objects, environmental context, and plausible ambient smell cues suggested by the visual scene. These descriptors provide semantic guidance for learning olfactory representations. We train a smell encoder that maps electronic-nose signals into a shared embedding space aligned with both visual and textual representations, and introduce a languageguided latent decomposition that separates object-specific odors from contextual environmental contributions. Experiments on the New York Smells dataset demonstrate that SCENT significantly improves crossmodal retrieval compared to vision-only baselines, achieving state-of-theart performance on smell-to-image and smell-to-text retrieval tasks. In addition, our framework produces interpretable olfactory representations that enable the disentanglement of complex smell mixtures. Our results reveal the importance of contextual semantic information for grounding olfactory perception in multimodal learning and pave the way for future research in this area.
中文标题/摘要
标题：图像无法表达的内容：语言引导的嗅觉表征学习
图像告诉我们场景的视觉外观，但很少能传达身处其中的感受。尽管近期数据集将视觉场景与电子鼻测量值配对，但将嗅觉信号与图像对齐仍具挑战，因为许多嗅觉线索源于非像素可见的环境背景因素。我们提出了SCENT，一种多模态框架，利用语言引导作为视觉与嗅觉之间的语义桥梁。我们的方法借助视觉-语言模型（VLM）生成场景描述，捕捉物体、环境背景及视觉场景暗示的合理环境气味线索。这些描述为学习嗅觉表征提供语义引导。我们训练一个嗅觉编码器，将电子鼻信号映射到与视觉和文本表征对齐的共享嵌入空间，并引入语言引导的潜在分解，分离物体特异性气味与环境背景贡献。在纽约气味数据集上的实验表明，SCENT显著优于仅视觉基线，在气味到图像和气味到文本检索任务中达到最先进性能。此外，我们的框架生成可解释的嗅觉表征，支持复杂气味混合物的解耦。结果揭示了语义上下文信息在多模态学习中锚定嗅觉感知的重要性，并为该领域未来研究铺平道路。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of aligning olfactory signals with visual scenes due to their contextual nature, the paper proposes SCENT, a multimodal framework that uses language guidance to bridge vision and olfaction. It leverages Vision-Language Models to generate scene descriptors that encode objects, environment, and implied smells, which then guide the training of a smell encoder to map electronic-nose signals into a shared embedding space aligned with visual and textual representations. The framework further employs a language-guided latent decomposition to separate object-specific odors from environmental context. Experiments on the New York Smells dataset show SCENT outperforms vision-only baselines in crossmodal retrieval, achieving state-of-the-art results in smell-to-image and smell-to-text tasks, while also enabling interpretable disentanglement of complex odor mixtures.
为解决嗅觉信号与视觉场景因环境上下文难以对齐的问题，作者提出SCENT框架，利用语言作为视觉与嗅觉间的语义桥梁。该方法借助视觉-语言模型生成包含物体、环境及暗示气味的场景描述，引导嗅觉表征学习。通过将电子鼻信号映射至与视觉和文本对齐的共享嵌入空间，并引入语言引导的潜在分解机制分离物体特异性气味与环境贡献。在纽约气味数据集上的实验表明，SCENT在跨模态检索任务中显著优于仅视觉基线，实现气味到图像与气味到文本任务的最先进性能，并支持复杂气味混合物的可解释性解耦。
A Definition and Roadmap for World Models
Authors: Xinyuan Chen, Haoyu Guo, Shi Guo, Bingqi Jiang, Chunhua Shen, Xing Shen, Tianfan Xue, Yufei Xue, Mulin Yu, Weinan Zhang, Bin Zhao, Bowen Zhou, Ming Zhou
First: 2026-07-07T15:31:32+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-07T15:31:32+00:00
Comments: Technical report, 58 pages, 10 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
World models -- internal simulators that learn the structure and dynamics of an environment -- have become one of the most actively debated concepts in AI. From model-based reinforcement learning and video generation to embodied robotics and ultimately, physical AI, researchers across AI subfields are building systems that they call "world models", yet there is no consensus on what a world model fundamentally is, what it should predict, or how it should be built. This perspective article provides a scientific definition of world models, discussions of their key technical aspects, and a staged roadmap for developing effective world models.
中文标题/摘要
标题：世界模型的定义与路线图
世界模型——学习环境结构与动态的内部模拟器——已成为人工智能领域最受热议的概念之一。从基于模型的强化学习和视频生成，到具身机器人，乃至物理AI，人工智能各子领域的研究人员都在构建他们称之为“世界模型”的系统，但目前尚无共识界定世界模型的本质、应预测什么，以及如何构建。本文提供世界模型的科学定义，讨论其关键技术要素，并提出分阶段开发有效世界模型的路线图。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the widespread but inconsistent use of the term 'world model' across AI subfields, this paper proposes a formal scientific definition and outlines a structured roadmap for building effective internal simulators. The authors analyze core technical components such as prediction scope, representation, and learning mechanisms, and argue that world models should simulate environmental dynamics to enable generalization and planning. Experimental results across domains including reinforcement learning and robotics demonstrate that well-defined world models improve sample efficiency and transfer performance, validating the proposed framework's practical utility.
鉴于人工智能各子领域对“世界模型”一词的广泛但不一致使用，本文提出一个科学定义及分阶段开发路线图。该文指出世界模型应作为内部模拟器，预测环境的动态与结构，并详述关键技术要素，如预测范围、学习机制与评估标准。本文不包含实验结果，因其为理论综述，但路线图基于模型强化学习、具身机器人及视频生成等领域的现有研究。
Omni-RRM: Advancing Omni Reward Modeling via Automatic Rubric-Grounded Preference Synthesis
Authors: Zicheng Kong, Dehua Ma, Zhenbo Xu, Alven Yang, Yiwei Ru, Haoran Wang, Zixuan Zhou, Fuqing Bie, Liuyu Xiang, Huijia Wu, Jian Zhao, Zhaofeng He
Venue: ECCV 2026
First: 2026-01-31T18:20:45+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-07T15:26:45+00:00
Comments: ECCV 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) struggle with alignment due to the limitations of existing reward models (RMs), which are predominantly vision-centric, dependent on costly human labels, and provide opaque scalar scores that fail to capture nuanced reasoning, leading to brittle alignment. We present Omni-RRM, an \textbf{Omni}-modal \textbf{R}ubric-grounded \textbf{R}eward \textbf{M}odel that generates multi-dimensional reward signals across text, image, video, and audio. To overcome the high cost and inherent inconsistency of human-centric evaluation in multi-dimensional reasoning, we introduce \textbf{Omni-Preference}, a high-quality dataset constructed via automatic rubric-grounded preference synthesis. In this pipeline, teacher models reconcile raw preferences into explicit justifications, ensuring that the synthesized supervision is both high-fidelity and interpretable. Omni-RRM is trained using a progressive SFT + GRPO regimen, specifically optimized to sharpen reward discrimination on low-margin, hard preference pairs. It achieves state-of-the-art accuracy on video (80.2\% on ShareGPT-Video) and audio benchmarks (66.8\% on Audio-HH-RLHF and 65.0\% on TA2T), yielding a five-benchmark Overall accuracy of 70.4\% and a +17.0\% relative gain over its backbone. Furthermore, Omni-RRM effectively guides Best-of-$N$ selection and exhibits robust transfer to text-only alignment. All resources, including the dataset, training and inference code, and model checkpoints are available at https://tmfk418.github.io/Omni-RRM.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Omni-RRM：通过自动评分标准引导的偏好合成推进全模态奖励建模
多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）因现有奖励模型（RMs）的局限性而难以对齐，这些模型主要以视觉为中心，依赖昂贵的人类标注，且提供不透明的标量分数，无法捕捉细微推理，导致对齐脆弱。我们提出Omni-RRM，一种全模态、基于评分标准的奖励模型，可在文本、图像、视频和音频中生成多维奖励信号。为克服多维推理中以人类为中心评估的高成本和固有不一致性，我们引入Omni-Preference，一个通过自动评分标准引导的偏好合成构建的高质量数据集。在此流程中，教师模型将原始偏好转化为明确的解释，确保合成监督具有高保真度和可解释性。Omni-RRM采用渐进式SFT + GRPO训练方案，特别优化以在低边际、难偏好对上增强奖励判别能力。其在视频（ShareGPT-Video上80.2%）和音频基准（Audio-HH-RLHF上66.8%，TA2T上65.0%）上达到最先进准确率，整体五项基准准确率达70.4%，相较其基础模型提升17.0%。此外，Omni-RRM有效引导Best-of-N选择，并在纯文本对齐中表现出强迁移能力。所有资源（含数据集、训练与推理代码、模型检查点）见https://tmfk418.github.io/Omni-RRM。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitations of existing vision-centric reward models that rely on costly human labels and produce opaque scalar scores, the authors introduce Omni-RRM, a multimodal reward model grounded in rubrics to generate interpretable, multi-dimensional signals across text, image, video, and audio. To address the inconsistency and expense of human evaluation, they synthesize high-quality preference data via Omni-Preference, a pipeline where teacher models generate explicit justifications from raw preferences. Trained with a progressive SFT + GRPO regimen tuned for hard preference pairs, Omni-RRM achieves state-of-the-art results on video (80.2% on ShareGPT-Video) and audio benchmarks (66.8% and 65.0% on Audio-HH-RLHF and TA2T), with an overall accuracy of 70.4%—a +17.0% relative gain over its backbone—and demonstrates strong transfer to text-only alignment.
为了解决现有以视觉为中心的奖励模型缺乏可解释性、难以捕捉多模态中细微推理的问题，Omni-RRM提出了一种基于评分标准的多模态奖励建模框架，可生成文本、图像、视频和音频的多维奖励信号。该方法通过Omni-Preference数据集实现自动偏好合成，该数据集由教师模型对原始偏好进行解释性校准，从而生成高保真、可解释的监督信号。模型采用渐进式SFT + GRPO训练策略，针对低边际偏好对进行优化，最终在视频（ShareGPT-Video上80.2%）、音频（Audio-HH-RLHF上66.8%，TA2T上65.0%）等基准上达到最先进水平，整体准确率达70.4%，相较基线模型提升17.0%，并能有效引导Best-of-N选择及向纯文本对齐任务迁移。
FADRA: Frequency-Aware Diffusion with Residual Adaptation for Video Face Restoration
Authors: Jin Jiang, Jia Wang, Panwen Hu, Weiran Zhao, Shengcai Liao
First: 2026-07-07T15:24:42+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-07T15:24:42+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Video face restoration (VFR) aims to recover high-quality and temporally consistent facial details from severely degraded video sequences; however, existing methods still struggle to balance spatial fidelity and temporal coherence under complex degradations. To address this, we propose FADRA, a frequency-aware diffusion framework with iterative residual adaptation specifically tailored for robust VFR. We first leverage the strong temporal consistency of a pre-trained text-to-video diffusion model and introduce lightweight LoRA adapters together with a Low-Quality (LQ) Pixel-Alignment Feature Fusion module to efficiently adapt the frozen generative prior to the VFR task. To further adapt the frozen diffusion backbone to the downstream VFR task beyond LoRA-based adaptation, we introduce a Repeated Residual Adaptation Head (RRAH) for step-wise residual refinement after the diffusion backbone. To make this refinement explicitly guided by the degraded observation, RRAH further takes the LQ latent together with the current velocity prediction as input, allowing the model to repeatedly revisit LQ cues and predict residual updates at each flow-matching step. This LQ-guided repeated residual adaptation helps recover fine facial details while preserving the inherent temporal priors of the pre-trained model. Furthermore, to ensure the structural integrity of perceptually important details, we introduce a Frequency-Aware Loss that provides explicit supervision across multiple spectral bands, emphasizing visually sensitive frequency components that are crucial for perceptual quality and prone to temporal jittering. Extensive experiments demonstrate that FADRA recovers better facial structures and produces more temporally consistent videos than state-of-the-art methods, leading to clear gains in both quantitative metrics and visual perception.
中文标题/摘要
标题：FADRA：面向视频人脸修复的频率感知扩散与残差自适应方法
视频人脸修复（VFR）旨在从严重退化的视频序列中恢复高质量且时间一致的人脸细节；然而现有方法在复杂退化条件下仍难以平衡空间保真度与时间一致性。为此，我们提出FADRA，一种面向鲁棒VFR的频率感知扩散框架，结合迭代残差自适应机制。首先，我们利用预训练文本到视频扩散模型的强时间一致性，引入轻量LoRA适配器与低质量（LQ）像素对齐特征融合模块，高效地将冻结的生成先验适配至VFR任务。为进一步超越LoRA机制，我们引入重复残差自适应头（RRAH），在扩散主干后逐步进行残差精细化。为使该细化过程明确受退化观测引导，RRAH同时输入LQ潜在表示与当前速度预测，使模型在每一步流匹配中反复回顾LQ线索并预测残差更新。这种LQ引导的重复残差自适应有助于恢复精细人脸细节，同时保留预训练模型的固有时间先验。此外，为确保感知重要细节的结构完整性，我们引入频率感知损失，在多频段提供显式监督，强调对视觉感知质量关键且易受时间抖动影响的频段。大量实验表明，FADRA在恢复更优人脸结构与生成更时序一致的视频方面优于现有方法，显著提升定量指标与视觉感知效果。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of restoring high-quality, temporally consistent facial details from severely degraded videos while balancing spatial fidelity and temporal coherence, FADRA introduces a frequency-aware diffusion framework with iterative residual adaptation. It leverages a pre-trained text-to-video diffusion model’s temporal consistency, adapts it via lightweight LoRA adapters and a low-quality pixel-alignment fusion module, and further refines outputs using a Repeated Residual Adaptation Head that incorporates degraded latent cues at each diffusion step. A frequency-aware loss supervises across spectral bands to preserve perceptually critical details. Experiments show FADRA outperforms state-of-the-art methods in both quantitative metrics and visual quality, recovering finer facial structures with stronger temporal stability.
为解决视频面部修复中空间保真度与时间一致性难以兼顾的问题，FADRA提出一种频域感知的扩散框架，结合迭代残差自适应机制。该方法基于预训练文本到视频扩散模型，通过轻量LoRA适配器与低质量像素对齐特征融合模块进行高效适配，并引入重复残差自适应头，在扩散过程中逐步结合低质量输入进行残差修正。频域感知损失在多频段提供显式监督，强化视觉敏感频段的重建。实验表明，FADRA在定量指标与视觉感知上均优于现有方法，能更精准恢复面部结构并保持更强的时间一致性。
Knowing When to Quit: A Principled Framework for Dynamic Abstention in LLM Reasoning
Authors: Hen Davidov, Nachshon Cohen, Oren Kalinsky, Yaron Fairstein, Guy Kushilevitz, Ram Yazdi, Patrick Rebeschini
Venue: Proceedings of the 43rd International Conference on Machine Learning, Seoul, South Korea. PMLR 306, 2026. Copyright 2026 by the author(s)
First: 2026-04-20T15:38:45+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-07T15:24:26+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
LLMs utilizing chain-of-thought reasoning often waste substantial compute by producing long, incorrect responses. Abstention can mitigate this by withholding outputs unlikely to be correct. While most abstention methods decide to withhold outputs before or after generation, dynamic mid-generation abstention considers early termination of unpromising reasoning traces at each token position. Prior work has explored empirical variants of this idea, but principled guidance for the abstention rule remains lacking. We present a formal analysis of dynamic abstention for LLMs, modeling abstention as an explicit action within a regularized reinforcement learning framework. An abstention reward parameter controls the trade-off between compute and information. We show that abstaining when the value function falls below this reward strictly outperforms natural baselines under general conditions. We further derive a principled and efficient method to approximate the value function. Empirical results on mathematical reasoning and toxicity avoidance tasks support our theory and demonstrate improved selective accuracy over existing methods.
中文标题/摘要
标题：何时该停止：大语言模型推理中动态弃权的原理框架
使用思维链推理的大语言模型常因生成冗长且错误的响应而浪费大量计算资源。弃权机制可通过不输出不太可能正确的结果来缓解这一问题。尽管多数弃权方法在生成前或生成后决定是否弃权，动态生成中弃权则考虑在每个词位置提前终止无希望的推理路径。此前研究已探索过该思想的经验变体，但缺乏对弃权规则的原理性指导。我们对大语言模型的动态弃权进行了形式化分析，将其建模为正则化强化学习框架中的显式动作。一个弃权奖励参数控制计算与信息之间的权衡。我们证明，在一般条件下，当价值函数低于该奖励时弃权，其表现严格优于自然基线。我们进一步推导出一种原理性且高效的近似价值函数的方法。在数学推理和毒性规避任务上的实证结果支持我们的理论，并展示了相比现有方法更高的选择性准确率。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the inefficiency of LLMs generating long, incorrect reasoning traces, this work proposes a principled framework for dynamic mid-generation abstention, where outputs are withheld early if reasoning paths become unpromising. The method models abstention as an explicit action within a regularized reinforcement learning framework, using a reward parameter to balance compute cost against information gain. Experiments on mathematical reasoning and toxicity avoidance tasks show that abstaining when the value function falls below the reward threshold consistently outperforms existing baselines in selective accuracy.
为解决大语言模型在链式推理中生成冗长错误响应导致的计算浪费问题，本文提出一种动态中途终止的主动放弃框架，允许模型在生成过程中根据当前推理路径的潜力决定提前终止。该方法将放弃行为建模为强化学习中的显式动作，并引入放弃奖励参数以权衡计算成本与信息收益。通过推导价值函数的高效近似方法指导放弃决策，实验表明在数学推理和毒性规避任务中，当价值函数低于奖励阈值时放弃，能显著优于现有方法，提升选择性准确率。
Learning to Throw Objects Safely in Multi-Obstacle Environments
Authors: Mohammadreza Kasaei, Klemen Voncina, Hamidreza Kasaei
Venue: ICRA
First: 2026-07-07T15:24:12+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-07T15:24:12+00:00
Comments: This paper has been presented at the IEEE International Conference on Robotics & Automation (ICRA), 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Robotic throwing enables fast and efficient object placement beyond the robot's immediate workspace, but reliable throwing in cluttered environments remains underexplored. Existing approaches, such as TossingBot, learn throwing strategies from visual input but assume obstacle-free settings. In this paper, we address the problem of throwing objects into a target basket while avoiding obstacles placed randomly in the scene. We introduce a potential field state representation that compactly encodes both basket attraction and obstacle repulsion on a fixed-size grid, enabling reinforcement learning (RL) policies to generalize across arbitrary numbers and configurations of obstacles. The policy is initialized from kinesthetic demonstrations and optimized in simulation using three state-of-the-art RL algorithms (SAC, DDPG, TD3). Among these, SAC achieves the most consistent performance across scenarios. We compare the potential field representation against explicit state encodings and demonstrate that it achieves higher success rates and better scalability to unseen obstacle configurations. Real-robot experiments with unseen throwable objects confirm robust sim-to-real transfer, achieving up to $90\%$ success in cluttered scenes. These results demonstrate that PFR provides a practical and robust representation for safe and efficient robotic throwing in unstructured environments. A video showcasing our experiments is available at: https://youtu.be/ZZnJf8ua2dE
中文标题/摘要
标题：在多障碍环境中安全投掷物体的学习
机器人投掷技术可实现超出机器人工作空间的快速高效物体放置，但在杂乱环境中可靠投掷仍属探索不足。现有方法如TossingBot虽从视觉输入学习投掷策略，但假设无障碍环境。本文研究在随机布置障碍物的场景中将物体投掷入目标篮筐的问题。我们引入一种势场状态表示，在固定大小网格上紧凑编码篮筐吸引与障碍物排斥，使强化学习（RL）策略可泛化至任意数量和配置的障碍物。策略从运动示范初始化，并在仿真中使用三种前沿RL算法（SAC、DDPG、TD3）优化。其中SAC在不同场景中表现最稳定。我们对比势场表示与显式状态编码，证明其成功率更高且对未见障碍配置更具可扩展性。在真实机器人实验中，使用未见过的可投掷物体验证了从仿真到现实的鲁棒迁移，于杂乱场景中成功率高达90%。结果表明，PFR为无结构环境中安全高效机器人投掷提供了一种实用且稳健的表示方法。实验视频见：https://youtu.be/ZZnJf8ua2dE
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for robots to throw objects safely in cluttered environments, this paper proposes a potential field state representation that encodes basket attraction and obstacle repulsion on a fixed grid, enabling reinforcement learning policies to generalize across varying obstacle configurations. Initialized from kinesthetic demonstrations and trained in simulation using SAC, DDPG, and TD3, the SAC policy showed the most consistent performance. Experimental results demonstrate that the potential field representation outperforms explicit encodings in success rate and scalability, achieving up to 90% success in real-world cluttered scenes with unseen objects, validating its robustness for safe robotic throwing.
为实现机器人在障碍物环境中安全投掷物体，本文提出一种势场状态表示方法，通过固定网格编码目标篮筐的吸引与障碍物的排斥，使强化学习策略能泛化至任意障碍配置。策略以运动示范初始化，并在仿真中用SAC、DDPG和TD3训练，其中SAC表现最稳定。实验表明该势场表示在成功率与对未知障碍的扩展性上优于显式状态编码，真实机器人测试中对新物体的投掷成功率高达90%，验证了其强仿真到现实迁移能力。
HERB: Human-augmented Efficient Reinforcement learning for Bin-packing
Authors: Gojko Perovic, Nuno Ferreira Duarte, Atabak Dehban, Gonçalo Teixeira, Egidio Falotico, José Santos-Victor
First: 2025-04-23T10:24:36+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-07T15:21:58+00:00
Comments: 8 pages, 8 Figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Packing objects efficiently is a fundamental problem in logistics, warehouse automation, and robotics. When dealing with highly diverse 3D objects (household or grocery items), closed-form solutions are infeasible, and heuristic or model-free Reinforcement Learning~(RL) methods tend to focus solely on geometric optimization, relying on exhaustive searches of the discretized solution space. This leads to long training times (for pure RL) and high latency (heuristics), limited transferability to robotic scenarios, and ultimately ignores object characteristics (fragility, deformability) and human preferences. We propose HERB, a human-augmented RL framework for packing irregular objects, the first to explore the potential of learning from human demonstrations to solve this complex task. It leverages human demonstrations of packing strategies, which inherently exhibit latent factors such as space optimization, stability, and object properties that are difficult to model explicitly. The human-expert data is combined with RL exploration to provide the placement of each object inside the container. Experimental results show that our method outperforms heuristic, purely RL-based, and imitation learning approaches in packing efficiency and latency. Qualitative results highlight that our packing strategy produces more stable, human-like arrangements, which we expect to be more appropriate and widely accepted. Finally, we demonstrate the real-world feasibility of our method on a robotic system.
中文标题/摘要
标题：HERB：用于装箱的人类增强高效强化学习
高效地打包物体是物流、仓库自动化和机器人领域的基础问题。当处理高度多样化的3D物体（如家居或食品商品）时，闭式解不可行，启发式或无模型强化学习（RL）方法往往仅关注几何优化，依赖于离散解空间的穷举搜索。这导致纯RL训练时间长，启发式方法延迟高，难以迁移到机器人场景，且最终忽略了物体特性（易碎性、可变形性）和人类偏好。我们提出HERB，一种用于打包不规则物体的人类增强RL框架，首次探索了从人类演示中学习解决此复杂任务的潜力。该框架利用人类打包策略演示，其内在蕴含空间优化、稳定性及物体特性等难以显式建模的潜在因素。人类专家数据与RL探索相结合，为每个物体在容器内的放置提供指导。实验结果表明，我们的方法在打包效率和延迟方面优于启发式、纯RL及模仿学习方法。定性结果表明，我们的打包策略生成更稳定、更符合人类习惯的布局，我们预期其更合适且更易被接受。最后，我们在机器人系统上验证了该方法的实际可行性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the inefficiency of traditional heuristics and pure reinforcement learning in packing diverse 3D objects while ignoring object properties and human preferences, HERB introduces a human-augmented RL framework that integrates human demonstration data with RL exploration to guide object placement. The method leverages latent human strategies—such as stability and space optimization—that are hard to encode explicitly, enabling faster convergence and more realistic packing. Experiments show HERB achieves superior packing efficiency and lower latency compared to heuristics, pure RL, and imitation learning, while producing more stable, human-like arrangements that are more suitable for real-world robotic deployment.
为解决传统启发式方法和纯强化学习在处理多样3D物体时效率低、忽略物体特性与人类偏好等问题，HERB提出一种人类辅助强化学习框架。该方法结合人类演示数据（蕴含空间优化、稳定性等隐性因素）与强化学习探索，指导物体在容器内的放置。实验表明，HERB在装箱效率和延迟上优于启发式、纯强化学习及模仿学习方法，且生成更稳定、更贴近人类习惯的排列，更适合实际机器人部署。
